import { Link } from '@tanstack/react-router'
import type { ReactNode } from 'react'

interface BackendUnavailablePageProps {
  title: string
  feature: string
  backTo?: '/events' | '/organizer/dashboard'
  children?: ReactNode
}

export function BackendUnavailablePage({
  title,
  feature,
  backTo = '/events',
  children,
}: BackendUnavailablePageProps) {
  return (
    <main className="mx-auto min-h-[60vh] max-w-3xl px-4 py-20 sm:px-6">
      <Link to={backTo} className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600 hover:text-navy">
        ← {backTo === '/events' ? 'Events' : 'Organizer dashboard'}
      </Link>
      <h1 className="mt-4 text-3xl font-extrabold text-navy-dark">{title}</h1>
      <p role="status" className="mt-4 rounded-xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 p-4 text-sm text-amber-900">
        {feature} is unavailable because the connected backend does not currently provide the required API.
        No local or demo data is being shown or saved.
      </p>
      {children && <div className="mt-4">{children}</div>}
    </main>
  )
}

FILE: src/components/ConfirmDialog.tsx
========================================================================================
import { Modal } from './Modal'

type ConfirmDialogProps = {
  open: boolean
  onClose: () => void
  onConfirm: () => void
  title: string
  description: string
  confirmLabel?: string
  cancelLabel?: string
  variant?: 'default' | 'danger'
}

export function ConfirmDialog({
  open,
  onClose,
  onConfirm,
  title,
  description,
  confirmLabel = 'Confirm',
  cancelLabel = 'Cancel',
  variant = 'default',
}: ConfirmDialogProps) {
  function handleConfirm() {
    onConfirm()
    onClose()
  }

  const confirmClasses =
    variant === 'danger'
      ? 'rounded-xl bg-red-600 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-red-700 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-500'
      : 'rounded-xl bg-navy px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-navy-dark focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-navy'

  const cancelClasses =
    'rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-slate-400'

  const footer = (
    <>
      <button type="button" onClick={onClose} className={cancelClasses}>
        {cancelLabel}
      </button>
      <button type="button" onClick={handleConfirm} className={confirmClasses}>
        {confirmLabel}
      </button>
    </>
  )

  return (
    <Modal open={open} onClose={onClose} title={title} description={description} footer={footer} />
  )
}

FILE: src/components/Header.tsx
========================================================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { Link, useNavigate, useLocation } from '@tanstack/react-router'
import { Menu, X } from 'lucide-react'
import { headerLinks } from '../routes/links'
import SiteLink from './SiteLink'
import { useAuth } from '../features/authentication/useAuth'

export default function Header() {
  const [menuOpen, setMenuOpen] = useState(false)
  const [scrolled, setScrolled] = useState(false)
  const location = useLocation()

  // Close menu on route change
  useEffect(() => {
    setMenuOpen(false)
  }, [location.pathname])

  useEffect(() => {
    const onScroll = () => setScrolled(window.scrollY > 10)
    window.addEventListener('scroll', onScroll)
    return () => window.removeEventListener('scroll', onScroll)
  }, [])

  // Close menu on Escape
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') setMenuOpen(false)
    }
    if (menuOpen) document.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown)
    return () => document.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown)
  }, [menuOpen])

  const { user, logout } = useAuth()
  const isAdmin = user?.role === 'admin'
  // Admins don't see the Events or Bookings links; everyone else sees the full menu.
  const visibleLinks = isAdmin ? headerLinks.filter((item) => !/event|booking/i.test(item.label)) : headerLinks
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const closeMenu = () => setMenuOpen(false)
  const handleLogout = () => {
    logout()
    setMenuOpen(false)
    navigate({ to: '/' })
  }

  return (
    <header className={`fixed inset-x-0 top-0 z-50 bg-white ${scrolled ? 'shadow-md' : 'border-b border-slate-100'}`}>
      <div className="mx-auto flex h-16 max-w-7xl items-center justify-between px-4 sm:px-6">
        <Link to="/" className="flex items-center gap-2 text-lg font-extrabold text-amber-300">
          Tic-EM
        </Link>

        {/* Desktop Nav */}
        <nav aria-label="Main navigation" className="hidden items-center gap-6 lg:flex xl:gap-8">
          {visibleLinks.map((item) => (
            <SiteLink key={item.label} item={item} className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-600 hover:text-navy" />
          ))}
          {user ? (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-4 border-l border-slate-200 pl-5">
              {user.role === 'customer' && <Link to="/tickets" className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-600 hover:text-navy">My Tickets</Link>}
              {user.role === 'organizer' && <Link to="/organizer/dashboard" className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-600 hover:text-navy">Dashboard</Link>}
              {user.role === 'staff' && <Link to="/scan" className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-600 hover:text-navy">Scan</Link>}
              {user.role === 'admin' && <Link to="/admin" className="text-sm font-medium text-red-600 hover:text-red-800 font-bold">Admin Panel</Link>}
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                <span aria-hidden="true" className="grid size-8 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full bg-navy-50 text-xs font-bold uppercase text-navy">
                  {user.name.trim().charAt(0)}
                </span>
                <span className="flex flex-col leading-tight">
                  <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">{user.name}</span>
                  <span className="text-[11px] font-medium capitalize text-slate-500">{user.role}</span>
                </span>
              </div>
              <button onClick={handleLogout} className="rounded-full border px-4 py-1.5 text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-slate-50">Logout</button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <Link to="/login" className="rounded-full bg-navy px-5 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-navy-dark">Login</Link>
          )}
        </nav>

        {/* Mobile Toggle */}
        <button 
          aria-label={menuOpen ? 'Close menu' : 'Open menu'}
          aria-expanded={menuOpen}
          aria-controls="mobile-menu"
          onClick={() => setMenuOpen(!menuOpen)} 
          className="text-navy lg:hidden"
        >
          {menuOpen ? <X /> : <Menu />}
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Mobile Nav */}
      {menuOpen && (
        <nav id="mobile-menu" aria-label="Mobile navigation" className="flex flex-col border-t bg-white px-4 pb-4 lg:hidden">
          {visibleLinks.map((item) => (
            <SiteLink key={item.label} item={item} onClick={closeMenu} className="rounded-lg px-3 py-3 font-medium hover:bg-navy-50" />
          ))}
          {user ? (
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 pt-2">
              {user.role === 'customer' && <Link to="/tickets" onClick={closeMenu} className="rounded-lg px-3 py-2 font-medium hover:bg-navy-50">My Tickets</Link>}
              {user.role === 'organizer' && <Link to="/organizer/dashboard" onClick={closeMenu} className="rounded-lg px-3 py-2 font-medium hover:bg-navy-50">Dashboard</Link>}
              {user.role === 'staff' && <Link to="/scan" onClick={closeMenu} className="rounded-lg px-3 py-2 font-medium hover:bg-navy-50">Scan</Link>}
              {user.role === 'admin' && <Link to="/admin" onClick={closeMenu} className="rounded-lg px-3 py-2 font-medium text-red-600 hover:bg-red-50">Admin Panel</Link>}
              <div className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-2.5 border-t border-slate-100 px-3 pt-3">
                <span aria-hidden="true" className="grid size-8 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full bg-navy-50 text-xs font-bold uppercase text-navy">
                  {user.name.trim().charAt(0)}
                </span>
                <span className="flex flex-col leading-tight">
                  <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">{user.name}</span>
                  <span className="text-[11px] font-medium capitalize text-slate-500">{user.role}</span>
                </span>
              </div>
              <button onClick={handleLogout} className="mt-2 rounded-full border py-2 text-center font-semibold hover:bg-slate-50">Logout</button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <Link to="/login" onClick={closeMenu} className="mt-2 rounded-full bg-navy py-3 text-center font-semibold text-white">Login</Link>
          )}
        </nav>
      )}
    </header>
  )
}

FILE: src/components/Modal.tsx
========================================================================================
import { useEffect, useRef, type ReactNode } from 'react'

type ModalProps = {
  open: boolean
  onClose: () => void
  title: string
  description?: string
  children?: ReactNode
  footer?: ReactNode
}

export function Modal({ open, onClose, title, description, children, footer }: ModalProps) {
  const dialogRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
  const closeButtonRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null)
  const lastFocusedRef = useRef<HTMLElement | null>(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return

    lastFocusedRef.current = document.activeElement as HTMLElement
    document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden'

    // Focus first focusable element or close button
    setTimeout(() => {
      if (closeButtonRef.current) {
        closeButtonRef.current.focus()
      } else if (dialogRef.current) {
        const focusable = dialogRef.current.querySelector<HTMLElement>(
          'button, [href], input, select, textarea, [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])',
        )
        focusable?.focus()
      }
    }, 0)

    function handleKeyDown(e: KeyboardEvent) {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        e.preventDefault()
        onClose()
        return
      }
      if (e.key === 'Tab' && dialogRef.current) {
        const focusableElements = Array.from(
          dialogRef.current.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(
            'button, [href], input, select, textarea, [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])',
          ),
        ).filter((el) => !el.hasAttribute('disabled') && el.getAttribute('aria-hidden') !== 'true')

        if (focusableElements.length === 0) return

        const first = focusableElements[0]
        const last = focusableElements[focusableElements.length - 1]
        const active = document.activeElement as HTMLElement

        if (e.shiftKey) {
          if (active === first || !dialogRef.current.contains(active)) {
            e.preventDefault()
            last.focus()
          }
        } else {
          if (active === last || !dialogRef.current.contains(active)) {
            e.preventDefault()
            first.focus()
          }
        }
      }
    }

    document.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown)
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown)
      document.body.style.overflow = ''
      lastFocusedRef.current?.focus()
    }
  }, [open, onClose])

  if (!open) return null

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4" role="presentation">
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/50" onClick={onClose} aria-hidden="true" />
      <div
        ref={dialogRef}
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-labelledby="modal-title"
        aria-describedby={description ? 'modal-description' : undefined}
        className="relative w-full max-w-md rounded-2xl bg-white p-6 shadow-xl"
      >
        <div className="mb-4">
          <h2 id="modal-title" className="text-xl font-bold text-navy-dark">
            {title}
          </h2>
          {description && (
            <p id="modal-description" className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-600">
              {description}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>
        {children}
        {footer && <div className="mt-6 flex flex-col-reverse gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:justify-end">{footer}</div>}
        <button
          ref={closeButtonRef}
          type="button"
          onClick={onClose}
          aria-label="Close dialog"
          className="absolute right-4 top-4 rounded-full p-1 text-slate-400 hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-600"
        >
          ×
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

FILE: src/components/QrCode.tsx
========================================================================================
import { QRCodeCanvas } from 'qrcode.react'
import { useMemo, type Ref } from 'react'
import { ticketQrValue } from '../lib/tickets'

interface QrCodeProps {
  value: string
  size?: number
  canvasRef?: Ref<HTMLCanvasElement>
  dataUrl?: string
}

export function QrCode({ value, size = 170, canvasRef, dataUrl }: QrCodeProps) {
  // the code holds the ticket id plus its security tag
  const qrValue = useMemo(() => ticketQrValue(value), [value])

  return (
    <div
      className="qr"
      role="img"
      aria-label={`QR code for ${value}`}
      style={{ width: size + 18 }}
    >
      {dataUrl ? (
        <img
          src={dataUrl}
          width={size}
          height={size}
          alt=""
          style={{ width: size, height: size, objectFit: 'contain' }}
        />
      ) : (
        <QRCodeCanvas
          ref={canvasRef}
          value={qrValue}
          size={size}
          level="M"
        />
      )}
    </div>
  )
}


========================================================================================
FILE: src/components/Reveal.tsx
========================================================================================
import { useEffect, useRef, type ReactNode, type CSSProperties } from 'react'

interface RevealProps {
  children: ReactNode
  delay?: number
  className?: string
}

export default function Reveal({ children, delay = 0, className = '' }: RevealProps) {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    const el = ref.current
    if (!el) return
    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
      ([entry]) => {
        if (entry.isIntersecting) {
          el.classList.add('is-visible')
          observer.disconnect()
        }
      },
      { threshold: 0.12 },
    )
    observer.observe(el)
    return () => observer.disconnect()
  }, [])

  const style = { '--d': `${delay}ms` } as CSSProperties

  return (
    <div ref={ref} style={style} className={`reveal ${className}`}>
      {children}
    </div>
  )
}


========================================================================================
FILE: src/components/SiteLink.tsx
========================================================================================
import { Link } from '@tanstack/react-router'
import type { NavLink } from '../routes/links'

interface SiteLinkProps {
  item: NavLink
  className?: string
  onClick?: () => void
}

export default function SiteLink({ item, className, onClick }: SiteLinkProps) {
  if (item.slug) {
    return (
      <a href={`#${item.slug}`} className={className} onClick={onClick}>
        {item.label}
      </a>
    )
  }
  if (item.to) {
    return (
      <Link to={item.to} className={className} onClick={onClick}>
        {item.label}
      </Link>
    )
  }
  return (
    <a href={item.href} className={className} onClick={onClick}>
      {item.label}
    </a>
  )
}

FILE: src/components/navAccess.test.ts
========================================================================================
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import type { Role } from '../features/authentication/authStore'
import { navAccess } from './navAccess'

// the names of the links this role is allowed to see
function allowed(role: Role | undefined): string[] {
  return Object.entries(navAccess(role))
    .filter(([, visible]) => visible)
    .map(([name]) => name)
}

describe('navAccess', () => {
  it('shows signed-out visitors only the public links', () => {
    expect(allowed(undefined)).toEqual(['publicLinks'])
  })

  it('shows customers only customer links', () => {
    expect(allowed('customer')).toEqual(['publicLinks', 'bookings', 'tickets'])
  })

  it('shows organizers only organizer links', () => {
    expect(allowed('organizer')).toEqual(['dashboard', 'staffManagement'])
  })

  it('shows staff only the scanner', () => {
    expect(allowed('staff')).toEqual(['scan'])
  })

  it('shows admins only the admin panel', () => {
    expect(allowed('admin')).toEqual(['admin'])
  })
})


========================================================================================
FILE: src/components/navAccess.ts
========================================================================================
import type { Role } from '../features/authentication/authStore'

// Which navigation links each kind of visitor may see (issue #89).
// The header asks this once, so desktop and mobile menus always agree.
export interface NavAccess {
  publicLinks: boolean // Home / Events / site links
  bookings: boolean
  tickets: boolean
  dashboard: boolean
  staffManagement: boolean
  scan: boolean
  admin: boolean
}

export function navAccess(role: Role | undefined): NavAccess {
  return {
    publicLinks: role === undefined || role === 'customer',
    bookings: role === 'customer',
    tickets: role === 'customer',
    dashboard: role === 'organizer',
    staffManagement: role === 'organizer',
    scan: role === 'staff',
    admin: role === 'admin',
  }
}


========================================================================================
FILE: src/components/ui/Modal.tsx
========================================================================================
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react'
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom'
import { X } from 'lucide-react'

interface ModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean
  onClose: () => void
  title: string
  children: React.ReactNode
}

export function Modal({ isOpen, onClose, title, children }: ModalProps) {
  const modalRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
  const previousFocus = useRef<HTMLElement | null>(null)
  // keep the latest onClose without re-running the focus effect on every render
  const onCloseRef = useRef(onClose)
  useEffect(() => {
    onCloseRef.current = onClose
  })

  // Handle Escape key & Body Scroll Lock
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return

    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') onCloseRef.current()
    }
    
    // Save current focus and lock scroll
    previousFocus.current = document.activeElement as HTMLElement
    document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden'
    document.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown)
    
    // Move focus into the modal
    setTimeout(() => {
      const focusable = modalRef.current?.querySelectorAll('button, [href], input, select, textarea, [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])')
      if (focusable && focusable.length > 0) {
        (focusable[0] as HTMLElement).focus()
      }
    }, 10)

    return () => {
      document.body.style.overflow = ''
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown)
      // Restore focus to where it was
      if (previousFocus.current) previousFocus.current.focus()
    }
  }, [isOpen])

  // Trap focus inside the modal
  const handleTabKey = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
    if (e.key !== 'Tab' || !modalRef.current) return
    
    const focusableElements = modalRef.current.querySelectorAll('button, [href], input, select, textarea, [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])')
    if (focusableElements.length === 0) return
    
    const first = focusableElements[0] as HTMLElement
    const last = focusableElements[focusableElements.length - 1] as HTMLElement

    if (e.shiftKey && document.activeElement === first) {
      e.preventDefault()
      last.focus()
    } else if (!e.shiftKey && document.activeElement === last) {
      e.preventDefault()
      first.focus()
    }
  }

  if (!isOpen) return null

  return createPortal(
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50 p-4">
      <div 
        ref={modalRef}
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-labelledby="modal-title"
        onKeyDown={handleTabKey}
        className="w-full max-w-md rounded-2xl bg-white shadow-xl flex flex-col max-h-[90vh]"
      >
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b px-6 py-4">
          <h2 id="modal-title" className="text-lg font-bold text-navy-dark">{title}</h2>
          <button onClick={onClose} aria-label="Close modal" className="rounded-full p-2 hover:bg-slate-100">
            <X size={20} />
          </button>
        </div>
        <div className="overflow-y-auto p-6">
          {children}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>,
    document.body
  )
}

import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import { Search, UserPlus, Power, Trash2, Eye } from 'lucide-react'
import { useAuth } from '../../authentication'
import {
  createAccount, deleteAccount, listManagedUsers, setAccountStatus,
  type AccountStatus, type ManagedUser, type Role,
} from '../../authentication/authStore'
import { listAllEvents, isEventOwnedBy } from '../../../lib/events'
import { listBookingsForUser, listTicketsForUser } from '../../../lib/tickets'
import { getKYC } from '../../../lib/verification'
import { Modal } from '../../../components/ui/Modal'
import { ConfirmDialog } from '../../../components/ConfirmDialog'
import { useToast } from '../../toast'

const PAGE_SIZE = 8
const ROLES: Role[] = ['customer', 'organizer', 'staff', 'admin']
const field = 'mt-1 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-navy'
const emptyForm = { name: '', email: '', password: '', role: 'customer' as Role, organizerId: '', actorPassword: '' }

const date = (iso: string) => new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString('en-GB', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' })

function Activity({ target, everyone }: { target: ManagedUser; everyone: ManagedUser[] }) {
  const rows: [string, string][] = []
  if (target.role === 'customer') {
    rows.push(['Tickets', String(listTicketsForUser(target.id).length)])
    rows.push(['Bookings', String(listBookingsForUser(target.id).length)])
  }
  if (target.role === 'organizer') {
    rows.push(['Events', String(listAllEvents().filter((e) => isEventOwnedBy(e, target)).length)])
    rows.push(['Staff', String(everyone.filter((u) => u.organizerId === target.id).length)])
    rows.push(['Verification', getKYC(target.id)?.status ?? 'Unverified'])
  }
  if (target.role === 'staff') {
    rows.push(['Organizer', everyone.find((u) => u.id === target.organizerId)?.name ?? 'None'])
    rows.push(['Assigned events', String(target.assignedEventIds.length)])
  }
  return (
    <dl className="mt-4 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-sm">
      {rows.map(([label, value]) => (
        <div key={label} className="rounded-lg bg-slate-50 px-3 py-2">
          <dt className="text-xs text-slate-500">{label}</dt>
          <dd className="font-semibold text-navy-dark">{value}</dd>
        </div>
      ))}
    </dl>
  )
}

export function UserManagement() {
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const { showToast } = useToast()
  const [, setVersion] = useState(0)
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('')
  const [role, setRole] = useState<Role | 'all'>('all')
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<AccountStatus | 'all'>('all')
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1)
  const [viewing, setViewing] = useState<ManagedUser | null>(null)
  const [deleting, setDeleting] = useState<ManagedUser | null>(null)
  const [creating, setCreating] = useState(false)
  const [form, setForm] = useState(emptyForm)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)

  const everyone = listManagedUsers() // read fresh each render; setVersion forces one after a change
  const organizers = everyone.filter((u) => u.role === 'organizer')

  const q = search.trim().toLowerCase()
  const filtered = everyone
    .filter((u) => (role === 'all' || u.role === role) && (status === 'all' || u.status === status))
    .filter((u) => !q || u.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || u.email.toLowerCase().includes(q))
    .sort((a, b) => b.createdAt.localeCompare(a.createdAt))

  const pages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(filtered.length / PAGE_SIZE))
  const current = Math.min(page, pages)
  const visible = filtered.slice((current - 1) * PAGE_SIZE, current * PAGE_SIZE)

  if (!user) return null

  function run(action: () => void, success: string) {
    try {
      action()
      setVersion((v) => v + 1)
      showToast(success, 'success')
    } catch (err) {
      showToast(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Something went wrong', 'error')
    }
  }

  function create(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault()
    setError(null)
    try {
      createAccount(user!, { ...form, organizerId: form.organizerId || undefined })
      setVersion((v) => v + 1)
      setCreating(false)
      setForm(emptyForm)
      showToast('Account created', 'success')
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Could not create the account')
    }
  }

  const filterClass = 'rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-navy'

  return (
    <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm sm:p-8">
      <div className="mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-navy-dark">Users ({filtered.length})</h2>
        <button onClick={() => { setError(null); setCreating(true) }} className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-navy px-5 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-navy-dark">
          <UserPlus size={16} /> Create user
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="mb-4 flex flex-wrap gap-3">
        <label className="relative min-w-56 flex-1">
          <Search size={16} className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
          <input aria-label="Search users" value={search} onChange={(e) => { setSearch(e.target.value); setPage(1) }} placeholder="Search name or email" className={`${filterClass} w-full pl-9`} />
        </label>
        <select aria-label="Filter by role" value={role} onChange={(e) => { setRole(e.target.value as Role | 'all'); setPage(1) }} className={filterClass}>
          <option value="all">All roles</option>
          {ROLES.map((r) => <option key={r} value={r} className="capitalize">{r}</option>)}
        </select>
        <select aria-label="Filter by status" value={status} onChange={(e) => { setStatus(e.target.value as AccountStatus | 'all'); setPage(1) }} className={filterClass}>
          <option value="all">All statuses</option>
          <option value="active">Active</option>
          <option value="disabled">Disabled</option>
        </select>
      </div>

      <div className="overflow-x-auto">
        <table className="w-full text-left text-sm text-slate-600">
          <thead className="bg-slate-50 text-xs uppercase text-slate-500">
            <tr>
              <th className="px-4 py-3">Name</th>
              <th className="px-4 py-3">Role</th>
              <th className="px-4 py-3">Status</th>
              <th className="px-4 py-3">Registered</th>
              <th className="px-4 py-3 text-right">Actions</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {visible.length === 0 && (
              <tr><td colSpan={5} className="px-4 py-8 text-center text-slate-500">No users match your search.</td></tr>
            )}
            {visible.map((u) => (
              <tr key={u.id} className="border-b border-slate-100 last:border-0 hover:bg-slate-50">
                <td className="px-4 py-3">
                  <div className="font-semibold text-navy-dark">{u.name}</div>
                  <div className="text-xs">{u.email}</div>
                </td>
                <td className="px-4 py-3 capitalize">{u.role}</td>
                <td className="px-4 py-3">
                  <span className={`rounded-full px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold ${u.status === 'active' ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700' : 'bg-slate-200 text-slate-700'}`}>
                    {u.status === 'active' ? 'Active' : 'Disabled'}
                  </span>
                </td>
                <td className="px-4 py-3 text-xs">{date(u.createdAt)}</td>
                <td className="px-4 py-3">
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-end gap-2">
                    <button onClick={() => setViewing(u)} className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-slate-50"><Eye size={14} /> View</button>
                    <button
                      onClick={() => run(() => setAccountStatus(user, u.id, u.status === 'active' ? 'disabled' : 'active'), u.status === 'active' ? 'Account disabled' : 'Account enabled')}
                      className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-slate-50"
                    ><Power size={14} /> {u.status === 'active' ? 'Disable' : 'Enable'}</button>
                    <button onClick={() => setDeleting(u)} className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg bg-red-600 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-white hover:bg-red-700"><Trash2 size={14} /> Delete</button>
                  </div>
                </td>
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-4 flex items-center justify-between text-sm text-slate-600">
        <span>Page {current} of {pages}</span>
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <button disabled={current <= 1} onClick={() => setPage(current - 1)} className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 px-3 py-1.5 font-semibold disabled:opacity-40">Previous</button>
          <button disabled={current >= pages} onClick={() => setPage(current + 1)} className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 px-3 py-1.5 font-semibold disabled:opacity-40">Next</button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <Modal isOpen={!!viewing} onClose={() => setViewing(null)} title={viewing?.name ?? ''}>
        {viewing && (
          <div className="text-sm text-slate-600">
            <p>{viewing.email}</p>
            <p className="mt-1 capitalize">{viewing.role} · {viewing.status} · registered {date(viewing.createdAt)}</p>
            <Activity target={viewing} everyone={everyone} />
          </div>
        )}
      </Modal>

      <Modal isOpen={creating} onClose={() => setCreating(false)} title="Create user">
        <form onSubmit={create} className="space-y-3">
          <label className="block text-sm font-medium">Full name
            <input className={field} value={form.name} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, name: e.target.value })} />
          </label>
          <label className="block text-sm font-medium">Email
            <input type="email" className={field} value={form.email} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, email: e.target.value })} />
          </label>
          <label className="block text-sm font-medium">Temporary password
            <input type="password" className={field} value={form.password} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, password: e.target.value })} placeholder="At least 8 characters" />
          </label>
          <label className="block text-sm font-medium">Role
            <select className={field} value={form.role} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, role: e.target.value as Role })}>
              {ROLES.map((r) => <option key={r} value={r} className="capitalize">{r}</option>)}
            </select>
          </label>
          {form.role === 'staff' && (
            <label className="block text-sm font-medium">Organizer
              <select className={field} value={form.organizerId} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, organizerId: e.target.value })}>
                <option value="">Choose an organizer</option>
                {organizers.map((o) => <option key={o.id} value={o.id}>{o.name}</option>)}
              </select>
            </label>
          )}
          {form.role === 'admin' && (
            <label className="block text-sm font-medium">Your password (to confirm)
              <input type="password" className={field} value={form.actorPassword} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, actorPassword: e.target.value })} />
              <span className="mt-1 block text-xs font-normal text-slate-500">Administrators can manage every account, so we ask for your password again.</span>
            </label>
          )}
          {error && <p role="alert" className="text-sm font-medium text-red-600">{error}</p>}
          <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 pt-2">
            <button type="button" onClick={() => setCreating(false)} className="rounded-full border px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-slate-50">Cancel</button>
            <button type="submit" className="rounded-full bg-navy px-5 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-navy-dark">Create</button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </Modal>

      <ConfirmDialog
        open={!!deleting}
        onClose={() => setDeleting(null)}
        onConfirm={() => { if (deleting) run(() => deleteAccount(user, deleting.id), 'Account deleted') }}
        title={deleting ? `Delete ${deleting.name}?` : 'Delete account?'}
        description={deleting?.role === 'organizer'
          ? 'Their login and all their staff logins will be removed. Their events, tickets and bookings stay on record. This cannot be undone.'
          : 'Their login will be removed. Any tickets and bookings stay on record. This cannot be undone.'}
        confirmLabel="Delete"
        variant="danger"
      />
    </div>
  )
}

FILE: src/features/admin/pages/AdminDashboardPage.tsx

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import { ShieldAlert, DollarSign, Ban, Trash2, ShieldCheck, ChevronDown, ChevronRight, AlertTriangle, RotateCcw } from 'lucide-react'
import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { listAllEvents, saveEvent, type StoredEvent } from '../../../lib/events'
import {
  AUDIT_ACTION_LABELS,
  deleteAuditEntries,
  getCurrentActor,
  isAdmin,
  listAuditLogs,
  recordAudit,
  recordDeniedAccess,
  verifyAuditLog,
  type AuditAction,
  type AuditActor,
  type AuditEntry,
} from '../../../lib/audit'
import { useToast } from '../../toast'
import {StatCard} from '../../organizer/components/StatCard'
import { UserManagement } from '../components/UserManagement'

type Tab = 'events' | 'users' | 'audit'

interface WeekGroup { key: string; label: string; entries: AuditEntry[] }
interface MonthGroup { key: string; label: string; count: number; weeks: WeekGroup[] }
interface PendingDelete { ids: string[]; title: string }

const pad = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, '0')

function weekStartOf(date: Date): Date {
  const d = new Date(date)
  d.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0)
  d.setDate(d.getDate() - ((d.getDay() + 6) % 7)) // weeks start on Monday
  return d
}

/** Groups newest-first logs into months, and weeks inside each month. */
function groupLogs(logs: AuditEntry[]): MonthGroup[] {
  const months = new Map<string, MonthGroup>()
  for (const log of logs) {
    const date = new Date(log.timestamp)
    const monthKey = `${date.getFullYear()}-${pad(date.getMonth() + 1)}`
    let month = months.get(monthKey)
    if (!month) {
      month = {
        key: monthKey,
        label: date.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { month: 'long', year: 'numeric' }),
        count: 0,
        weeks: [],
      }
      months.set(monthKey, month)
    }
    const start = weekStartOf(date)
    const end = new Date(start)
    end.setDate(end.getDate() + 6)
    const weekKey = `${monthKey}|${start.getFullYear()}-${pad(start.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(start.getDate())}`
    let week = month.weeks.find((w) => w.key === weekKey)
    if (!week) {
      const fmt = (d: Date) => d.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { day: 'numeric', month: 'short' })
      week = { key: weekKey, label: `Week of ${fmt(start)} – ${fmt(end)} ${end.getFullYear()}`, entries: [] }
      month.weeks.push(week)
    }
    week.entries.push(log)
    month.count += 1
  }
  return Array.from(months.values())
}

const tabClass = (active: boolean) =>
  `pb-3 font-semibold text-sm ${active ? 'border-b-2 border-navy text-navy' : 'text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-700'}`

export function AdminDashboardPage() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient()
  const { showToast } = useToast()

  // The signed-in user (read from the 'tm_user' session). Only administrators may use this page.
  const [actor] = useState<AuditActor | null>(() => getCurrentActor())
  const authorized = isAdmin(actor)

  const [events, setEvents] = useState<StoredEvent[]>(() => listAllEvents())
  const [logs, setLogs] = useState<AuditEntry[]>(() => (isAdmin(actor) ? listAuditLogs(actor) : []))
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<Tab>('events')
  const [auditFilter, setAuditFilter] = useState<'all' | AuditAction>('all')
  const [auditSearch, setAuditSearch] = useState('')
  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState<Set<string> | null>(null)
  const [pendingDelete, setPendingDelete] = useState<PendingDelete | null>(null)

  // Log attempts by non-admins to open this page.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (actor && !authorized) recordDeniedAccess(actor, 'Admin Dashboard')
  }, [actor, authorized])

  const filteredLogs = useMemo(() => {
    const term = auditSearch.trim().toLowerCase()
    return logs.filter((log) => {
      if (auditFilter !== 'all' && log.action !== auditFilter) return false
      if (!term) return true
      return [log.actorName, log.targetLabel, log.details, AUDIT_ACTION_LABELS[log.action]]
        .join(' ')
        .toLowerCase()
        .includes(term)
    })
  }, [logs, auditFilter, auditSearch])

  const groups = useMemo(() => groupLogs(filteredLogs), [filteredLogs])

  if (!actor || !authorized) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 pb-16 pt-20">
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-xl px-4 text-center">
          <ShieldAlert className="mx-auto mb-4 text-red-600" size={40} />
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-navy-dark">Access denied</h1>
          <p className="mt-2 text-slate-600">
            This area is for administrators only. Sign in with an administrator account to continue.
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  // `admin` is guaranteed to be an administrator from here on.
  const admin: AuditActor = actor

  const totalRevenue = events.reduce(
    (sum, event) => sum + event.ticketTypes.reduce((tSum, type) => tSum + type.sold * type.price, 0),
    0,
  )
  const integrity = verifyAuditLog(admin)

  function refreshAll() {
    setEvents(listAllEvents())
    setLogs(listAuditLogs(admin))
  }

  /** Runs an admin action, shows its result, and refreshes every list (including the audit log). */
  function attempt(fn: () => void, successMessage?: string, tone: 'success' | 'info' = 'success') {
    try {
      fn()
      if (successMessage) showToast(successMessage, tone)
    } catch (err) {
      showToast(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Action failed', 'info')
    }
    refreshAll()
  }

  function updateEventStatus(event: StoredEvent, newStatus: 'Published' | 'Draft' | 'Pending') {
    const action: AuditAction =
      newStatus === 'Published' ? 'EVENT_APPROVED' : event.status === 'Pending' ? 'EVENT_REJECTED' : 'EVENT_UNPUBLISHED'
    attempt(
      () => {
        recordAudit(admin, {
          action,
          targetType: 'event',
          targetId: String(event.id),
          targetLabel: event.title,
          details: `${event.status} → ${newStatus}`,
        })
        saveEvent({ ...event, status: newStatus })
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['events'] })
      },
      newStatus === 'Published' ? 'Event approved and published' : newStatus === 'Draft' ? 'Event rejected' : undefined,
      newStatus === 'Published' ? 'success' : 'info',
    )
  }

  // Flip between Published and Draft. Used by the Event Moderation table buttons.
  function toggleEventStatus(event: StoredEvent) {
    updateEventStatus(event, event.status === 'Published' ? 'Draft' : 'Published')
  }

  const defaultOpen = new Set<string>(
    [groups[0]?.key, groups[0]?.weeks[0]?.key].filter((k): k is string => !!k),
  )
  const isOpen = (key: string) => (expanded ?? defaultOpen).has(key)
  function toggleOpen(key: string) {
    const next = new Set(expanded ?? defaultOpen)
    if (next.has(key)) next.delete(key)
    else next.add(key)
    setExpanded(next)
  }

  function requestDelete(entries: AuditEntry[], title: string) {
    const deletable = entries.filter((e) => e.action !== 'LOGS_DELETED')
    if (deletable.length === 0) {
      showToast('Nothing to delete. Deletion records are protected.', 'info')
      return
    }
    setPendingDelete({ ids: deletable.map((e) => e.id), title })
  }

  function confirmDelete() {
    if (!pendingDelete) return
    const { ids, title } = pendingDelete
    setPendingDelete(null)
    attempt(() => {
      deleteAuditEntries(admin, ids, title)
    }, `${ids.length} audit record${ids.length === 1 ? '' : 's'} deleted`, 'info')
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 pb-16 pt-20">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 sm:px-6">
        <div className="mb-8">
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-extrabold sm:text-4xl text-navy-dark">Admin Control Center</h1>
          <p className="mt-2 text-slate-600">Moderate events, manage users and review the audit trail.</p>
        </div>

        <div className="mb-8 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
          <StatCard title="Total Platform Revenue" value={`MK ${totalRevenue.toLocaleString()}`} icon={<DollarSign />} />
          <StatCard title="Total Events" value={events.length.toString()} icon={<ShieldAlert />} />
        </div>

        <div className="mb-4 flex flex-wrap gap-x-6 gap-y-2 border-b border-slate-200">
          <button onClick={() => setActiveTab('events')} className={tabClass(activeTab === 'events')}>Event Moderation</button>
          <button onClick={() => setActiveTab('users')} className={tabClass(activeTab === 'users')}>User Management</button>
          <button onClick={() => setActiveTab('audit')} className={tabClass(activeTab === 'audit')}>Audit Log</button>
        </div>

        {activeTab === 'users' && <UserManagement />}

        {activeTab === 'events' && (
          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm sm:p-8">
            <h2 className="text-xl font-bold mb-4 text-navy-dark">Event Moderation</h2>
            <div className="overflow-x-auto">
              <table className="w-full text-left text-sm text-slate-600">
                <thead className="bg-slate-50 text-xs uppercase text-slate-500">
                  <tr>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3 rounded-tl-lg">Event Name</th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3">Organizer</th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3">Status</th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3 text-right rounded-tr-lg">Actions</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                  {events.length === 0 && (
                    <tr><td colSpan={4} className="px-4 py-8 text-center text-slate-500">No events to moderate yet.</td></tr>
                  )}
                  {events.map((event) => (
                    <tr key={event.id} className="border-b border-slate-100 last:border-0 hover:bg-slate-50">
                      <td className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-navy-dark">{event.title}</td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-3">{event.organizer}</td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-3">
                        <span className={`rounded-full px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold ${event.status === 'Published' ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700' : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-700'}`}>{event.status}</span>
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right">
                        {event.status === 'Published' ? (
                          <button onClick={() => toggleEventStatus(event)} className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg bg-red-600 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-white hover:bg-red-700 ml-auto"><Ban size={14} /> Unpublish</button>
                        ) : (
                          <button onClick={() => toggleEventStatus(event)} className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg bg-emerald-600 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-white hover:bg-emerald-700 ml-auto"><RotateCcw size={14} /> Publish</button>
                        )}
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {activeTab === 'audit' && (
          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm sm:p-8">
            <div className="mb-4 flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
              <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-navy-dark">Audit Log</h2>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold ${integrity.intact ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700' : 'bg-red-100 text-red-700'}`}>
                  {integrity.intact ? <ShieldCheck size={14} /> : <ShieldAlert size={14} />}
                  {integrity.intact ? 'Log integrity verified' : 'Log has been altered'}
                </span>
                <button
                  onClick={() => requestDelete(logs, 'all audit records')}
                  disabled={logs.length === 0}
                  className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg bg-red-600 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-white hover:bg-red-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  <Trash2 size={14} /> Delete all
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="mb-4 flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row">
              <input
                value={auditSearch}
                onChange={(e) => setAuditSearch(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Search by administrator, target or details"
                className="flex-1 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm"
              />
              <select
                value={auditFilter}
                onChange={(e) => setAuditFilter(e.target.value as 'all' | AuditAction)}
                className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm"
              >
                <option value="all">All actions</option>
                {(Object.keys(AUDIT_ACTION_LABELS) as AuditAction[]).map((key) => (
                  <option key={key} value={key}>{AUDIT_ACTION_LABELS[key]}</option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>

            {groups.length === 0 ? (
              <p className="rounded-lg bg-slate-50 px-4 py-8 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">
                No audit records match. Administrative actions will appear here as they happen.
              </p>
            ) : (
              <div className="max-h-[70vh] space-y-3 overflow-y-auto pr-1">
                {groups.map((month) => {
                  const monthOpen = isOpen(month.key)
                  return (
                    <div key={month.key} className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200">
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 bg-slate-50 px-4 py-3">
                        <button onClick={() => toggleOpen(month.key)} className="flex flex-1 items-center gap-2 text-left text-sm font-bold text-navy-dark">
                          {monthOpen ? <ChevronDown size={16} /> : <ChevronRight size={16} />}
                          {month.label}
                          <span className="rounded-full bg-white px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500">{month.count}</span>
                        </button>
                        <button
                          onClick={() => requestDelete(month.weeks.flatMap((w) => w.entries), `month of ${month.label}`)}
                          className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-semibold text-red-600 hover:underline"
                        >
                          <Trash2 size={13} /> Delete month
                        </button>
                      </div>

                      {monthOpen && (
                        <div className="space-y-2 p-3">
                          {month.weeks.map((week) => {
                            const weekOpen = isOpen(week.key)
                            return (
                              <div key={week.key} className="rounded-lg border border-slate-100">
                                <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 px-3 py-2">
                                  <button onClick={() => toggleOpen(week.key)} className="flex flex-1 items-center gap-2 text-left text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">
                                    {weekOpen ? <ChevronDown size={14} /> : <ChevronRight size={14} />}
                                    {week.label}
                                    <span className="rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500">{week.entries.length}</span>
                                  </button>
                                  <button
                                    onClick={() => requestDelete(week.entries, week.label.toLowerCase())}
                                    className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-semibold text-red-600 hover:underline"
                                  >
                                    <Trash2 size={13} /> Delete week
                                  </button>
                                </div>

                                {weekOpen && (
                                  <div className="max-h-72 overflow-auto border-t border-slate-100">
                                    <table className="w-full text-left text-sm text-slate-600">
                                      <thead className="sticky top-0 bg-slate-50 text-xs uppercase text-slate-500">
                                        <tr>
                                          <th className="px-4 py-2">Date and time</th>
                                          <th className="px-4 py-2">Administrator</th>
                                          <th className="px-4 py-2">Action</th>
                                          <th className="px-4 py-2">Affected</th>
                                          <th className="px-4 py-2">Details</th>
                                          <th className="px-4 py-2 text-right">Delete</th>
                                        </tr>
                                      </thead>
                                      <tbody>
                                        {week.entries.map((log) => (
                                          <tr key={log.id} className="border-b border-slate-100 last:border-0 hover:bg-slate-50">
                                            <td className="px-4 py-2 whitespace-nowrap">{new Date(log.timestamp).toLocaleString()}</td>
                                            <td className="px-4 py-2 font-semibold text-navy-dark">{log.actorName}</td>
                                            <td className="px-4 py-2">{AUDIT_ACTION_LABELS[log.action] ?? log.action}</td>
                                            <td className="px-4 py-2">{log.targetLabel} <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">({log.targetType})</span></td>
                                            <td className="px-4 py-2">{log.details || '—'}</td>
                                            <td className="px-4 py-2 text-right">
                                              {log.action === 'LOGS_DELETED' ? (
                                                <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">Protected</span>
                                              ) : (
                                                <button
                                                  onClick={() => requestDelete([log], 'a single record')}
                                                  aria-label="Delete this record"
                                                  className="text-red-600 hover:text-red-700"
                                                >
                                                  <Trash2 size={15} />
                                                </button>
                                              )}
                                            </td>
                                          </tr>
                                        ))}
                                      </tbody>
                                    </table>
                                  </div>
                                )}
                              </div>
                            )
                          })}
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  )
                })}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {pendingDelete && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50 px-4" role="dialog" aria-modal="true">
          <div className="w-full max-w-md rounded-2xl bg-white p-6 shadow-xl">
            <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
              <div className="rounded-full bg-red-100 p-2 text-red-600"><AlertTriangle size={20} /></div>
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-navy-dark">Delete audit records?</h3>
                <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-600">
                  You are about to permanently delete <strong>{pendingDelete.ids.length}</strong> record{pendingDelete.ids.length === 1 ? '' : 's'} ({pendingDelete.title}). This cannot be undone.
                </p>
                <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-600">
                  Deleting audit records reduces accountability. This deletion will be recorded in the log with your name and the time.
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="mt-6 flex justify-end gap-2">
              <button onClick={() => setPendingDelete(null)} className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50">Cancel</button>
              <button onClick={confirmDelete} className="rounded-lg bg-red-600 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-red-700">Yes, delete</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}


========================================================================================
FILE: src/features/authentication/AuthProvider.tsx
========================================================================================
import { useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { AuthContext } from "./useAuth";
import { getUser,loginRequest,logoutRequest,registerRequest,type User } from "./authStore";


export function AuthProvider({children}:{children: ReactNode}){
    const [user,setUser] = useState<User | null>(getUser);

    const login = async (email:string,password:string)=>{
        const next = await loginRequest(email,password);
        setUser(next);
        return next;
    };
    const register = async (name:string,email:string,password:string, role?: 'customer' | 'organizer') => {
        const next = await registerRequest(name,email,password,role);
        setUser(next);
        return next;
    };
    const logout = () => {
        logoutRequest();
        setUser(null);
    };
    return <AuthContext.Provider value={{user,login,register,logout}}>{children}</AuthContext.Provider>;
}

FILE: src/features/authentication/accounts.test.ts
