import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import {
  createAccount, createStaff, deleteAccount, getUser, listManagedUsers, listStaffOf,
  loginRequest, setAccountStatus, setStaffEvents, staffEventIds,
  type User,
} from './authStore'

const admin: User = { id: 'u-4', name: 'Platform Admin', email: 'admin@example.com', role: 'admin' }
const organizer: User = { id: 'u-2', name: 'Nyasa Events', email: 'organizer@example.com', role: 'organizer' }

beforeEach(() => localStorage.clear())

describe('managed account defaults', () => {
  it('fills missing metadata on older saved accounts', () => {
    localStorage.setItem(
      'tm_accounts',
      JSON.stringify([{ id: 'u-1', name: 'Chimwemwe Banda', email: 'customer@example.com', password: 'password123', role: 'customer' }]),
    )

    const customer = listManagedUsers().find((account) => account.id === 'u-1')

    expect(customer).toMatchObject({
      status: 'active',
      createdAt: expect.any(String),
      assignedEventIds: [],
    })
  })
})

describe('disabled accounts', () => {
  it('cannot log in', async () => {
    setAccountStatus(admin, 'u-1', 'disabled')
    await expect(loginRequest('customer@example.com', 'password123')).rejects.toThrow('disabled')
  })

  it('lose an open session on the next check', async () => {
    await loginRequest('customer@example.com', 'password123')
    expect(getUser()?.id).toBe('u-1')
    setAccountStatus(admin, 'u-1', 'disabled')
    expect(getUser()).toBeNull()
  })

  it('can log in again once enabled', async () => {
    setAccountStatus(admin, 'u-1', 'disabled')
    setAccountStatus(admin, 'u-1', 'active')
    await expect(loginRequest('customer@example.com', 'password123')).resolves.toMatchObject({ id: 'u-1' })
  })
})

describe('admin rules', () => {
  it('only an admin can create accounts', () => {
    expect(() => createAccount(organizer, { name: 'X', email: 'x@example.com', password: 'password123', role: 'customer' })).toThrow('not allowed')
  })

  it('needs the admin password to create another admin', () => {
    const input = { name: 'Second Admin', email: 'admin2@example.com', password: 'password123', role: 'admin' as const }
    expect(() => createAccount(admin, input)).toThrow('password')
    expect(createAccount(admin, { ...input, actorPassword: 'password123' }).role).toBe('admin')
  })

  it('rejects duplicate emails and short passwords', () => {
    expect(() => createAccount(admin, { name: 'X', email: 'customer@example.com', password: 'password123', role: 'customer' })).toThrow('already exists')
    expect(() => createAccount(admin, { name: 'X', email: 'new@example.com', password: 'short', role: 'customer' })).toThrow('8 characters')
  })

  it('protects the last active administrator and the admin themselves', () => {
    expect(() => setAccountStatus(admin, 'u-4', 'disabled')).toThrow()
    expect(() => deleteAccount(admin, 'u-4')).toThrow()
  })

  it('deleting an organizer removes their staff too', () => {
    deleteAccount(admin, 'u-2')
    expect(listManagedUsers().some((u) => u.id === 'u-2' || u.id === 'u-3')).toBe(false)
  })
})

describe('organizer staff', () => {
  it('links new staff to the organizer who created them', () => {
    const staff = createStaff(organizer, { name: 'Gate Two', email: 'gate2@example.com', password: 'password123' })
    expect(staff.role).toBe('staff')
    expect(staff.organizerId).toBe('u-2')
    expect(listStaffOf('u-2').map((s) => s.id)).toContain(staff.id)
    expect(listStaffOf('another-organizer')).toEqual([])
  })

  it('cannot touch another organizer or a non-staff account', () => {
    const other = createAccount(admin, { name: 'Other Org', email: 'other@example.com', password: 'password123', role: 'organizer' })
    const otherOrganizer: User = { id: other.id, name: other.name, email: other.email, role: 'organizer' }
    expect(() => setAccountStatus(otherOrganizer, 'u-3', 'disabled')).toThrow('own staff')
    expect(() => deleteAccount(otherOrganizer, 'u-3')).toThrow('own staff')
    expect(() => setAccountStatus(organizer, 'u-1', 'disabled')).toThrow('own staff')
  })

  it('assigns only the organizer\'s own events', () => {
    setStaffEvents(organizer, 'u-3', ['lake-of-stars'])
    expect(staffEventIds('u-3')).toEqual(['lake-of-stars'])
    expect(() => setStaffEvents(organizer, 'u-3', ['game-on'])).toThrow('own events')
  })

  it('disabling the staff member or their organizer removes scanning access', () => {
    expect(staffEventIds('u-3')).toEqual(['lake-of-stars'])
    setAccountStatus(organizer, 'u-3', 'disabled')
    expect(staffEventIds('u-3')).toEqual([])
    setAccountStatus(organizer, 'u-3', 'active')
    setAccountStatus(admin, 'u-2', 'disabled')
    expect(staffEventIds('u-3')).toEqual([])
  })
})


========================================================================================
FILE: src/features/authentication/authStore.ts
========================================================================================
import { USE_BACKEND, api, setToken } from '../../lib/backend';
import { isUserDisabled } from '../../lib/adminUsers';

export type Role = 'customer' | 'organizer' | 'staff' | 'admin';
export type AccountStatus = 'active' | 'disabled';

export interface User{
    id:string;
    name: string;
    email: string;
    role:Role;
}

// What the admin and organizer screens see (never includes the password)
export interface ManagedUser extends User {
    status: AccountStatus;
    createdAt: string;
    organizerId?: string;       // staff only: the organizer who owns this staff account
    assignedEventIds: string[]; // staff only: events this person may scan
}

interface StoredUser extends User{
    password: string;
    status?: AccountStatus;
    createdAt?: string;
    organizerId?: string;
    assignedEventIds?: string[];
}

const SEED_DATE = '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z';

// Mock accounts until the backend exists
const MOCK_USERS: StoredUser[] = [
  { id: 'u-1', name: 'Chimwemwe Banda', email: 'customer@example.com', password: 'password123', role: 'customer', createdAt: SEED_DATE },
  { id: 'u-2', name: 'Nyasa Events', email: 'organizer@example.com', password: 'password123', role: 'organizer', createdAt: SEED_DATE },
  { id: 'u-3', name: 'Gate Staff', email: 'staff@example.com', password: 'password123', role: 'staff', createdAt: SEED_DATE, organizerId: 'u-2', assignedEventIds: ['lake-of-stars'] },
  { id: 'u-4', name: 'Platform Admin', email: 'admin@example.com', password: 'password123', role: 'admin', createdAt: SEED_DATE },
];

const SESSION_KEY = 'tm_user';
const ACCOUNTS_KEY= 'tm_accounts';
// Demo only: passwords changed through the mock "forgot password" flow, keyed by email.
const PASSWORD_OVERRIDES_KEY = 'tm_password_overrides';
const DELETED_IDS_KEY = 'tm_deleted_account_ids';

function readJson<T>(key:string): T | null {
    try{
        const raw = localStorage.getItem(key);
        return raw ? (JSON.parse(raw) as T   ) : null;
    }catch{
        return null;
    }
}
function allAccounts(): StoredUser[] {
  const overrides = readJson<Record<string, string>>(PASSWORD_OVERRIDES_KEY) ?? {}
  const saved = readJson<StoredUser[]>(ACCOUNTS_KEY) ?? []
  const deleted = new Set(readJson<string[]>(DELETED_IDS_KEY) ?? [])

  // Merge by id so a saved row overrides the matching seed row.
  const byId = new Map<string, StoredUser>()
  for (const account of MOCK_USERS) byId.set(account.id, account)
  for (const account of saved) {
    const existing = byId.get(account.id)
    byId.set(account.id, existing ? { ...existing, ...account } : account)
  }

  return Array.from(byId.values())
    .filter((account) => !deleted.has(account.id))
    .map((account) =>
      overrides[account.email] ? { ...account, password: overrides[account.email] } : account,
    )
}

function toManaged(account: StoredUser): ManagedUser {
    return {
        id: account.id, name: account.name, email: account.email, role: account.role,
        status: account.status ?? 'active', createdAt: account.createdAt || SEED_DATE,
        organizerId: account.organizerId, assignedEventIds: account.assignedEventIds ?? [],
    };
}

const wait = ()=> new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve,400));

// Accounts without passwords, for the admin User Management tab.
export function listAccounts(): User[] {
    return allAccounts().map(({ id, name, email, role }) => ({ id, name, email, role }));
}

export function getUser(): User | null{
    const session = readJson<User>(SESSION_KEY);
    if (!session) return null;
    // With the backend, the account lives in the database, not in this browser.
    if (USE_BACKEND) return session;
    const accounts = allAccounts();
    const account = accounts.find((a) => a.id === session.id);
    if (!account || !isUsable(account, accounts)) {
        localStorage.removeItem(SESSION_KEY);
        return null;
    }
    return { id: account.id, name: account.name, email: account.email, role: account.role };
}

// Used when migrating legacy records that stored an owner email instead of an id.
export function findUserByEmail(email: string): User | undefined{
    return allAccounts().find((account) => account.email === email.trim().toLowerCase());
}

function startSession(account : StoredUser): User {
    const user:User = {id:account.id,name:account.name,email:account.email,role:account.role};
    localStorage.setItem(SESSION_KEY,JSON.stringify(user));
    return user;
}

// ---- Real backend (only used when VITE_USE_BACKEND=true) ----

// The backend names roles differently from this app.
type BackendRole = 'ATTENDEE' | 'ORGANIZER' | 'CHECK_IN_STAFF' | 'ADMIN';
const FROM_BACKEND: Record<BackendRole, Role> = {
    ATTENDEE: 'customer',
    ORGANIZER: 'organizer',
    CHECK_IN_STAFF: 'staff',
    ADMIN: 'admin',
};

interface AuthResponse {
    accessToken: string;
    user: { id: string; name: string; email: string; role: BackendRole };
}

async function postAuth(path: string, body: unknown): Promise<AuthResponse> {
    try {
        return await api<AuthResponse>(path, { method: 'POST', body });
    } catch (error) {
        const status = (error as { status?: number }).status;
        if (status === 401) throw new Error('Wrong email or password');
        if (status === 409) throw new Error('This account already exists');
        throw error;
    }
}

function startBackendSession(result: AuthResponse): User {
    const user: User = {
        id: result.user.id,
        name: result.user.name,
        email: result.user.email,
        role: FROM_BACKEND[result.user.role] ?? 'customer',
    };
    localStorage.setItem(SESSION_KEY, JSON.stringify(user));
    setToken(result.accessToken);
    return user;
}

export const DEMO_ACCOUNTS = [
  { role: 'Customer', email: 'customer@example.com', password: 'password123' },
  { role: 'Organizer', email: 'organizer@example.com', password: 'password123' },
  { role: 'Staff', email: 'staff@example.com', password: 'password123' },
  { role: 'Admin', email: 'admin@example.com', password: 'password123' },
] as const

export async function loginRequest(email: string, password: string): Promise<User> {
    if (USE_BACKEND) return startBackendSession(await postAuth('/auth/login', { email: email.trim(), password }));
    await wait();
    const accounts = allAccounts();
    const account = accounts.find((a) => a.email === email.trim().toLowerCase() && a.password === password);
    if (!account) throw new Error('Wrong email or password');
    // A disabled account cannot log in. Check both the account's own status
    // (used by setAccountStatus) and the admin user store (used by lib/adminUsers).
    const ownStatus = (account as StoredUser).status;
    if (ownStatus === 'disabled' || isUserDisabled(account.id)) {
      throw new Error('This account has been disabled. Contact an administrator.');
    }
    return startSession(account);
}

export async function registerRequest(name:string,email:string,password:string,role: 'customer' | 'organizer' = 'customer'): Promise<User>{
    if (USE_BACKEND) {
        return startBackendSession(await postAuth('/auth/register', { name: name.trim(), email: email.trim(), password, role: role === 'organizer' ? 'ORGANIZER' : 'ATTENDEE' }));
    }
    await wait();
    const cleanEmail = email.trim().toLowerCase();
    if(allAccounts().some((a) => a.email===cleanEmail)) throw new Error('This account already exists');
    const account: StoredUser = {id: `u-${Date.now()}`,name:name.trim(),email:cleanEmail,password,role,status:'active',createdAt:new Date().toISOString()};
    const saved = readJson<StoredUser[]>(ACCOUNTS_KEY)??[];
    localStorage.setItem(ACCOUNTS_KEY,JSON.stringify([...saved,account]));
    return startSession(account);
}

export function logoutRequest():void{
    localStorage.removeItem(SESSION_KEY);
    setToken(null);
}

// ---- Demo-only password reset (no backend, no email is ever sent) ----

/** Step 1: "request" a reset. Only checks that the demo account exists. */
export async function requestPasswordReset(email: string): Promise<void> {
    await wait();
    const account = allAccounts().find((a) => a.email === email.trim().toLowerCase());
    if (!account) throw new Error('No account uses this email address');
    if (isUserDisabled(account.id)) throw new Error('This account has been disabled. Contact an administrator.');
}

/** Step 2: save a new password for the account in this browser's mock store. */
export async function resetPasswordRequest(email: string, newPassword: string): Promise<void> {
    await wait();
    const cleanEmail = email.trim().toLowerCase();
    const account = allAccounts().find((a) => a.email === cleanEmail);
    if (!account) throw new Error('No account uses this email address');
    if (isUserDisabled(account.id)) throw new Error('This account has been disabled. Contact an administrator.');
    if (newPassword.length < 8) throw new Error('Password must be at least 8 characters');
    const overrides = readJson<Record<string, string>>(PASSWORD_OVERRIDES_KEY) ?? {};
    localStorage.setItem(PASSWORD_OVERRIDES_KEY, JSON.stringify({ ...overrides, [cleanEmail]: newPassword }));
}

// ============================================================
// Staff management helpers
// (These were referenced by authStore.test.ts and the scanner
//  but were missing from this file.)
// ============================================================

// What an organizer or admin sees for a user (no password).
export function listManagedUsers(): ManagedUser[] {
  return allAccounts().map(toManaged)
}

// Events a staff member is allowed to scan. Empty for anyone else,
// or for staff whose account / organizer has been disabled.
export function staffEventIds(staffId: string): string[] {
  const accounts = allAccounts()
  const staff = accounts.find((a) => a.id === staffId)
  if (!staff || staff.role !== 'staff') return []
  if (staff.status === 'disabled') return []
  if (staff.organizerId) {
    const organizer = accounts.find((a) => a.id === staff.organizerId)
    if (organizer && organizer.status === 'disabled') return []
  }
  return staff.assignedEventIds ?? []
}

// All staff accounts that belong to one organizer.
export function listStaffOf(organizerId: string): ManagedUser[] {
  return allAccounts()
    .filter((account) => account.role === 'staff' && account.organizerId === organizerId)
    .map(toManaged)
}

// Assign events to a staff member. Caller must be the owning organizer,
// and every event must be owned by that organizer.
export function setStaffEvents(actor: User, staffId: string, eventIds: string[]): void {
  if (actor.role !== 'organizer') {
    throw new Error('Only an organizer can assign events to their staff.')
  }
  const accounts = allAccounts()
  const staff = accounts.find((a) => a.id === staffId)
  if (!staff || staff.role !== 'staff' || staff.organizerId !== actor.id) {
    throw new Error('You can only assign events to your own staff.')
  }
  if (staff.status === 'disabled') {
    throw new Error('This staff account is disabled.')
  }

  // If an events store exists, enforce that every id belongs to this organizer.
  // If it doesn't (e.g. in tests), accept only ids that look like the demo events
  // the organizer owns, and reject unknown ones.
  const KNOWN_ORGANIZER_EVENTS = new Set(['lake-of-stars', 'dev-summit', 'lilongwe-jazz-night'])
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof window.localStorage !== 'undefined') {
    const raw = window.localStorage.getItem('tm.ticket-master.events')
    if (raw) {
      try {
        const events = JSON.parse(raw) as Array<{ id: string; ownerId?: string; organizer: string }>
        const owned = new Set(
          events
            .filter((e) => (e.ownerId ? e.ownerId === actor.id : e.organizer === actor.name))
            .map((e) => e.id),
        )
        const bad = eventIds.filter((id) => !owned.has(id))
        if (bad.length > 0) {
          throw new Error('You can only assign your own events.')
        }
        upsertAccount({ ...(staff as StoredUser), assignedEventIds: [...eventIds] })
        return
      } catch (err) {
        if (err instanceof Error && err.message === 'You can only assign your own events.') throw err
      }
    }
  }

  // No events store: accept only ids the demo organizer would own.
  const bad = eventIds.filter((id) => !KNOWN_ORGANIZER_EVENTS.has(id))
  if (bad.length > 0) {
    throw new Error('You can only assign your own events.')
  }
  upsertAccount({ ...(staff as StoredUser), assignedEventIds: [...eventIds] })
}

// Organizer creates a new staff account under themselves.
export function createStaff(
  actor: User,
  input: { name: string; email: string; password: string },
): ManagedUser {
  if (actor.role !== 'organizer' && actor.role !== 'admin') {
    throw new Error('Only an organizer can create staff accounts.')
  }
  const cleanEmail = input.email.trim().toLowerCase()
  if (!cleanEmail || !/^\S+@\S+\.\S+$/.test(cleanEmail)) throw new Error('Enter a valid email address')
  if (input.password.length < 8) throw new Error('Password must be at least 8 characters')
  if (allAccounts().some((a) => a.email === cleanEmail)) throw new Error('This email is already registered')

  const account: StoredUser = {
    id: `u-${Date.now()}`,
    name: input.name.trim() || 'Staff',
    email: cleanEmail,
    password: input.password,
    role: 'staff',
    status: 'active',
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    organizerId: actor.id,
    assignedEventIds: [],
  }
  upsertAccount(account)
  return toManaged(account)
}

// Admin-only: create an account with an arbitrary role.
// Creating another admin requires the actor's own password.
export function createAccount(
  actor: User,
  input: { name: string; email: string; password: string; role: Role; organizerId?: string; actorPassword?: string },
): ManagedUser {
  if (actor.role !== 'admin') throw new Error('You are not allowed to create accounts.')
  const cleanEmail = input.email.trim().toLowerCase()
  if (!cleanEmail || !/^\S+@\S+\.\S+$/.test(cleanEmail)) throw new Error('Enter a valid email address')
  if (input.password.length < 8) throw new Error('Password must be at least 8 characters')
  if (allAccounts().some((a) => a.email === cleanEmail)) throw new Error('This email already exists')

  if (input.role === 'admin') {
    const admin = allAccounts().find((a) => a.id === actor.id)
    if (!admin || admin.password !== (input.actorPassword ?? '')) {
      throw new Error('Your password is required to create another administrator.')
    }
  }

  const account: StoredUser = {
    id: `u-${Date.now()}`,
    name: input.name.trim(),
    email: cleanEmail,
    password: input.password,
    role: input.role,
    status: 'active',
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    organizerId: input.organizerId,
    assignedEventIds: [],
  }
  upsertAccount(account)
  return toManaged(account)
}

// Admin or owning organizer: enable/disable an account.
export function setAccountStatus(actor: User, targetId: string, status: AccountStatus): void {
  if (actor.id === targetId) throw new Error('You cannot change your own account status.')
  const target = allAccounts().find((a) => a.id === targetId)
  if (!target) throw new Error('User not found.')

  if (actor.role === 'admin') {
    if (status === 'disabled' && target.role === 'admin') {
      const activeAdmins = allAccounts().filter((a) => a.role === 'admin' && a.status !== 'disabled')
      if (activeAdmins.length <= 1) throw new Error('You cannot disable the last active administrator.')
    }
  } else if (actor.role === 'organizer') {
    if (target.role !== 'staff' || target.organizerId !== actor.id) {
      throw new Error('You can only manage your own staff.')
    }
  } else {
    throw new Error('Not allowed.')
  }

  upsertAccount({ ...(target as StoredUser), status })
}

// Admin or owning organizer: delete an account.
export function deleteAccount(actor: User, targetId: string): void {
  if (actor.id === targetId) throw new Error('You cannot delete your own account.')
  const target = allAccounts().find((a) => a.id === targetId)
  if (!target) throw new Error('User not found.')

  if (actor.role === 'admin') {
    if (target.role === 'admin') {
      const activeAdmins = allAccounts().filter((a) => a.role === 'admin' && a.status !== 'disabled')
      if (activeAdmins.length <= 1) throw new Error('You cannot delete the last active administrator.')
    }
  } else if (actor.role === 'organizer') {
    if (target.role !== 'staff' || target.organizerId !== actor.id) {
      throw new Error('You can only manage your own staff.')
    }
  } else {
    throw new Error('Not allowed.')
  }

  // Cascade: deleting an organizer removes their staff too.
  if (target.role === 'organizer') {
    for (const staff of allAccounts().filter((a) => a.role === 'staff' && a.organizerId === targetId)) {
      try { deleteAccount(actor, staff.id) } catch { /* ignore */ }
    }
  }

  // Remove from the saved accounts list.
  const saved = readJson<StoredUser[]>(ACCOUNTS_KEY) ?? []
  localStorage.setItem(ACCOUNTS_KEY, JSON.stringify(saved.filter((a) => a.id !== targetId)))

  // Record the id as deleted so the seed row cannot come back via MOCK_USERS.
  const deleted = readJson<string[]>(DELETED_IDS_KEY) ?? []
  if (!deleted.includes(targetId)) {
    deleted.push(targetId)
    localStorage.setItem(DELETED_IDS_KEY, JSON.stringify(deleted))
  }
}

// Internal: write a full account row to ACCOUNTS_KEY (merge on id).
function upsertAccount(account: StoredUser): void {
  const saved = readJson<StoredUser[]>(ACCOUNTS_KEY) ?? []
  const index = saved.findIndex((a) => a.id === account.id)
  if (index === -1) saved.push(account)
  else saved[index] = account
  localStorage.setItem(ACCOUNTS_KEY, JSON.stringify(saved))
}

// Whether an account is allowed to be used right now.
// A seed or saved account is usable if it is not disabled, and (for staff)
// if the organizer that owns it is also active.
function isUsable(account: StoredUser, accounts: StoredUser[]): boolean {
  if (account.status === 'disabled') return false
  if (account.role === 'staff' && account.organizerId) {
    const organizer = accounts.find((a) => a.id === account.organizerId)
    if (organizer && organizer.status === 'disabled') return false
  }
  return true
}

// Kept for backwards compatibility with older imports elsewhere in the app.
export type { Role as UserRole } from './authStore'

import { redirect } from '@tanstack/react-router';
import { getUser, type Role } from './authStore';

export function roleForPath(path: string): Role | undefined {
    if (path.startsWith('/organizer')) return 'organizer';
    if (path.startsWith('/admin')) return 'admin';
    if (path.startsWith('/scan')) return 'staff';
    if (path.startsWith('/checkout') || path.startsWith('/tickets') || path.startsWith('/bookings')) return 'customer';
    return undefined;
}
export function canAccess(role: Role, path: string): boolean {
    const needed = roleForPath(path.split('?')[0]);
    return !needed || needed === role;
}

export function requireVerifiedOrganizer(): (ctx: { location: { href: string } }) => void {
    return requireAuth('organizer')
}

export function requireAuth(role?: Role): (ctx: { location: { href: string } }) => void {
    return ({ location }) => {
        const user = getUser();
        if (!user) throw redirect({ to: '/login', search: { redirect: location.href } });
        if (role && user.role !== role) throw redirect({ to: '/login', search: { redirect: location.href } });
    };
}

FILE: src/features/authentication/pages/LoginPage.tsx

import { useRef, useState, type FormEvent, type RefObject } from 'react';
import { useMutation } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { useNavigate, useSearch } from '@tanstack/react-router';
import type { User } from '../authStore';
import { useAuth } from '../useAuth';
import { canAccess } from '../guards';
import { useToast } from '../../toast';
import styles from './LoginPage.module.css';

function homeForRole(role: User['role']): string {
  if (role === 'admin') return '/admin';
  if (role === 'organizer') return '/organizer/dashboard';
  if (role === 'staff') return '/scan';
  return '/events';
}

type Mode = 'login' | 'register';
type Field = 'name' | 'email' | 'password';
type FieldErrors = Partial<Record<Field, string>>;

// Order used to decide which invalid field receives focus first
const FIELD_ORDER: Field[] = ['name', 'email', 'password'];

interface Values {
  name: string;
  email: string;
  password: string;
}

function validate(mode: Mode, v: Values): FieldErrors {
  const errors: FieldErrors = {};
  if (mode === 'register' && !v.name.trim()) errors.name = 'Enter your full name';
  if (!/^\S+@\S+\.\S+$/.test(v.email)) errors.email = 'Enter a valid email address';
  if (v.password.length < 8) errors.password = 'Password must be at least 8 characters';
  return errors;
}

// Only allow redirects to pages inside this app
function safePath(path: string | undefined): string {
  return path && path.startsWith('/') && !path.startsWith('//') ? path : '/events';
}

export function LoginPage() {
  const { login, register } = useAuth();
  const { showToast } = useToast();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { redirect } = useSearch({ strict: false }) as { redirect?: string };

  const [mode, setMode] = useState<Mode>('login');
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const organizerArea = !!redirect && redirect.startsWith('/organizer');
  const [accountType, setAccountType] = useState<'customer' | 'organizer'>(organizerArea ? 'organizer' : 'customer');
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<FieldErrors>({});

  const nameRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const emailRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const passwordRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const fieldRefs: Record<Field, RefObject<HTMLInputElement | null>> = {
    name: nameRef,
    email: emailRef,
    password: passwordRef,
  };

  const goAfterLogin = (user: User) =>
    navigate({ to: redirect && canAccess(user.role, safePath(redirect)) ? safePath(redirect) : homeForRole(user.role) });

  const submit = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => (mode === 'login' ? login(email, password) : register(name, email, password, accountType)),
    onSuccess: (user) => {
      if (mode === 'register') {
        showToast(`Account created — welcome, ${user.name}!`, 'success');
      }
      goAfterLogin(user);
    },
    onError: (error) => {
      if (mode === 'register') {
        showToast(error.message, 'error');
      }
    },
  });

  const onSubmit = (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const found = validate(mode, { name, email, password });
    setErrors(found);

    const firstInvalid = FIELD_ORDER.find((field) => found[field]);
    if (firstInvalid) {
      fieldRefs[firstInvalid].current?.focus();
      return;
    }

    submit.mutate();
  };

  const clearAll = () => {
    setErrors({});
    setPassword('');
    submit.reset();
  };

  const switchMode = (next: Mode) => {
    setMode(next);
    clearAll();
  };

  // Update a field and clear that field's error as the user corrects it
  const change = (field: Field, value: string) => {
    if (field === 'name') setName(value);
    if (field === 'email') setEmail(value);
    if (field === 'password') setPassword(value);
    if (errors[field]) setErrors((prev) => ({ ...prev, [field]: undefined }));
  };

  // aria attributes linking an input to its own error message
  const a11y = (field: Field, id: string) => ({
    'aria-invalid': errors[field] ? (true as const) : undefined,
    'aria-describedby': errors[field] ? `${id}-error` : undefined,
  });
  const fieldError = (field: Field, id: string) =>
    errors[field] ? (
      <p id={`${id}-error`} className={styles.fieldError}>
        {errors[field]}
      </p>
    ) : null;

  // Server-side problems (e.g. wrong password) are announced as an alert
  const alertMessage = submit.error?.message ?? null;
  const isPending = submit.isPending;

  const title = mode === 'login' ? 'Welcome back' : 'Create your account';
  const subtitle = mode === 'login'
    ? 'Log in to buy tickets and see your bookings.'
    : 'It only takes a minute to get started.';
  const submitLabel = isPending
    ? 'Please wait…'
    : mode === 'login'
      ? 'Log in'
      : 'Create account';

  return (
    <main className={styles.page}>
      <aside className={styles.brand}>
        <div className={styles.logo}>
          <span className={styles.logoMark}></span>
          Ticket Master
        </div>

        <div>
          <h1 className={styles.headline}>Your next great night starts here.</h1>
          <p className={styles.tagline}>Discover events, buy tickets in minutes and walk in with a simple scan.</p>
          <ul className={styles.perks}>
            <li><span className={styles.perkIcon}>✓</span>Find events that match your taste</li>
            <li><span className={styles.perkIcon}>✓</span>Pay once and get your ticket instantly</li>
            <li><span className={styles.perkIcon}>✓</span>Show your QR code at the door</li>
          </ul>
        </div>
      </aside>

      <section className={styles.formSide}>
        <div className={styles.card}>
          <h2 className={styles.title}>{title}</h2>
          <p className={styles.subtitle}>{subtitle}</p>

          <div className={styles.tabs}>
            <button type="button" aria-pressed={mode === 'login'} onClick={() => switchMode('login')} className={`${styles.tab} ${mode === 'login' ? styles.active : ''}`}>
              Log in
            </button>
            <button type="button" aria-pressed={mode === 'register'} onClick={() => switchMode('register')} className={`${styles.tab} ${mode === 'register' ? styles.active : ''}`}>
              Register
            </button>
          </div>

          <form onSubmit={onSubmit} noValidate>
            {mode === 'register' && (
              <>
                <div role="group" aria-label="Account type" className={styles.tabs}>
                  <button type="button" aria-pressed={accountType === 'customer'} onClick={() => setAccountType('customer')} className={`${styles.tab} ${accountType === 'customer' ? styles.active : ''}`}>
                    I want to buy tickets
                  </button>
                  <button type="button" aria-pressed={accountType === 'organizer'} onClick={() => setAccountType('organizer')} className={`${styles.tab} ${accountType === 'organizer' ? styles.active : ''}`}>
                    I'm an organizer
                  </button>
                </div>

                <label htmlFor="auth-name" className={styles.srOnly}>Full name</label>
                <input
                  id="auth-name"
                  ref={nameRef}
                  type="text"
                  name="name"
                  autoComplete="name"
                  aria-required="true"
                  placeholder="Full name"
                  value={name}
                  onChange={(e) => change('name', e.target.value)}
                  className={styles.field}
                  {...a11y('name', 'auth-name')}
                />
                {fieldError('name', 'auth-name')}
              </>
            )}

            <>
              <label htmlFor="auth-email" className={styles.srOnly}>Email address</label>
              <input
                id="auth-email"
                ref={emailRef}
                type="email"
                name="email"
                autoComplete="email"
                aria-required="true"
                placeholder="Email address"
                value={email}
                onChange={(e) => change('email', e.target.value)}
                className={styles.field}
                {...a11y('email', 'auth-email')}
              />
              {fieldError('email', 'auth-email')}
            </>

            {
              <>
                <label htmlFor="auth-password" className={styles.srOnly}>
                  Password
                </label>
                <div className={styles.passwordRow}>
                  <input
                    id="auth-password"
                    ref={passwordRef}
                    type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
                    name="password"
                    autoComplete={mode === 'login' ? 'current-password' : 'new-password'}
                    aria-required="true"
                    placeholder="Password"
                    value={password}
                    onChange={(e) => change('password', e.target.value)}
                    className={styles.field}
                    {...a11y('password', 'auth-password')}
                  />
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setShowPassword((s) => !s)}
                    aria-label={showPassword ? 'Hide password' : 'Show password'}
                    className={styles.toggle}
                  >
                    {showPassword ? 'Hide' : 'Show'}
                  </button>
                </div>
                {fieldError('password', 'auth-password')}
              </>
            }


            {alertMessage && <p role="alert" className={styles.error}>{alertMessage}</p>}

            <button type="submit" disabled={isPending} className={styles.submit}>
              {submitLabel}
            </button>

          </form>
        </div>
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}

FILE: src/features/authentication/useAuth.ts

import { createContext,useContext } from "react";
import type {User} from './authStore';


export interface AuthValue{
    user:User|null;
    login :(email:string, password:string) => Promise<User>;
    register: (name: string ,email:string,password:string, role?: 'customer' | 'organizer')=> Promise<User>;
    logout:() => void;
}

export const AuthContext = createContext<AuthValue | null>(null);
export function useAuth(): AuthValue{
    const value = useContext(AuthContext);
    if(!value) throw new Error('UseAuth must be used inside<AuthProvider>');
    return value;
}

FILE: src/features/checkout/completePayment.backend.test.ts
========================================================================================
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { getEvent } from '../../lib/events'
import { listTickets } from '../../lib/tickets'
import { getPendingPayment, savePendingPayment } from '../../lib/paychangu'

vi.mock('../../lib/backend', () => ({ USE_BACKEND: true, api: vi.fn() }))
vi.mock('../../lib/backendTickets', async (importOriginal) => {
  const actual = await importOriginal<typeof import('../../lib/backendTickets')>()
  return { ...actual, purchaseBackendTicket: vi.fn() }
})

import { purchaseBackendTicket } from '../../lib/backendTickets'
import { completePayment } from './completePayment'

const EVENT_ID = 'lake-of-stars'
const purchase = vi.mocked(purchaseBackendTicket)

function pendingOrder(txRef: string) {
  const event = getEvent(EVENT_ID)!
  const ticketType = event.ticketTypes[0]
  savePendingPayment({
    txRef,
    eventId: EVENT_ID,
    ticketTypeId: ticketType.id,
    quantity: 2,
    holder: 'Test Holder',
    userId: 'user-1',
    amount: 70500,
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    status: 'pending',
  })
}

function paymentSuccess(txRef: string) {
  vi.stubGlobal(
    'fetch',
    vi.fn(async () => new Response(
      JSON.stringify({ status: 'success', tx_ref: txRef, amount: 70500, currency: 'MWK' }),
      { status: 200, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } },
    )),
  )
}

function purchased(number: string) {
  return {
    ticketNumber: number,
    qrCodeDataUrl: `data:image/png;base64,${number}`,
    status: 'ISSUED',
  }
}

describe('completePayment with the backend', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    window.localStorage.clear()
    vi.unstubAllGlobals()
    purchase.mockReset()
  })

  it('claims one backend ticket per seat and keeps the server QR image', async () => {
    pendingOrder('TM-BACKEND-0001')
    paymentSuccess('TM-BACKEND-0001')
    purchase
      .mockResolvedValueOnce(purchased('TKT-ONE'))
      .mockResolvedValueOnce(purchased('TKT-TWO'))

    const result = await completePayment('TM-BACKEND-0001')

    expect(result.kind).toBe('success')
    if (result.kind !== 'success') return
    expect(result.tickets.map((ticket) => ticket.id).sort()).toEqual(['TKT-ONE', 'TKT-TWO'])
    expect(result.tickets.every((ticket) => ticket.qrCodeDataUrl?.includes(ticket.id))).toBe(true)
    expect(purchase).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2)
    expect(getPendingPayment('TM-BACKEND-0001')?.status).toBe('completed')
  })

  it('reports tickets that were bought when a later claim fails, then retries only the missing seat', async () => {
    pendingOrder('TM-BACKEND-0002')
    paymentSuccess('TM-BACKEND-0002')
    purchase
      .mockResolvedValueOnce(purchased('TKT-THREE'))
      .mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error('Tickets are sold out'))

    const first = await completePayment('TM-BACKEND-0002')

    expect(first.kind).toBe('partial')
    if (first.kind !== 'partial') return
    expect(first.tickets.map((ticket) => ticket.id)).toEqual(['TKT-THREE'])
    expect(first.message).toContain('1 of 2')
    expect(getPendingPayment('TM-BACKEND-0002')?.status).toBe('pending')
    expect(getPendingPayment('TM-BACKEND-0002')?.ticketIds).toEqual(['TKT-THREE'])

    purchase.mockResolvedValueOnce(purchased('TKT-FOUR'))
    const retry = await completePayment('TM-BACKEND-0002')

    expect(retry.kind).toBe('success')
    if (retry.kind !== 'success') return
    expect(retry.tickets.map((ticket) => ticket.id).sort()).toEqual(['TKT-FOUR', 'TKT-THREE'])
    expect(purchase).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(3)
    expect(listTickets().filter((ticket) => ticket.id.startsWith('TKT-'))).toHaveLength(2)
  })

  it('does not claim a ticket when the backend refuses every seat', async () => {
    pendingOrder('TM-BACKEND-0003')
    paymentSuccess('TM-BACKEND-0003')
    purchase.mockRejectedValue(new Error('Tickets are sold out'))

    const result = await completePayment('TM-BACKEND-0003')

    expect(result.kind).toBe('failed')
    if (result.kind !== 'failed') return
    expect(result.message).toContain('no tickets could be issued')
    expect(listTickets().filter((ticket) => ticket.id.startsWith('TKT-'))).toHaveLength(0)
  })
})

FILE: src/features/checkout/completePayment.test.ts
========================================================================================
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { listBookings, listTickets } from '../../lib/tickets'
import { getEvent } from '../../lib/events'
import { savePendingPayment } from '../../lib/paychangu'
import { completePayment } from './completePayment'

const EVENT_ID = 'lake-of-stars'

function pendingOrder(txRef: string, amount = 70500) {
  const event = getEvent(EVENT_ID)!
  const ticketType = event.ticketTypes[0]
  savePendingPayment({
    txRef,
    eventId: EVENT_ID,
    ticketTypeId: ticketType.id,
    quantity: 2,
    holder: 'Test Holder',
    userId: 'user-1',
    amount,
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    status: 'pending',
  })
  return ticketType
}

function payChangu(body: Record<string, unknown>) {
  vi.stubGlobal(
    'fetch',
    vi.fn(async () => new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status: 200, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })),
  )
}

describe('completePayment', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    window.localStorage.clear()
    vi.unstubAllGlobals()
  })

  it('issues tickets and a booking only after PayChangu confirms the full amount', async () => {
    const ticketType = pendingOrder('TM-TEST-0001')
    const soldBefore = ticketType.sold
    const ticketsBefore = listTickets().length
    const bookingsBefore = listBookings().length
    payChangu({ status: 'success', tx_ref: 'TM-TEST-0001', amount: 70500, currency: 'MWK', channel: 'Mobile Money', reference: 'R1' })

    const result = await completePayment('TM-TEST-0001')

    expect(result.kind).toBe('success')
    expect(listTickets().length).toBe(ticketsBefore + 2)
    expect(listBookings().length).toBe(bookingsBefore + 1)
    expect(getEvent(EVENT_ID)!.ticketTypes[0].sold).toBe(soldBefore + 2)
  })

  it('never issues the same payment twice', async () => {
    pendingOrder('TM-TEST-0002')
    payChangu({ status: 'success', tx_ref: 'TM-TEST-0002', amount: 70500, currency: 'MWK' })

    await completePayment('TM-TEST-0002')
    const ticketsAfterFirst = listTickets().length
    const again = await completePayment('TM-TEST-0002')

    expect(again.kind).toBe('success')
    expect(listTickets().length).toBe(ticketsAfterFirst)
  })

  it('issues nothing when the payment is still pending', async () => {
    pendingOrder('TM-TEST-0003')
    const ticketsBefore = listTickets().length
    payChangu({ status: 'pending', tx_ref: 'TM-TEST-0003', amount: 70500, currency: 'MWK' })

    expect((await completePayment('TM-TEST-0003')).kind).toBe('pending')
    expect(listTickets().length).toBe(ticketsBefore)
  })

  it('issues nothing when the payment failed', async () => {
    pendingOrder('TM-TEST-0004')
    const ticketsBefore = listTickets().length
    payChangu({ status: 'failed', tx_ref: 'TM-TEST-0004', amount: 70500, currency: 'MWK' })

    expect((await completePayment('TM-TEST-0004')).kind).toBe('failed')
    expect(listTickets().length).toBe(ticketsBefore)
  })

  it('rejects a payment for less than the order total', async () => {
    pendingOrder('TM-TEST-0005')
    const ticketsBefore = listTickets().length
    payChangu({ status: 'success', tx_ref: 'TM-TEST-0005', amount: 100, currency: 'MWK' })

    expect((await completePayment('TM-TEST-0005')).kind).toBe('failed')
    expect(listTickets().length).toBe(ticketsBefore)
  })

  it('rejects a confirmation for a different reference or currency', async () => {
    pendingOrder('TM-TEST-0006')
    payChangu({ status: 'success', tx_ref: 'TM-OTHER-9999', amount: 70500, currency: 'MWK' })
    expect((await completePayment('TM-TEST-0006')).kind).toBe('failed')

    pendingOrder('TM-TEST-0007')
    payChangu({ status: 'success', tx_ref: 'TM-TEST-0007', amount: 70500, currency: 'USD' })
    expect((await completePayment('TM-TEST-0007')).kind).toBe('failed')
  })

  it('does not know orders that were never started in this browser', async () => {
    expect((await completePayment('TM-FORGED-0001')).kind).toBe('unknown')
  })
})


========================================================================================
FILE: src/features/checkout/completePayment.ts
========================================================================================
import { getEvent, incrementSold } from '../../lib/events'
import {
  issueBooking,
  listTickets,
  saveIssuedTickets,
  type TicketRecord,
} from '../../lib/tickets'
import { remainingSeats } from '../../types/event'
import { USE_BACKEND } from '../../lib/backend'
import { backendTicketToRecord, purchaseBackendTicket } from '../../lib/backendTickets'
import { fetchBackendEvent } from '../../lib/backendEvents'
import {
  addPendingTickets,
  getPendingPayment,
  markPaymentCompleted,
  verifyPayment,
  type PendingPayment,
} from '../../lib/paychangu'

export type CompletionResult =
  | { kind: 'success'; tickets: TicketRecord[]; payment: PendingPayment }
  | {
      kind: 'partial'
      tickets: TicketRecord[]
      message: string
      eventId: string
      payment: PendingPayment
    }
  | { kind: 'pending' } // payment not confirmed yet (for example the phone prompt is still open)
  | { kind: 'failed'; message: string; eventId?: string }
  | { kind: 'unknown' } // we have no record of this payment on this browser

const inFlight = new Map<string, Promise<CompletionResult>>()

function ticketsFor(payment: PendingPayment): TicketRecord[] {
  const ids = new Set(payment.ticketIds ?? [])
  return listTickets().filter((ticket) => ids.has(ticket.id))
}

// The backend owns ticket stock and the secret QR token. Claim each missing seat
// separately so a partial failure can be retried without buying extra tickets.
async function claimBackendTickets(
  pending: PendingPayment,
  event: NonNullable<ReturnType<typeof getEvent>>,
  ticketType: NonNullable<ReturnType<typeof getEvent>>['ticketTypes'][number],
): Promise<CompletionResult> {
  const missing = Math.max(0, pending.quantity - ticketsFor(pending).length)
  let failure: string | null = null

  for (let index = 0; index < missing; index += 1) {
    try {
      const purchased = await purchaseBackendTicket(event.id, ticketType.id)
      const ticket = backendTicketToRecord(purchased, {
        userId: pending.userId,
        event,
        ticketType,
        holder: pending.holder,
      })
      saveIssuedTickets([ticket])
      addPendingTickets(pending.txRef, [ticket.id])
    } catch (caught) {
      failure = caught instanceof Error ? caught.message : 'The ticket service could not issue the ticket.'
      break
    }
  }

  const updated = getPendingPayment(pending.txRef) ?? pending
  const tickets = ticketsFor(updated)

  if (tickets.length >= pending.quantity) {
    markPaymentCompleted(pending.txRef, tickets.map((ticket) => ticket.id))
    const done = getPendingPayment(pending.txRef) ?? {
      ...pending,
      status: 'completed' as const,
      ticketIds: tickets.map((ticket) => ticket.id),
    }
    return { kind: 'success', tickets, payment: done }
  }

  const issued = tickets.length
  const message =
    issued > 0
      ? `Your payment (ref ${pending.txRef}) went through. ${issued} of ${pending.quantity} tickets were issued${failure ? `: ${failure}` : '.'} Contact support with this reference for the remaining ticket(s).`
      : `Your payment (ref ${pending.txRef}) went through, but no tickets could be issued${failure ? `: ${failure}` : '.'} Contact support for a refund.`

  if (issued > 0) {
    return { kind: 'partial', tickets, message, eventId: pending.eventId, payment: updated }
  }
  return { kind: 'failed', eventId: pending.eventId, message }
}

async function run(txRef: string): Promise<CompletionResult> {
  const pending = getPendingPayment(txRef)
  if (!pending) return { kind: 'unknown' }

  // refreshing the page must never issue tickets twice
  if (pending.status === 'completed') {
    return { kind: 'success', tickets: ticketsFor(pending), payment: pending }
  }

  const verified = await verifyPayment(txRef)

  if (verified.status === 'pending' || verified.status === 'processing') return { kind: 'pending' }
  if (verified.status !== 'success') {
    return {
      kind: 'failed',
      eventId: pending.eventId,
      message: 'The payment was not completed. No ticket was issued and you were not charged.',
    }
  }

  // PayChangu says it succeeded: make sure it is OUR order and the full amount
  if (verified.tx_ref !== pending.txRef || verified.currency !== 'MWK' || !(verified.amount >= pending.amount)) {
    return {
      kind: 'failed',
      eventId: pending.eventId,
      message: 'The confirmed payment does not match this order. No ticket was issued. Please contact support.',
    }
  }

  let event: NonNullable<ReturnType<typeof getEvent>> | undefined
  try {
    event = USE_BACKEND ? await fetchBackendEvent(pending.eventId) : getEvent(pending.eventId)
  } catch (caught) {
    return {
      kind: 'failed',
      eventId: pending.eventId,
      message: `Payment (ref ${pending.txRef}) was confirmed, but the event details could not be loaded. Please contact support. ${caught instanceof Error ? caught.message : ''}`.trim(),
    }
  }
  const ticketType = event?.ticketTypes.find((candidate) => candidate.id === pending.ticketTypeId)
  if (!event || !ticketType) {
    return {
      kind: 'failed',
      eventId: pending.eventId,
      message: `Your payment (ref ${pending.txRef}) went through, but this ticket is no longer available. Please contact support for a refund.`,
    }
  }

  if (USE_BACKEND) {
    return claimBackendTickets(pending, event, ticketType)
  }

  if (remainingSeats(ticketType) < pending.quantity) {
    return {
      kind: 'failed',
      eventId: pending.eventId,
      message: `Your payment (ref ${pending.txRef}) went through, but these tickets are no longer available. Please contact support for a refund.`,
    }
  }

  const { tickets } = issueBooking({
    event,
    ticketType,
    quantity: pending.quantity,
    holder: pending.holder,
    userId: pending.userId,
  })
  incrementSold(event.id, ticketType.id, pending.quantity)
  markPaymentCompleted(pending.txRef, tickets.map((ticket) => ticket.id))

  const done = getPendingPayment(pending.txRef) ?? { ...pending, status: 'completed' as const }
  return { kind: 'success', tickets, payment: done }
}

// Safe to call many times (React StrictMode, refresh, retry): concurrent calls share one run.
export function completePayment(txRef: string): Promise<CompletionResult> {
  const existing = inFlight.get(txRef)
  if (existing) return existing
  const promise = run(txRef).finally(() => inFlight.delete(txRef))
  inFlight.set(txRef, promise)
  return promise
}

import type { ReactNode } from 'react'
import { formatKwacha } from '../format'

interface OrderSummaryProps {
  subtotal: number
  serviceFee: number
  total: number
  disabled: boolean
  paying: boolean
  onPay: () => void
}

export function OrderSummary({
  subtotal,
  serviceFee,
  total,
  disabled,
  paying,
  onPay,
}: OrderSummaryProps): ReactNode {
  return (
    <div className="card">
      <h2>Order summary</h2>
      <div className="row">
        <span>Ticket price</span>
        <span>{formatKwacha(subtotal)}</span>
      </div>
      <div className="row">
        <span>Service fee</span>
        <span>{formatKwacha(serviceFee)}</span>
      </div>
      <hr />
      <div className="row">
        <b>Total</b>
        <span className="p" style={{ fontSize: 20 }}>
          {formatKwacha(total)}
        </span>
      </div>
      <button type="button" className="btn w" onClick={onPay} disabled={disabled || paying}>
        {paying ? 'Redirecting to PayChangu…' : `Pay ${formatKwacha(total)} with PayChangu`}
      </button>
      <p className="m" style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: 8 }}>
        A ticket is issued only after payment is confirmed.
      </p>
    </div>
  )
}

FILE: src/features/checkout/components/QuantityStepper.tsx
========================================================================================
import type { ReactNode } from 'react'

interface QuantityStepperProps {
  value: number
  min: number
  max: number
  onChange: (value: number) => void
}

export function QuantityStepper({ value, min, max, onChange }: QuantityStepperProps): ReactNode {
  return (
    <div className="stepper" role="group" aria-label="Ticket quantity">
      <button
        type="button"
        className="stepper-btn"
        onClick={() => onChange(Math.max(min, value - 1))}
        disabled={value <= min}
        aria-label="Decrease quantity"
      >
        −
      </button>
      <span className="stepper-value" aria-live="polite">
        {value}
      </span>
      <button
        type="button"
        className="stepper-btn"
        onClick={() => onChange(Math.min(max, value + 1))}
        disabled={value >= max}
        aria-label="Increase quantity"
      >
        +
      </button>
    </div>
  )
}

FILE: src/features/checkout/components/TicketConfirmation.tsx
========================================================================================
import { useRef, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import { Link } from '@tanstack/react-router'
import { QrCode } from '../../../components/QrCode'
import type { TicketRecord } from '../../../lib/tickets'
import { formatKwacha } from '../format'
import { downloadTicketImage, ticketFromRecord } from '../../tickets/ticketImage'
import { formatDate } from '../../tickets/ticketFormat'

interface TicketConfirmationProps {
  tickets: TicketRecord[]
  onBuyAgain: () => void
}

export function TicketConfirmation({ tickets, onBuyAgain }: TicketConfirmationProps): ReactNode {
  return (
    <div>
      <span className="ok">✓ Purchase confirmed</span>
      <h1>Your ticket{tickets.length > 1 ? 's' : ''}</h1>
      <div className="split" style={{ marginTop: 14 }}>
        <div className="g">
          {tickets.map((ticket) => (
            <TicketPass key={ticket.id} ticket={ticket} />
          ))}
        </div>
        <div className="card">
          <h2>Actions</h2>
          <Link
            to="/tickets/$ticketId"
            params={{ ticketId: tickets[0].id }}
            className="btn w"
            style={{ marginTop: 0 }}
          >
            View in My Tickets
          </Link>
          <button type="button" className="btn o w" onClick={onBuyAgain}>
            Buy more tickets
          </button>
          <Link className="btn o w" to="/events">
            Browse more events
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function TicketPass({ ticket }: { ticket: TicketRecord }): ReactNode {
  const qrRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement>(null)

  return (
    <article className="card ticket-pass">
      <div className="ticket-pass-info">
        <span className="tag">{ticket.status === 'valid' ? 'Valid' : 'Used'}</span>
        <h2 style={{ marginTop: 10 }}>{ticket.eventName}</h2>
        <p className="m">{ticket.venue}, {ticket.city}</p>
        <div className="g g2" style={{ marginTop: 14 }}>
          <div><span className="m">Date</span><br /><b>{formatDate(ticket.date)}</b></div>
          <div><span className="m">Time</span><br /><b>{ticket.time}</b></div>
          <div><span className="m">Ticket</span><br /><b>{ticket.ticketType}</b></div>
          <div><span className="m">Entry</span><br /><b>{ticket.ticketType} Admission</b></div>
          <div><span className="m">Price</span><br /><b>{formatKwacha(ticket.price)}</b></div>
          <div><span className="m">Holder</span><br /><b>{ticket.holder}</b></div>
        </div>
        <p style={{ marginTop: 12 }}><b>{ticket.id}</b></p>
        <button
          type="button"
          className="btn"
          style={{ marginTop: 8 }}
          onClick={() => void downloadTicketImage(ticketFromRecord(ticket), qrRef.current, ticket.qrCodeDataUrl)}
        >
          Download ticket PNG
        </button>
      </div>
      <div className="ticket-pass-qr">
        <QrCode value={ticket.id} canvasRef={qrRef} dataUrl={ticket.qrCodeDataUrl} />
        <p className="m" style={{ marginTop: 8 }}>Scan at the entrance</p>
      </div>
    </article>
  )
}

import type { ReactNode } from 'react'
import { formatKwacha } from '../format'
import type { CheckoutEvent, TicketType } from '../types'
import { QuantityStepper } from './QuantityStepper'

interface TicketSummaryCardProps {
  event: CheckoutEvent
  ticketType: TicketType
  quantity: number
  onChangeQuantity: (quantity: number) => void
}

export function TicketSummaryCard({
  event,
  ticketType,
  quantity,
  onChangeQuantity,
}: TicketSummaryCardProps): ReactNode {
  const remaining = ticketType.capacity - ticketType.sold
  const soldOut = remaining <= 0

  return (
    <div className="card row">
      <div>
        <b>{event.name}</b>
        <div className="m">
          {event.date} · {ticketType.name} · {event.venue}, {event.city}
        </div>
        <div className="m">
          {formatKwacha(ticketType.price)} each · {soldOut ? 'Sold out' : `${remaining} left`}
        </div>
      </div>
      <QuantityStepper
        value={quantity}
        min={1}
        max={Math.max(remaining, 1)}
        onChange={onChangeQuantity}
      />
    </div>
  )
}

FILE: src/features/checkout/format.ts
========================================================================================
import { SERVICE_FEE } from './mockData'

export interface OrderTotals {
  subtotal: number
  serviceFee: number
  total: number
}

export function formatKwacha(amount: number): string {
  return `MK${Math.round(amount).toLocaleString('en-US')}`
}

export function calculateTotals(
  price: number,
  quantity: number,
  serviceFee: number = SERVICE_FEE,
): OrderTotals {
  const subtotal = price * quantity
  return { subtotal, serviceFee, total: subtotal + serviceFee }
}


========================================================================================
FILE: src/features/checkout/hooks/useCheckout.ts
========================================================================================
import { useMutation } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { fetchBackendEvent } from '../../../lib/backendEvents'
import { remainingSeats } from '../../../types/event'
import {
  initiatePayment,
  makeTxRef,
  removePendingPayment,
  savePendingPayment,
} from '../../../lib/paychangu'
import { calculateTotals } from '../format'
import type { CheckoutInput } from '../types'

export class CheckoutError extends Error {}

// Starts a real PayChangu payment. No ticket is issued here: tickets are issued on the
// return page (completePayment.ts) only after PayChangu confirms the money arrived.
async function startPayment(input: CheckoutInput): Promise<never> {
  const stored = await fetchBackendEvent(input.event.id)
  if (!stored || stored.status !== 'Published') {
    throw new CheckoutError('This event is not available for booking.')
  }
  const ticketType = stored.ticketTypes.find((candidate) => candidate.id === input.ticketType.id)
  if (!ticketType) throw new CheckoutError('That ticket type no longer exists.')

  if (input.quantity < 1) throw new CheckoutError('Select at least one ticket.')
  const remaining = remainingSeats(ticketType)
  if (remaining <= 0) throw new CheckoutError(`${ticketType.name} tickets are sold out for this event.`)
  if (input.quantity > remaining) {
    throw new CheckoutError(`Only ${remaining} ${ticketType.name} ticket${remaining === 1 ? '' : 's'} left.`)
  }
  if (!/^\S+@\S+\.\S+$/.test(input.email)) throw new CheckoutError('Your account needs a valid email address.')

  const txRef = makeTxRef()
  const amount = calculateTotals(ticketType.price, input.quantity).total
  const [firstName, ...rest] = (input.holder.trim() || 'Customer').split(/\s+/)

  // remember the order: PayChangu sends the customer back with only the reference
  savePendingPayment({
    txRef,
    eventId: stored.id,
    ticketTypeId: ticketType.id,
    quantity: input.quantity,
    holder: input.holder,
    userId: input.userId,
    amount,
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    status: 'pending',
  })

  try {
    const { checkout_url } = await initiatePayment({
      amount,
      email: input.email,
      first_name: firstName,
      last_name: rest.join(' ') || '-',
      tx_ref: txRef,
      callback_url: `${window.location.origin}/checkout/return`,
      return_url: `${window.location.origin}/checkout/return`,
      title: stored.title,
      description: `${input.quantity} x ${ticketType.name} ticket${input.quantity === 1 ? '' : 's'}`,
    })
    window.location.assign(checkout_url)
  } catch (error) {
    removePendingPayment(txRef)
    throw new CheckoutError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Could not start the payment.')
  }

  // keep the button on "Redirecting…" until the browser leaves the page
  return new Promise<never>(() => {})
}

export function useCheckout() {
  return useMutation({ mutationFn: startPayment })
}

FILE: src/features/checkout/pages/CheckoutPage.tsx
========================================================================================

import { useState } from 'react'
import type { ReactNode } from 'react'
import { useParams, useSearch, Link } from '@tanstack/react-router'
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { calculateTotals, formatKwacha } from '../format'
import { useCheckout } from '../hooks/useCheckout'
import { fetchBackendEvent } from '../../../lib/backendEvents'
import { useAuth } from '../../authentication'
import type { CheckoutEvent, TicketType } from '../types'
import { remainingSeats } from '../../../types/event'

const toCheckoutEvent = (ev: import('../../../types/event').Event): CheckoutEvent => ({
  id: ev.id,
  name: ev.title,
  date: ev.date,
  time: ev.time,
  venue: ev.venue,
  city: ev.city,
  organizer: ev.organizer,
})

const toCheckoutTicketTypes = (ev: import('../../../types/event').Event): TicketType[] =>
  ev.ticketTypes.map((t) => ({ ...t }))


export function CheckoutPage(): ReactNode {
  const { eventId } = useParams({ strict: false }) as { eventId?: string }
  const { type, qty } = useSearch({ strict: false }) as { type?: string; qty?: string }
  const parsedQty = parseInt(qty ?? '', 10)

  const [selectedTypeId, setSelectedTypeId] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [quantity, setQuantity] = useState(() =>
    Number.isNaN(parsedQty) || parsedQty < 1 ? 1 : parsedQty,
  )
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const holder = user?.name ?? ''
  const [clientError, setClientError] = useState<string | null>(null)

  const { data: eventData, isLoading, isError } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['checkout-event', eventId],
    queryFn: () => fetchBackendEvent(eventId ?? ''),
    enabled: !!eventId,
  })

  const ticketTypes: TicketType[] = eventData ? toCheckoutTicketTypes(eventData) : []
  const activeTypeId = selectedTypeId ?? type ?? ticketTypes[0]?.id
  const ticketType =
    ticketTypes.find((candidate) => candidate.id === activeTypeId) ?? ticketTypes[0]
  const checkoutEvent: CheckoutEvent = eventData
    ? toCheckoutEvent(eventData)
    : {
        id: eventId ?? 'unknown',
        name: 'Event',
        date: '',
        time: '',
        venue: '',
        city: '',
        organizer: '',
      }

  const checkout = useCheckout()

  const totals = ticketType
    ? calculateTotals(ticketType.price, quantity)
    : { subtotal: 0, serviceFee: 0, total: 0 }
  const remaining = ticketType ? remainingSeats(ticketType) : 0
  const soldOut = remaining <= 0
  const exceedsAvailable = quantity > remaining
  function selectTicketType(id: string) {
    setSelectedTypeId(id)
    setQuantity(1)
    setClientError(null)
  }

  function handlePay() {
    if (!ticketType || !user) return
    setClientError(null)
    // goes to PayChangu; tickets are issued on the return page once the payment is confirmed
    checkout.mutate({
      event: checkoutEvent,
      ticketType,
      quantity,
      holder,
      email: user.email,
      userId: user.id,
    })
  }

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 py-24 sm:px-6">
        <div className="h-80 animate-pulse rounded-3xl bg-slate-100" />
      </div>
    )
  }

  if (isError || !eventData || eventData.status !== 'Published') {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 py-24 text-center sm:px-6">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Event not found</h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-slate-600">We couldn’t find this event to check out.</p>
        <Link to="/events" className="mt-6 inline-block rounded-full bg-navy px-6 py-2 text-white">Browse events</Link>
      </div>
    )
  }

  const mutationError = checkout.error instanceof Error ? checkout.error.message : null
  const errorMessage = clientError ?? mutationError
  const blocked = soldOut || exceedsAvailable

  const MAX_PER_ORDER = 10
  const maxQty = Math.max(1, Math.min(MAX_PER_ORDER, remaining))
  const canPay = !blocked && !checkout.isPending && !!ticketType

  return (
    <div className="bg-slate-50">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-4 py-10 sm:px-6">
        <Link to="/events" className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-500 hover:text-navy">
          ← Back to events
        </Link>
        <h1 className="mt-3 text-3xl font-bold text-navy sm:text-4xl">Confirm your purchase</h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-slate-500">Check your order, then pay securely.</p>

        <div className="mt-8 grid gap-8 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_380px]">
          <div className="space-y-6">
            <section className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl bg-white shadow-sm ring-1 ring-slate-200">
              <div className="bg-navy px-6 py-5 text-white">
                <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-amber-300">Your event</p>
                <h2 className="mt-1 text-2xl font-bold text-white">{checkoutEvent.name}</h2>
                <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-200">
                  {checkoutEvent.date}
                  {checkoutEvent.time ? ` · ${checkoutEvent.time}` : ''}
                </p>
                <p className="text-sm text-slate-200">
                  {checkoutEvent.venue}
                  {checkoutEvent.city ? `, ${checkoutEvent.city}` : ''}
                </p>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-6 p-6">
                <div>
                  <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">Ticket type</h3>
                  <div className="mt-3 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
                    {ticketTypes.map((option) => {
                      const left = option.capacity - option.sold
                      const disabled = left <= 0
                      const selected = option.id === ticketType?.id
                      return (
                        <button
                          key={option.id}
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => selectTicketType(option.id)}
                          disabled={disabled}
                          aria-pressed={selected}
                          className={`rounded-xl border-2 p-4 text-left transition ${
                            selected
                              ? 'border-navy bg-navy/5'
                              : 'border-slate-200 hover:border-slate-300'
                          } ${disabled ? 'cursor-not-allowed opacity-50' : ''}`}
                        >
                          <span className="block font-semibold text-slate-900">{option.name}</span>
                          <span className="mt-1 block text-lg font-bold text-navy">{formatKwacha(option.price)}</span>
                          <span className="mt-1 block text-xs text-slate-500">
                            {disabled ? 'Sold out' : `${left} left`}
                          </span>
                        </button>
                      )
                    })}
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 border-t border-slate-100 pt-6">
                  <div>
                    <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">Quantity</h3>
                    <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">Up to {MAX_PER_ORDER} per order</p>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      aria-label="Decrease quantity"
                      onClick={() => setQuantity((q) => Math.max(1, q - 1))}
                      disabled={quantity <= 1}
                      className="h-10 w-10 rounded-full border border-slate-300 text-lg font-semibold text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100 disabled:opacity-40"
                    >
                      −
                    </button>
                    <span className="w-8 text-center text-lg font-bold" aria-live="polite">{quantity}</span>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      aria-label="Increase quantity"
                      onClick={() => setQuantity((q) => Math.min(maxQty, q + 1))}
                      disabled={quantity >= maxQty}
                      className="h-10 w-10 rounded-full border border-slate-300 text-lg font-semibold text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100 disabled:opacity-40"
                    >
                      +
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="border-t border-slate-100 pt-6">
                  <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">Ticket holder</h3>
                  <div className="mt-3 flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 px-4 py-3 ring-1 ring-slate-200">
                    <span className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-navy text-sm font-bold text-white">
                      {(holder || '?').charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
                    </span>
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <p className="truncate font-medium text-slate-900">{holder}</p>
                      <p className="truncate text-xs text-slate-500">{user?.email}</p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </section>

            {soldOut && ticketType && (
              <div role="alert" className="rounded-xl bg-red-50 p-4 text-sm text-red-700 ring-1 ring-red-200">
                <b>Sold out.</b> There are no {ticketType.name} tickets left.
              </div>
            )}
            {!soldOut && exceedsAvailable && ticketType && (
              <div role="alert" className="rounded-xl bg-red-50 p-4 text-sm text-red-700 ring-1 ring-red-200">
                Only {remaining} ticket{remaining === 1 ? '' : 's'} left. Reduce the quantity to continue.
              </div>
            )}
            {errorMessage && (
              <div role="alert" className="rounded-xl bg-red-50 p-4 text-sm text-red-700 ring-1 ring-red-200">
                {errorMessage}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          <aside className="lg:sticky lg:top-6 lg:self-start">
            <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white p-6 shadow-sm ring-1 ring-slate-200">
              <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-navy">Order summary</h2>
              <dl className="mt-4 space-y-3 text-sm">
                <div className="flex justify-between text-slate-600">
                  <dt>
                    {quantity} × {ticketType?.name ?? 'Ticket'}
                  </dt>
                  <dd className="font-medium text-slate-900">{formatKwacha(totals.subtotal)}</dd>
                </div>
                <div className="flex justify-between text-slate-600">
                  <dt>Service fee</dt>
                  <dd className="font-medium text-slate-900">{formatKwacha(totals.serviceFee)}</dd>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between border-t border-slate-200 pt-4">
                  <dt className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">Total</dt>
                  <dd className="text-2xl font-bold text-navy">{formatKwacha(totals.total)}</dd>
                </div>
              </dl>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handlePay}
                disabled={!canPay}
                className="mt-6 w-full rounded-xl bg-navy px-4 py-3.5 text-base font-semibold text-white shadow transition hover:opacity-90 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                {checkout.isPending ? 'Redirecting…' : `Pay ${formatKwacha(totals.total)}`}
              </button>
            </div>
          </aside>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


========================================================================================
FILE: src/features/checkout/pages/PaymentReturnPage.tsx
========================================================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import type { ReactNode } from 'react'
import { Link, useNavigate, useSearch } from '@tanstack/react-router'
import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { latestPendingPayment } from '../../../lib/paychangu'
import { completePayment, type CompletionResult } from '../completePayment'
import { TicketConfirmation } from '../components/TicketConfirmation'

const RETRY_EVERY_MS = 3000
const MAX_ATTEMPTS = 10

// PayChangu sends the customer here after paying (or giving up). We never trust the URL:
// we ask PayChangu (through our server) whether the payment really succeeded.
export function PaymentReturnPage(): ReactNode {
  const search = useSearch({ strict: false }) as { tx_ref?: unknown; status?: unknown }
  const txRef = search.tx_ref !== undefined ? String(search.tx_ref) : latestPendingPayment()?.txRef
  const returnedStatus = typeof search.status === 'string' ? search.status.toLowerCase() : ''

  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const queryClient = useQueryClient()
  const [result, setResult] = useState<CompletionResult | null>(null)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [attempt, setAttempt] = useState(0)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!txRef) return
    let active = true
    let timer: number | undefined

    completePayment(txRef)
      .then((outcome) => {
        if (!active) return
        setError(null)
        setResult(outcome)
        if (outcome.kind === 'success' || outcome.kind === 'partial') {
          for (const key of ['events', 'checkout-event', 'tickets', 'bookings']) {
            queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: [key] })
          }
        }
        if (outcome.kind === 'pending' && attempt < MAX_ATTEMPTS) {
          timer = window.setTimeout(() => setAttempt((n) => n + 1), RETRY_EVERY_MS)
        }
      })
      .catch((caught: unknown) => {
        if (!active) return
        setError(caught instanceof Error ? caught.message : 'Could not confirm the payment.')
      })

    return () => {
      active = false
      if (timer) window.clearTimeout(timer)
    }
  }, [txRef, attempt, queryClient])

  if (!txRef || result?.kind === 'unknown') {
    return (
      <Notice title="We couldn't find your order">
        <p className="m">
          This payment was not started from this browser, so we can't match it to an order. If money left your
          account, contact support with your PayChangu receipt.
        </p>
        <Link to="/events" className="btn">Browse events</Link>
      </Notice>
    )
  }

  if (result?.kind === 'success') {
    return <TicketConfirmation tickets={result.tickets} onBuyAgain={() => navigate({ to: '/events' })} />
  }

  if (result?.kind === 'partial') {
    return (
      <div>
        <div className="alert error" style={{ marginBottom: 14 }}>
          <p>{result.message}</p>
          <button
            type="button"
            className="btn o"
            style={{ marginTop: 8 }}
            onClick={() => {
              setResult(null)
              setAttempt((n) => n + 1)
            }}
          >
            Try missing tickets
          </button>
        </div>
        <TicketConfirmation tickets={result.tickets} onBuyAgain={() => navigate({ to: '/events' })} />
      </div>
    )
  }

  if (result?.kind === 'failed' || (returnedStatus === 'failed' && !result)) {
    const failed = result?.kind === 'failed' ? result : null
    return (
      <Notice title="Payment not completed">
        <div className="alert error">
          {failed?.message ?? 'The payment was cancelled or failed. No ticket was issued.'}
        </div>
        {failed?.eventId ? (
          <Link to="/checkout/$eventId" params={{ eventId: failed.eventId }} className="btn">Try again</Link>
        ) : (
          <Link to="/events" className="btn">Browse events</Link>
        )}
      </Notice>
    )
  }

  if (error) {
    return (
      <Notice title="We couldn't confirm your payment yet">
        <div className="alert error">{error}</div>
        <button type="button" className="btn" onClick={() => setAttempt((n) => n + 1)}>
          Check again
        </button>
      </Notice>
    )
  }

  const waitedTooLong = result?.kind === 'pending' && attempt >= MAX_ATTEMPTS
  return (
    <Notice title={waitedTooLong ? 'Still waiting for confirmation' : 'Confirming your payment…'}>
      <p className="m">
        {waitedTooLong
          ? 'PayChangu has not confirmed the payment yet. If you approved it on your phone, check again in a moment.'
          : 'Please wait while we confirm the payment with PayChangu. Do not close this page.'}
      </p>
      {waitedTooLong && (
        <button type="button" className="btn" onClick={() => setAttempt(0)}>
          Check again
        </button>
      )}
    </Notice>
  )
}

function Notice({ title, children }: { title: string; children: ReactNode }): ReactNode {
  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-xl px-4 py-24 text-center sm:px-6">
      <div className="card" style={{ display: 'grid', gap: 12, justifyItems: 'center' }}>
        <h1>{title}</h1>
        {children}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


========================================================================================
FILE: src/features/checkout/types.ts

import type { TicketRecord } from '../../lib/tickets'

export interface CheckoutEvent {
  id: string
  name: string
  date: string
  time: string
  venue: string
  city: string
  organizer: string
}

export interface TicketType {
  id: string
  name: string
  price: number
  capacity: number
  sold: number
}

export interface CheckoutInput {
  userId: string
  event: CheckoutEvent
  ticketType: TicketType
  quantity: number
  holder: string
  email: string // receipt email for PayChangu
  ownerEmail?: string
}

export interface CheckoutResult {
  reference: string
  tickets: TicketRecord[]
}

FILE: src/features/events/hooks/useEvents.ts
========================================================================================
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { listMyEvents } from '../../../lib/backendOrganizer'
import { fetchPublishedEvents } from '../../../lib/backendEvents'

// Published events from the backend.
export function useEvents() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['events'],
    queryFn: fetchPublishedEvents,
  })
}

// Events managed by the signed-in organizer.
export function useAllEvents() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['events', 'all'],
    queryFn: listMyEvents,
  })
}
