FILE: src/features/events/pages/EventDetailsPage.tsx
========================================================================================
import { useState } from 'react'
import { useParams, Link } from '@tanstack/react-router'
import { Calendar, Clock, MapPin, Users } from 'lucide-react'
import { useEvents } from '../hooks/useEvents'
import { remainingSeats, fromPrice, type Event, type TicketType } from '../../../types/event'
import Reveal from '../../../components/Reveal'

function formatMoney(amount: number): string {
  return amount === 0 ? 'Free' : `MK ${amount.toLocaleString()}`
}

export function EventDetailsPage() {
  const { eventId } = useParams({ from: '/events/$eventId' })
  const { data: events = [], isLoading, isError } = useEvents()
  const event = events.find((e: Event) => e.id === eventId)

  const [quantity, setQuantity] = useState(1)
  const [selectedTypeId, setSelectedTypeId] = useState<string | null>(null)

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 py-24 sm:px-6">
        <div className="h-96 animate-pulse rounded-3xl bg-slate-100" />
      </div>
    )
  }

  if (isError || !event) {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 py-24 text-center sm:px-6">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Event not found</h1>
        <Link to="/events" className="mt-6 inline-block rounded-full bg-navy px-6 py-2 text-white">
          Browse events
        </Link>
      </div>
    )
  }

  const types = event.ticketTypes
  const selectedType = types.find((t) => t.id === (selectedTypeId ?? types[0]?.id)) ?? types[0]
  const from = fromPrice(event)

  const remaining = selectedType ? remainingSeats(selectedType) : 0
  const qty = Math.max(1, quantity)
  const canBuy = selectedType && remaining > 0 && qty <= remaining
  const total = selectedType ? selectedType.price * qty : 0

  const checkoutLink = selectedType
    ? `/checkout/${event.id}?type=${selectedType.id}&qty=${qty}`
    : `/checkout/${event.id}`

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen pb-16 pt-20">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 sm:px-6">
        <Reveal>
          <div className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-3xl shadow-2xl">
            <img src={event.image} alt={event.title} className="h-80 w-full object-cover sm:h-[420px]" />
            <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-navy-dark/90 via-navy-dark/40 to-transparent" />
            <div className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 p-6 sm:p-10">
              {event.category && (
                <span className="inline-block rounded-full bg-white px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-navy">
                  {event.category}
                </span>
              )}
              <h1 className="mt-3 text-3xl font-extrabold text-white sm:text-5xl">{event.title}</h1>
              <p className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4 text-white/90 sm:text-lg">
                <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Calendar size={18} /> {new Date(event.date).toLocaleDateString('en-GB')}
                </span>
                <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Clock size={18} /> {event.time}
                </span>
                <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <MapPin size={18} /> {event.venue}, {event.city}
                </span>
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </Reveal>

        <div className="mt-10 grid gap-8 lg:grid-cols-3">
          <Reveal delay={100} className="lg:col-span-2">
            <div className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-100 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm sm:p-8">
              <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold">About this event</h2>
              <p className="mt-4 leading-relaxed text-slate-700">{event.description}</p>
              <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-t pt-6">
                {event.organizer && <div>
                  <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">Organized by</p>
                  <p className="font-semibold text-navy-dark">{event.organizer}</p>
                </div>}
                <div className="text-right">
                  <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">Tickets from</p>
                  <p className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-navy">{formatMoney(from)}</p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </Reveal>

          <Reveal delay={200}>
            <div className="sticky top-24 space-y-6 rounded-3xl border border-slate-100 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm sm:p-8">
              <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold">Get tickets</h2>

              <div className="space-y-3">
                {types.map((type: TicketType) => {
                  const rem = remainingSeats(type)
                  const soldOut = rem <= 0
                  return (
                    <label
                      key={type.id}
                      className={`flex cursor-pointer items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-2xl border p-4 transition ${
                        soldOut
                          ? 'cursor-not-allowed border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 opacity-60'
                          : selectedType?.id === type.id
                            ? 'border-navy bg-navy-50'
                            : 'border-slate-200 hover:border-navy'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                        <input
                          type="radio"
                          name="ticketType"
                          value={type.id}
                          checked={selectedType?.id === type.id}
                          onChange={() => setSelectedTypeId(type.id)}
                          disabled={soldOut}
                        />
                        <div>
                          <p className="font-semibold">{type.name}</p>
                          <p className="text-sm text-slate-600">{formatMoney(type.price)}</p>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-right">
                        {soldOut ? (
                          <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-red-600">Sold out</span>
                        ) : (
                          <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-slate-600">
                            <Users size={14} /> {rem} left
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </label>
                  )
                })}
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 p-4">
                <label className="text-sm font-medium">Quantity</label>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setQuantity((q) => Math.max(1, q - 1))}
                    className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full border bg-white hover:bg-slate-100"
                    disabled={qty <= 1}
                  >
                    −
                  </button>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    min={1}
                    max={remaining}
                    value={qty}
                    onChange={(e) => {
                      const val = parseInt(e.target.value, 10)
                      setQuantity(Number.isNaN(val) ? 1 : Math.min(Math.max(1, val), remaining))
                    }}
                    className="w-16 rounded-xl border bg-white px-2 py-1 text-center"
                    disabled={remaining <= 0}
                  />
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setQuantity((q) => Math.min(remaining, q + 1))}
                    className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full border bg-white hover:bg-slate-100"
                    disabled={remaining <= 0 || qty >= remaining}
                  >
                    +
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="border-t pt-4">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <span className="text-slate-600">Total</span>
                  <span className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-navy">{formatMoney(total)}</span>
                </div>
                <Link
                  to={checkoutLink}
                  className={`mt-4 block w-full rounded-xl py-3 text-center font-semibold text-white transition ${
                    canBuy ? 'bg-navy hover:bg-navy-dark' : 'cursor-not-allowed bg-slate-400'
                  }`}
                  aria-disabled={!canBuy}
                  onClick={(e) => {
                    if (!canBuy) e.preventDefault()
                  }}
                >
                  {selectedType && remaining <= 0 ? 'Sold out' : 'Buy ticket'}
                </Link>
                <p className="mt-2 text-center text-xs text-slate-500">
                  You’ll be asked to sign in if you’re not logged in.
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </Reveal>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


========================================================================================
FILE: src/features/events/pages/EventsPage.tsx
========================================================================================
import { useNavigate, useSearch } from '@tanstack/react-router'
import { filtersFromSearch, filterEvents, searchFromFilters, type Filters } from '../../landing/filters'
import { useEvents } from '../hooks/useEvents'
import EventCard from '../../landing/components/EventCard'
import SearchBar from '../../landing/components/SearchBar'
import Reveal from '../../../components/Reveal'

export function EventsPage() {
  const search = useSearch({ from: '/events' })
  const navigate = useNavigate({ from: '/events' })
  const filters = filtersFromSearch(search)
  const { data: events = [], isLoading, isError } = useEvents()
  const visibleEvents = filterEvents(events, filters)

  function setFilters(next: Filters, options?: { replace?: boolean }) {
    navigate({ search: searchFromFilters(next), replace: options?.replace })
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen">
      <section className="bg-navy-50 py-12 pt-24">
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 sm:px-6">
          <Reveal>
            <h1 className="text-3xl font-extrabold sm:text-4xl">Discover Events</h1>
            <p className="mt-2">
              {isLoading ? 'Loading events...' : `${visibleEvents.length} event(s) found`}
            </p>
          </Reveal>

          <div className="mt-8">
            <SearchBar filters={filters} setFilters={setFilters} />
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section id="events" className="py-12">
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 sm:px-6">
          {isLoading && (
            <div className="grid gap-6 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
              {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6].map((n) => (
                <div key={n} className="h-80 animate-pulse rounded-2xl bg-slate-100" />
              ))}
            </div>
          )}

          {isError && (
            <p className="rounded-2xl bg-red-50 p-6 text-center text-red-700">
              Could not load events. Please try again.
            </p>
          )}

          {!isLoading && visibleEvents.length === 0 && (
            <p className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 p-10 text-center">
              No events match your search. Try adjusting the filters.
            </p>
          )}

          {!isLoading && visibleEvents.length > 0 && (
            <div className="grid gap-6 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
              {visibleEvents.map((event, i) => (
                <Reveal key={event.id} delay={(i % 3) * 100}>
                  <EventCard event={event} />
                </Reveal>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  )
}

import { useState } from 'react'
import { Mail, MapPin, Phone, Check } from 'lucide-react'
import Reveal from '../../../components/Reveal'

const details = [
  { icon: MapPin, text: 'Lilongwe, Central Region, Malawi' },
  { icon: Phone, text: '+265 999 000 000' },
  { icon: Mail, text: 'support@ticketmaster.mw' },
]

const inputStyle = 'w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-navy'

// Contact section. The form only shows a thank you message (there is no backend yet)
export default function Contact() {
  const [sent, setSent] = useState(false)

  return (
    <section id="contact" className="py-20">
      <div className="mx-auto grid max-w-7xl gap-10 px-4 sm:px-6 lg:grid-cols-2">
        <Reveal>
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-widest text-navy">Contact us</p>
          <h2 className="mt-2 text-3xl font-extrabold sm:text-4xl">Let's talk about your event</h2>
          <p className="mt-4">Have a question about tickets, or want to host an event? Send us a message and we will reply within 24 hours.</p>
          <ul className="mt-8 space-y-4">
            {details.map((d) => (
              <li key={d.text} className="flex items-center gap-4">
                <span className="flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-navy-50 text-navy"><d.icon size={20} /></span>
                {d.text}
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </Reveal>

        <Reveal delay={150}>
          {sent ? (
            <div className="flex h-full flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-3xl bg-navy-50 p-10 text-center">
              <Check size={56} className="text-navy" />
              <h3 className="mt-4 text-2xl font-bold">Message sent!</h3>
              <p className="mt-2">Thank you, we will get back to you soon.</p>
              <button onClick={() => setSent(false)} className="mt-6 font-semibold text-navy">Send another message</button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-4 rounded-3xl border border-slate-100 bg-white p-6 shadow-xl sm:p-8">
              <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
                <input placeholder="Your name" className={inputStyle} />
                <input type="email" placeholder="Email address" className={inputStyle} />
              </div>
              <input placeholder="Subject" className={inputStyle} />
              <textarea rows={5} placeholder="Your message" className={inputStyle} />
              <button onClick={() => setSent(true)} className="w-full rounded-xl bg-navy py-3 font-semibold text-white hover:bg-navy-dark">Send message</button>
            </div>
          )}
        </Reveal>
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}

FILE: src/features/landing/components/EventCard.tsx
========================================================================================
import { Link } from '@tanstack/react-router'
import { Calendar, MapPin } from 'lucide-react'
import { fromPrice, type Event } from '../../../types/event'

interface EventCardProps {
  event: Event
}

export default function EventCard({ event }: EventCardProps) {
  const date = new Date(event.date).toLocaleDateString('en-GB', {
    day: 'numeric',
    month: 'short',
    year: 'numeric',
  })
  const from = fromPrice(event)
  const price = from === 0 ? 'Free' : `MK ${from.toLocaleString()}`

  return (
    <Link
      to="/events/$eventId"
      params={{ eventId: String(event.id) }}
      className="group block overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 bg-white shadow-sm transition duration-300 hover:-translate-y-2 hover:shadow-2xl"
    >
      <div className="relative h-52 overflow-hidden">
        <img
          src={event.image}
          alt={event.title}
          loading="lazy"
          className="h-full w-full object-cover transition duration-500 group-hover:scale-110"
        />
        {event.category && (
          <span className="absolute left-3 top-3 rounded-full bg-white px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-navy">
            {event.category}
          </span>
        )}
      </div>
      <div className="p-5">
        <h3 className="text-lg font-bold group-hover:text-navy">{event.title}</h3>
        <p className="mt-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
          <Calendar size={15} /> {date}
        </p>
        <p className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
          <MapPin size={15} /> {event.venue}, {event.city}
        </p>
        <div className="mt-4 flex items-center justify-between border-t pt-4">
          <span className="text-lg font-extrabold text-navy">{price}</span>
          <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-navy">View event →</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Link>
  )
}

import { useEvents } from '../../events'
import { filterEvents, type Filters } from '../filters'
import EventCard from './EventCard'
import Reveal from '../../../components/Reveal'

interface EventsSectionProps {
  filters: Filters
}

export default function EventsSection({ filters }: EventsSectionProps) {
  const { data: events = [], isLoading, isError } = useEvents()
  const visibleEvents = filterEvents(events, filters)

  return (
    <section id="events" className="py-20">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 sm:px-6">
        <Reveal className="text-center">
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-widest text-navy">Upcoming</p>
          <h2 className="mt-2 text-3xl font-extrabold sm:text-4xl">Featured Events</h2>
          <p className="mt-3">
            {isLoading ? 'Loading events...' : `${visibleEvents.length} event(s) found`}
          </p>
        </Reveal>

        {isLoading && (
          <div className="mt-10 grid gap-6 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
            {[1, 2, 3].map((n) => (
              <div key={n} className="h-80 animate-pulse rounded-2xl bg-slate-100" />
            ))}
          </div>
        )}

        {isError && (
          <p className="mt-10 text-center text-red-600">
            Could not load the events. Please try again.
          </p>
        )}

        {!isLoading && visibleEvents.length === 0 && (
          <p className="mt-10 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 p-10 text-center">
            No events match your search. Try changing the filters.
          </p>
        )}

        <div className="mt-10 grid gap-6 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
          {visibleEvents.map((event, i) => (
            <Reveal key={event.id} delay={(i % 3) * 100}>
              <EventCard event={event} />
            </Reveal>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}


========================================================================================
FILE: src/features/landing/components/HeroSlider.tsx
========================================================================================
import { useCallback, useState } from 'react'
import { ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, Pause, Play } from 'lucide-react'
import { heroSlides } from '../../../mocks/heroSlides'
import SearchBar from './SearchBar'
import type { Filters } from '../filters'
import { useAutoplay } from '../hooks/useAutoplay'

interface HeroSliderProps {
  filters: Filters
  setFilters: (f: Filters, options?: { replace?: boolean }) => void
  onShowAll?: (filters: Filters) => void
}

export default function HeroSlider({ filters, setFilters, onShowAll }: HeroSliderProps) {
  const [current, setCurrent] = useState(0)
  const total = heroSlides.length

  const goTo = useCallback(
    (index: number) => setCurrent((index + total) % total),
    [total],
  )

  // Auto-advance stops on hover, keyboard focus, reduced motion, or when the
  // visitor presses pause (WCAG 2.2.2).
  const next = useCallback(() => goTo(current + 1), [goTo, current])
  const { paused, reducedMotion, togglePause, handlers } = useAutoplay(6000, next)

  const arrowStyle =
    'absolute top-1/2 z-10 hidden h-12 w-12 -translate-y-1/2 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-white/20 text-white backdrop-blur-sm hover:bg-white/40 sm:flex'

  return (
    <section className="relative min-h-screen bg-navy-dark" {...handlers}>
      <div className="absolute inset-0 overflow-hidden">
        {heroSlides.map((slide, i) => (
          <img
            key={slide.image}
            src={slide.image}
            alt={slide.title}
            aria-hidden={i !== current}
            className={`absolute inset-0 h-full w-full object-cover transition-all duration-1500 motion-reduce:transition-none ${
              i === current ? 'scale-105 opacity-100' : 'scale-100 opacity-0'
            }`}
          />
        ))}
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-linear-to-b from-navy-dark/70 via-navy-dark/50 to-navy-dark/80" />
      </div>

      <div className="relative z-20 mx-auto flex min-h-screen max-w-5xl flex-col items-center justify-center px-4 pb-20 pt-28 text-center">
        <div key={current} className="fade-up">
          <h1 className="text-4xl font-extrabold text-white sm:text-5xl lg:text-7xl">
            {heroSlides[current].title}
          </h1>
          <p className="mx-auto mt-4 max-w-2xl text-base text-white/85 sm:text-xl">
            {heroSlides[current].text}
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="mt-10 w-full">
          <SearchBar filters={filters} setFilters={setFilters} onShowAll={onShowAll} />
        </div>
      </div>

      <button aria-label="Previous slide" onClick={() => goTo(current - 1)} className={`${arrowStyle} left-4`}>
        <ChevronLeft />
      </button>
      <button aria-label="Next slide" onClick={() => goTo(current + 1)} className={`${arrowStyle} right-4`}>
        <ChevronRight />
      </button>

      <div className="absolute bottom-6 left-0 right-0 z-10 flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
        {!reducedMotion && (
          <button
            aria-label={paused ? 'Resume hero slideshow' : 'Pause hero slideshow'}
            onClick={togglePause}
            className="mr-2 flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-white/20 text-white backdrop-blur-sm hover:bg-white/40"
          >
            {paused ? <Play size={14} /> : <Pause size={14} />}
          </button>
        )}
        {heroSlides.map((_, i) => (
          <button
            key={i}
            aria-label={`Slide ${i + 1}`}
            onClick={() => setCurrent(i)}
            className={`h-2 rounded-full transition-all ${i === current ? 'w-8 bg-white' : 'w-2 bg-white/50'}`}
          />
        ))}
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}

FILE: src/features/landing/components/SearchBar.test.tsx
========================================================================================
// @vitest-environment jsdom
import { act, useState } from 'react'
import { createRoot, type Root } from 'react-dom/client'
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import SearchBar from './SearchBar'
import { defaultFilters, type Filters } from '../filters'

;(globalThis as { IS_REACT_ACT_ENVIRONMENT?: boolean }).IS_REACT_ACT_ENVIRONMENT = true

const mocks = vi.hoisted(() => ({
  events: [
    { id: 1, title: 'Lake of Stars Festival', category: 'Music', city: 'Salima', image: 'a.jpg' },
    { id: 2, title: 'Lake Malawi Marathon', category: 'Sports', city: 'Mangochi', image: 'b.jpg' },
    { id: 3, title: 'Zomba Tech Meetup', category: 'Tech', city: 'Zomba', image: 'c.jpg' },
  ],
}))

vi.mock('@tanstack/react-router', async () => {
  const { createElement } = await import('react')
  return {
    Link: ({ children, to, params, ...rest }: Record<string, unknown>) =>
      createElement('a', { href: to as string, ...rest }, children as never),
  }
})

vi.mock('../../events', () => ({ useEvents: () => ({ data: mocks.events }) }))

vi.mock('../filters', () => ({
  categories: ['All', 'Music', 'Sports'],
  cities: ['All'],
  priceOptions: [{ value: 'all', label: 'Any price' }],
  sortOptions: [{ value: 'date', label: 'Date' }],
  defaultFilters: { search: '', category: 'All', city: 'All', price: 'all', sort: 'date' },
  filterEvents: (events: { title: string }[], f: { search: string }) =>
    events.filter((e) => e.title.toLowerCase().includes(f.search.trim().toLowerCase())),
}))

function Harness() {
  const [filters, setFilters] = useState<Filters>(defaultFilters)
  return <SearchBar filters={filters} setFilters={setFilters} />
}

describe('SearchBar combobox', () => {
  let container: HTMLDivElement
  let root: Root

  const input = () => container.querySelector('input') as HTMLInputElement
  const options = () => [...container.querySelectorAll('[role=option]')] as HTMLElement[]

  function typeText(value: string) {
    const setter = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(HTMLInputElement.prototype, 'value')?.set
    act(() => {
      setter?.call(input(), value)
      input().dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }))
    })
  }

  function press(target: HTMLElement, key: string, init: KeyboardEventInit = {}) {
    act(() => {
      target.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keydown', { key, bubbles: true, cancelable: true, ...init }))
    })
  }

  beforeEach(() => {
    container = document.createElement('div')
    document.body.appendChild(container)
    root = createRoot(container)
    act(() => root.render(<Harness />))
  })

  afterEach(() => {
    act(() => root.unmount())
    container.remove()
  })

  it('gives the input a name and combobox state', () => {
    expect(input().getAttribute('role')).toBe('combobox')
    expect(input().getAttribute('aria-label')).toBe('Search events')
    expect(input().getAttribute('aria-expanded')).toBe('false')

    typeText('lake')
    expect(input().getAttribute('aria-expanded')).toBe('true')
    expect(container.querySelector('[role=listbox]')?.getAttribute('aria-label')).toBe('Search results')
    expect(input().getAttribute('aria-controls')).toBe(container.querySelector('[role=listbox]')?.id)
    // 2 matching events + the "See all" option
    expect(options()).toHaveLength(3)
  })

  it('announces the result count', () => {
    typeText('lake')
    expect(container.querySelector('[role=status]')?.textContent).toBe('2 events found')
    typeText('zomba')
    expect(container.querySelector('[role=status]')?.textContent).toBe('1 event found')
    typeText('nothing')
    expect(container.querySelector('[role=status]')?.textContent).toBe('No events found for nothing')
  })

  it('moves focus with the arrow keys', () => {
    typeText('lake')
    act(() => input().focus())

    press(input(), 'ArrowDown')
    expect(document.activeElement).toBe(options()[0])
    expect(options()[0].getAttribute('aria-selected')).toBe('true')
    expect(options()[1].getAttribute('aria-selected')).toBe('false')

    press(options()[0], 'ArrowDown')
    expect(document.activeElement).toBe(options()[1])
    expect(options()[1].getAttribute('aria-selected')).toBe('true')

    press(options()[1], 'ArrowUp')
    expect(document.activeElement).toBe(options()[0])

    // up from the first option returns to the input
    press(options()[0], 'ArrowUp')
    expect(document.activeElement).toBe(input())
  })

  it('closes with Escape and returns focus to the input', () => {
    typeText('lake')
    act(() => input().focus())
    press(input(), 'ArrowDown')
    expect(document.activeElement).toBe(options()[0])

    press(options()[0], 'Escape')
    expect(container.querySelector('[role=listbox]')).toBeNull()
    expect(input().getAttribute('aria-expanded')).toBe('false')
    expect(document.activeElement).toBe(input())
  })

  it('closes the list with Tab', () => {
    typeText('lake')
    act(() => input().focus())
    press(input(), 'Tab')
    expect(container.querySelector('[role=listbox]')).toBeNull()
  })

  it('shows which category chip is selected and the filters state', () => {
    const trigger = container.querySelector('[aria-label=Filters]') as HTMLButtonElement
    expect(trigger.getAttribute('aria-expanded')).toBe('false')

    act(() => trigger.click())
    expect(trigger.getAttribute('aria-expanded')).toBe('true')
    expect(container.querySelector('[role=dialog]')?.getAttribute('aria-label')).toBe('Filters')

    const chip = (name: string) =>
      [...container.querySelectorAll('[aria-pressed]')].find((b) => b.textContent === name) as HTMLElement
    expect(chip('All').getAttribute('aria-pressed')).toBe('true')
    expect(chip('Music').getAttribute('aria-pressed')).toBe('false')

    act(() => chip('Music').click())
    expect(chip('Music').getAttribute('aria-pressed')).toBe('true')
    expect(chip('All').getAttribute('aria-pressed')).toBe('false')
  })
})


========================================================================================
FILE: src/features/landing/components/SearchBar.tsx
========================================================================================
import { useEffect, useId, useRef, useState, type ChangeEvent, type KeyboardEvent } from 'react'
import { Link } from '@tanstack/react-router'
import { Search, SlidersHorizontal } from 'lucide-react'
import { useEvents } from '../../events'
import {
  categories,
  cities,
  priceOptions,
  sortOptions,
  defaultFilters,
  filterEvents,
  type Filters,
} from '../filters'

const selectStyle =
  'mt-1 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm font-normal text-slate-700 outline-none focus:border-navy'

interface HighlightProps {
  text: string
  word: string
}

function Highlight({ text, word }: HighlightProps) {
  const start = text.toLowerCase().indexOf(word.toLowerCase())
  if (!word || start === -1) return <>{text}</>
  const end = start + word.length
  return (
    <>
      {text.slice(0, start)}
      <b className="text-navy">{text.slice(start, end)}</b>
      {text.slice(end)}
    </>
  )
}

interface SearchBarProps {
  filters: Filters
  setFilters: (f: Filters, options?: { replace?: boolean }) => void
  onShowAll?: (filters: Filters) => void
}

type OpenBox = 'none' | 'filters' | 'results'

const MAX_RESULTS = 5

// Search box with a results list. It follows the ARIA "combobox" pattern:
//   - the input announces itself as a combobox and says if the list is open
//   - the results are a listbox of options; Arrow keys move focus through them
//   - Enter opens the focused option, Escape closes and returns to the input,
//     Tab closes the list
//   - a hidden live region announces how many events were found
export default function SearchBar({ filters, setFilters, onShowAll }: SearchBarProps) {
  const [openBox, setOpenBox] = useState<OpenBox>('none')
  const [activeIndex, setActiveIndex] = useState(-1)
  const wrapperRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null)
  const { data: events = [] } = useEvents()

  // unique ids so two search bars on one page never clash
  const uid = useId()
  const resultsId = `${uid}-results`
  const filtersId = `${uid}-filters`
  const optionPrefix = `${uid}-option-`

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleClick = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      const target = e.target as Node
      if (wrapperRef.current && !wrapperRef.current.contains(target)) {
        setOpenBox('none')
        setActiveIndex(-1)
      }
    }
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClick)
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClick)
  }, [])

  const update = (name: keyof Filters, value: string, options?: { replace?: boolean }) =>
    setFilters({ ...filters, [name]: value }, options)

  const handleTyping = (e: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    // Typeahead replaces the current entry; committed filters push a new one.
    update('search', e.target.value, { replace: true })
    setOpenBox(e.target.value.trim() ? 'results' : 'none')
    setActiveIndex(-1)
  }

  const activeCount = (['category', 'city', 'price'] as const).filter(
    (name) => filters[name] !== defaultFilters[name],
  ).length

  const matches = filterEvents(events, filters)
  const shown = matches.slice(0, MAX_RESULTS)
  // every shown event is an option, plus one last "See all" option
  const optionCount = matches.length > 0 ? shown.length + 1 : 0
  const word = filters.search.trim()

  const closeBox = (restoreFocus = false) => {
    setOpenBox('none')
    setActiveIndex(-1)
    if (restoreFocus) inputRef.current?.focus({ preventScroll: true })
  }

  const goToEvents = (restoreFocus = false) => {
    closeBox(restoreFocus)
    if (onShowAll) {
      onShowAll(filters)
      return
    }
    document.getElementById('events')?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' })
  }

  const focusOption = (index: number) => document.getElementById(`${optionPrefix}${index}`)?.focus()

  const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    const target = e.target as HTMLElement
    const onInput = target === inputRef.current
    const optionIndex = target.id.startsWith(optionPrefix)
      ? Number(target.id.slice(optionPrefix.length))
      : -1
    const onOption = optionIndex !== -1

    // Escape closes whichever panel is open and goes back to the input
    if (e.key === 'Escape' && openBox !== 'none') {
      e.preventDefault()
      closeBox(true)
      return
    }

    if (openBox === 'results' && optionCount > 0) {
      if (e.key === 'ArrowDown') {
        e.preventDefault()
        focusOption(onOption ? Math.min(optionIndex + 1, optionCount - 1) : 0)
        return
      }
      if (e.key === 'ArrowUp') {
        e.preventDefault()
        if (onInput) focusOption(optionCount - 1)
        else if (optionIndex <= 0) inputRef.current?.focus()
        else focusOption(optionIndex - 1)
        return
      }
      if (onOption && e.key === 'Home') {
        e.preventDefault()
        focusOption(0)
        return
      }
      if (onOption && e.key === 'End') {
        e.preventDefault()
        focusOption(optionCount - 1)
        return
      }
      // typing while an option has focus: go back to the input so typing continues
      if (onOption && (e.key === 'Backspace' || (e.key.length === 1 && !e.ctrlKey && !e.metaKey && !e.altKey))) {
        inputRef.current?.focus()
        return
      }
    }

    // Tab closes the results list
    if (e.key === 'Tab' && openBox === 'results') {
      if (onOption) {
        if (e.shiftKey) e.preventDefault()
        // focus goes back to the input; Tab then moves on from there
        inputRef.current?.focus()
      }
      closeBox()
      return
    }

    // ArrowDown in the input reopens the list for the current search text
    if (e.key === 'ArrowDown' && onInput && openBox === 'none' && word) {
      e.preventDefault()
      setOpenBox('results')
    }
  }

  // read out by screen readers when the number of results changes
  const resultsCount = `${matches.length} event${matches.length === 1 ? '' : 's'} found`
  const statusText =
    openBox === 'results' ? (matches.length === 0 ? `No events found for ${word}` : resultsCount) : ''

  return (
    <div ref={wrapperRef} onKeyDown={handleKeyDown} className="relative mx-auto w-full max-w-3xl">
      <form
        onSubmit={(e) => {
          e.preventDefault()
          goToEvents()
        }}
        className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-white p-2 pl-5 shadow-2xl"
      >
        <Search size={20} className="shrink-0 text-slate-400" />
        <input
          ref={inputRef}
          role="combobox"
          aria-label="Search events"
          aria-expanded={openBox === 'results'}
          aria-controls={resultsId}
          aria-autocomplete="list"
          autoComplete="off"
          value={filters.search}
          onChange={handleTyping}
          onFocus={() => setActiveIndex(-1)}
          placeholder="Search concerts, sports, workshops..."
          className="min-w-0 flex-1 bg-transparent py-2 text-sm text-slate-700 outline-none sm:text-base"
        />

        <button
          type="button"
          aria-label="Filters"
          aria-expanded={openBox === 'filters'}
          aria-controls={filtersId}
          aria-haspopup="dialog"
          onClick={() => setOpenBox(openBox === 'filters' ? 'none' : 'filters')}
          className={`relative flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full ${
            openBox === 'filters' ? 'bg-navy text-white' : 'bg-navy-50 text-navy hover:bg-navy-100'
          }`}
        >
          <SlidersHorizontal size={20} />
          {activeCount > 0 && (
            <span className="absolute -right-1 -top-1 flex h-5 w-5 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-navy-dark text-[11px] font-bold text-white">
              {activeCount}
            </span>
          )}
        </button>

        <button
          type="submit"
          className="hidden h-11 rounded-full bg-navy px-6 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-navy-dark sm:block"
        >
          Search
        </button>
      </form>

      <div role="status" aria-live="polite" className="sr-only">
        {statusText}
      </div>

      {openBox === 'results' && (
        <div
          id={resultsId}
          role={matches.length > 0 ? 'listbox' : undefined}
          aria-label={matches.length > 0 ? 'Search results' : undefined}
          className="absolute left-0 right-0 top-full z-30 mt-3 max-h-[70vh] overflow-y-auto rounded-2xl bg-white p-2 text-left shadow-2xl"
        >
          {matches.length === 0 && <p className="p-4 text-sm">No events found for "{word}"</p>}

          {shown.map((event, i) => (
            <Link
              key={event.id}
              id={`${optionPrefix}${i}`}
              role="option"
              aria-selected={activeIndex === i}
              tabIndex={-1}
              onFocus={() => setActiveIndex(i)}
              to="/events/$eventId"
              params={{ eventId: String(event.id) }}
              onClick={() => closeBox()}
              className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl p-2 hover:bg-navy-50 focus:bg-navy-50 focus:outline-2 focus:outline-navy"
            >
              <img src={event.image} alt="" className="h-12 w-12 rounded-lg object-cover" />
              <span>
                <span className="block font-semibold text-slate-700">
                  <Highlight text={event.title} word={word} />
                </span>
                <span className="text-xs">
                  {event.category} · {event.city}
                </span>
              </span>
            </Link>
          ))}

          {matches.length > 0 && (
            <button
              type="button"
              id={`${optionPrefix}${shown.length}`}
              role="option"
              aria-selected={activeIndex === shown.length}
              tabIndex={-1}
              onFocus={() => setActiveIndex(shown.length)}
              onClick={(e) => goToEvents(e.detail === 0)}
              className="w-full rounded-xl p-3 text-sm font-semibold text-navy hover:bg-navy-50 focus:bg-navy-50 focus:outline-2 focus:outline-navy"
            >
              See all {matches.length} result(s)
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {openBox === 'filters' && (
        <div
          id={filtersId}
          role="dialog"
          aria-label="Filters"
          className="absolute left-0 right-0 top-full z-30 mt-3 max-h-[70vh] overflow-y-auto rounded-2xl bg-white p-5 text-left shadow-2xl"
        >
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-navy-dark">Category</p>
          <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            {categories.map((c) => (
              <button
                key={c}
                type="button"
                aria-pressed={filters.category === c}
                onClick={() => update('category', c)}
                className={`rounded-full px-4 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium ${
                  filters.category === c ? 'bg-navy text-white' : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-200'
                }`}
              >
                {c}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>

          <div className="mt-4 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3">
            <label className="text-sm font-semibold text-navy-dark">
              City
              <select value={filters.city} onChange={(e) => update('city', e.target.value)} className={selectStyle}>
                {cities.map((c) => (
                  <option key={c}>{c}</option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </label>
            <label className="text-sm font-semibold text-navy-dark">
              Price
              <select value={filters.price} onChange={(e) => update('price', e.target.value)} className={selectStyle}>
                {priceOptions.map((p) => (
                  <option key={p.value} value={p.value}>
                    {p.label}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </label>
            <label className="text-sm font-semibold text-navy-dark">
              Sort by
              <select value={filters.sort} onChange={(e) => update('sort', e.target.value)} className={selectStyle}>
                {sortOptions.map((s) => (
                  <option key={s.value} value={s.value}>
                    {s.label}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </label>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-5 flex items-center justify-between">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setFilters(defaultFilters)}
              className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-500 hover:text-navy"
            >
              Reset all
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={(e) => goToEvents(e.detail === 0)}
              className="rounded-full bg-navy px-6 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-navy-dark"
            >
              Show {matches.length} event(s)
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}


========================================================================================
FILE: src/features/landing/components/Testimonials.tsx
========================================================================================
import { useCallback, useState } from 'react'
import { ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, Pause, Play, Star, User } from 'lucide-react'
import { useTestimonials } from '../hooks/useTestimonials'
import Reveal from '../../../components/Reveal'
import { useAutoplay } from '../hooks/useAutoplay'

// "What people say" slider. Slides move by themselves every 5 seconds.
export default function Testimonials() {
  const { data: reviews = [] } = useTestimonials()
  const [current, setCurrent] = useState(0)
  const total = reviews.length || 1

  const goTo = useCallback(
    (index: number) => setCurrent((index + total) % total),
    [total],
  )

  // Auto-advance stops on hover, keyboard focus, reduced motion, or when the
  // visitor presses pause (WCAG 2.2.2).
  const next = useCallback(() => goTo(current + 1), [goTo, current])
  const { running, paused, reducedMotion, togglePause, handlers } = useAutoplay(5000, next)

  const arrowStyle = 'absolute top-1/2 z-10 flex h-11 w-11 -translate-y-1/2 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-white text-navy shadow-lg hover:bg-navy hover:text-white'

  return (
    <section id="reviews" className="bg-navy-50 py-20">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl px-4 sm:px-6" {...handlers}>
        <Reveal className="text-center">
          <h2 className="text-3xl font-extrabold sm:text-4xl">What People Say</h2>
          <p className="mt-3">Hear from people who use Ticket Master</p>
        </Reveal>

        <Reveal className="relative mt-12">
          <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-3xl shadow-xl">
            {/* all slides in a row, we move the row left/right */}
            <div className="flex transition-transform duration-700 ease-in-out motion-reduce:transition-none" aria-live={running ? 'off' : 'polite'} style={{ transform: `translateX(-${current * 100}%)` }}>
              {reviews.map((r, i) => (
                <div key={r.name} role="group" aria-roledescription="slide" aria-label={`Review ${i + 1} of ${reviews.length}`} aria-hidden={i !== current} className="w-full shrink-0 bg-white p-8 sm:p-12">
                  <div className="flex gap-1 text-amber-400">
                    {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((s) => <Star key={s} size={20} fill="currentColor" />)}
                  </div>
                  <p className="mt-5 min-h-[110px] text-lg leading-relaxed">"{r.text}"</p>
                  <div className="mt-6 flex items-center gap-4">
                    <div className="flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-navy text-white"><User /></div>
                    <div>
                      <p className="font-bold text-navy-dark">{r.name}</p>
                      <p className="text-sm">{r.place}</p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
          <button aria-label="Previous review" onClick={() => goTo(current - 1)} className={`${arrowStyle} -left-3 sm:-left-5`}><ChevronLeft size={20} /></button>
          <button aria-label="Next review" onClick={() => goTo(current + 1)} className={`${arrowStyle} -right-3 sm:-right-5`}><ChevronRight size={20} /></button>
        </Reveal>

        <div className="mt-6 flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
          {!reducedMotion && (
            <button
              aria-label={paused ? 'Resume reviews slideshow' : 'Pause reviews slideshow'}
              onClick={togglePause}
              className="mr-2 flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-white text-navy shadow hover:bg-navy hover:text-white"
            >
              {paused ? <Play size={14} /> : <Pause size={14} />}
            </button>
          )}
          {reviews.map((_, i) => (
            <button key={i} aria-label={`Review ${i + 1}`} onClick={() => setCurrent(i)} className={`h-2 rounded-full transition-all ${i === current ? 'w-8 bg-navy' : 'w-2 bg-slate-300'}`} />
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}

FILE: src/features/landing/filters.test.ts
========================================================================================
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import {
  defaultFilters,
  filtersFromSearch,
  searchFromFilters,
  validateEventSearch,
} from './filters'

describe('event search params', () => {
  it('keeps valid non-default filters', () => {
    expect(
      validateEventSearch({
        search: 'jazz',
        category: 'Concerts',
        city: 'Lilongwe',
        price: 'under20',
        sort: 'low',
      }),
    ).toEqual({
      search: 'jazz',
      category: 'Concerts',
      city: 'Lilongwe',
      price: 'under20',
      sort: 'low',
    })
  })

  it('omits defaults so the URL stays clean', () => {
    expect(
      validateEventSearch({
        search: '',
        category: 'All',
        city: 'All cities',
        price: 'any',
        sort: 'date',
      }),
    ).toEqual({})
  })

  it('falls back to defaults for invalid values', () => {
    expect(validateEventSearch({ category: 'Wat', city: 'Atlantis', price: 'cheap', sort: 'random' })).toEqual(
      {},
    )

    const filters = filtersFromSearch(validateEventSearch({ category: 'Wat' }))
    expect(filters).toEqual(defaultFilters)
  })

  it('round-trips a full filter state through the URL', () => {
    const filters = {
      search: 'lake',
      category: 'Festivals',
      city: 'Mangochi',
      price: 'under20',
      sort: 'high',
    }
    const url = searchFromFilters(filters)
    expect(filtersFromSearch(validateEventSearch(url))).toEqual(filters)
  })

  it('turns a reset into an empty search object', () => {
    expect(searchFromFilters(defaultFilters)).toEqual({})
  })
})


========================================================================================
FILE: src/features/landing/filters.ts
========================================================================================
import { fromPrice, type Event } from '../../types/event'

export const categories = ['All', 'Concerts', 'Sports', 'Festivals', 'Workshops', 'Community']
export const cities = ['All cities', 'Lilongwe', 'Blantyre', 'Mzuzu', 'Zomba', 'Mangochi', 'Salima']

export const priceOptions = [
  { value: 'any', label: 'Any price' },
  { value: 'free', label: 'Free' },
  { value: 'under10', label: 'Under MK 10,000' },
  { value: 'under20', label: 'Under MK 20,000' },
  { value: 'over20', label: 'MK 20,000 and above' },
]

export const sortOptions = [
  { value: 'date', label: 'Soonest first' },
  { value: 'low', label: 'Price: low to high' },
  { value: 'high', label: 'Price: high to low' },
]

export interface Filters {
  search: string
  category: string
  city: string
  price: string
  sort: string
}

export const defaultFilters: Filters = {
  search: '',
  category: 'All',
  city: 'All cities',
  price: 'any',
  sort: 'date',
}

// The URL only carries non-default filters, so links stay short and readable.
export type EventSearch = Partial<Filters>

const priceValues = priceOptions.map((option) => option.value)
const sortValues = sortOptions.map((option) => option.value)

function picked(value: unknown, allowed: readonly string[]): string | undefined {
  return typeof value === 'string' && allowed.includes(value) ? value : undefined
}

// validateSearch for both `/` and `/events`. Unknown or invalid values are
// dropped, which makes them fall back to the defaults instead of breaking.
export function validateEventSearch(raw: Record<string, unknown>): EventSearch {
  const search: EventSearch = {}

  if (typeof raw.search === 'string' && raw.search !== '') {
    search.search = raw.search
  }

  const category = picked(raw.category, categories)
  if (category && category !== defaultFilters.category) search.category = category

  const city = picked(raw.city, cities)
  if (city && city !== defaultFilters.city) search.city = city

  const price = picked(raw.price, priceValues)
  if (price && price !== defaultFilters.price) search.price = price

  const sort = picked(raw.sort, sortValues)
  if (sort && sort !== defaultFilters.sort) search.sort = sort

  return search
}

// Merge partial URL state with the defaults for filtering/sorting.
export function filtersFromSearch(search: EventSearch): Filters {
  return {
    search: search.search ?? defaultFilters.search,
    category: search.category ?? defaultFilters.category,
    city: search.city ?? defaultFilters.city,
    price: search.price ?? defaultFilters.price,
    sort: search.sort ?? defaultFilters.sort,
  }
}

// Turn the full filter state back into a clean search object.
export function searchFromFilters(filters: Filters): EventSearch {
  const search: EventSearch = {}
  if (filters.search !== '') search.search = filters.search
  if (filters.category !== defaultFilters.category) search.category = filters.category
  if (filters.city !== defaultFilters.city) search.city = filters.city
  if (filters.price !== defaultFilters.price) search.price = filters.price
  if (filters.sort !== defaultFilters.sort) search.sort = filters.sort
  return search
}

function matchesPrice(price: number, option: string): boolean {
  if (option === 'free') return price === 0
  if (option === 'under10') return price < 10000
  if (option === 'under20') return price < 20000
  if (option === 'over20') return price >= 20000
  return true
}

export function filterEvents(events: Event[], filters: Filters): Event[] {
  const search = filters.search.trim().toLowerCase()

  return events
    .filter((e) => filters.category === 'All' || e.category === filters.category)
    .filter((e) => filters.city === 'All cities' || e.city === filters.city)
    .filter((e) => matchesPrice(fromPrice(e), filters.price))
    .filter((e) =>
      `${e.title} ${e.city} ${e.venue} ${e.category}`.toLowerCase().includes(search),
    )
    .sort((a, b) => {
      if (filters.sort === 'low') return fromPrice(a) - fromPrice(b)
      if (filters.sort === 'high') return fromPrice(b) - fromPrice(a)
      return new Date(a.date).getTime() - new Date(b.date).getTime()
    })
}

FILE: src/features/landing/hooks/useAutoplay.test.tsx
========================================================================================
// @vitest-environment jsdom
import { act, useCallback, useState } from 'react'
import { createRoot, type Root } from 'react-dom/client'
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { useAutoplay } from './useAutoplay'

;(globalThis as { IS_REACT_ACT_ENVIRONMENT?: boolean }).IS_REACT_ACT_ENVIRONMENT = true

let advanced = 0
let latest: ReturnType<typeof useAutoplay> | undefined

// tiny stand-in for a carousel: every advance changes `n`, like a new slide
function Probe() {
  const [n, setN] = useState(0)
  const advance = useCallback(() => {
    advanced += 1
    setN(n + 1)
  }, [n])
  latest = useAutoplay(1000, advance)
  return null
}

function mockMotionPreference(reduce: boolean) {
  window.matchMedia = (query: string) =>
    ({
      matches: reduce,
      media: query,
      addEventListener: () => {},
      removeEventListener: () => {},
    }) as unknown as MediaQueryList
}

describe('useAutoplay', () => {
  let container: HTMLDivElement
  let root: Root

  function mount() {
    container = document.createElement('div')
    root = createRoot(container)
    act(() => root.render(<Probe />))
  }

  beforeEach(() => {
    vi.useFakeTimers()
    advanced = 0
    latest = undefined
    mockMotionPreference(false)
  })

  afterEach(() => {
    act(() => root.unmount())
    vi.useRealTimers()
  })

  it('advances after the delay', () => {
    mount()
    act(() => {
      vi.advanceTimersByTime(1000)
    })
    expect(advanced).toBe(1)
  })

  it('stops while the user has paused and continues on resume', () => {
    mount()
    act(() => latest?.togglePause())
    act(() => {
      vi.advanceTimersByTime(5000)
    })
    expect(advanced).toBe(0)

    act(() => latest?.togglePause())
    act(() => {
      vi.advanceTimersByTime(1000)
    })
    expect(advanced).toBe(1)
  })

  it('stops while the mouse is over the carousel', () => {
    mount()
    act(() => latest?.handlers.onMouseEnter())
    act(() => {
      vi.advanceTimersByTime(5000)
    })
    expect(advanced).toBe(0)

    act(() => latest?.handlers.onMouseLeave())
    act(() => {
      vi.advanceTimersByTime(1000)
    })
    expect(advanced).toBe(1)
  })

  it('never advances when reduced motion is requested', () => {
    mockMotionPreference(true)
    mount()
    act(() => {
      vi.advanceTimersByTime(10000)
    })
    expect(advanced).toBe(0)
    expect(latest?.reducedMotion).toBe(true)
  })
})


========================================================================================
FILE: src/features/landing/hooks/useAutoplay.ts
========================================================================================
import { useEffect, useState, type FocusEvent } from 'react'

// true when the visitor asked their device for less motion
function usePrefersReducedMotion(): boolean {
  const query = '(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)'
  const [reduced, setReduced] = useState(
    () => typeof window.matchMedia === 'function' && window.matchMedia(query).matches,
  )

  useEffect(() => {
    if (typeof window.matchMedia !== 'function') return
    const media = window.matchMedia(query)
    const onChange = () => setReduced(media.matches)
    media.addEventListener('change', onChange)
    return () => media.removeEventListener('change', onChange)
  }, [])

  return reduced
}

// Auto-advance for a carousel (WCAG 2.2.2 Pause, Stop, Hide).
// The timer only runs when ALL of these are true:
//   - the visitor has not pressed the pause button
//   - the mouse is not over the carousel
//   - keyboard focus is not inside the carousel
//   - the device does not ask for reduced motion
// `advance` must change whenever the slide changes so the timer restarts.
export function useAutoplay(delayMs: number, advance: () => void) {
  const reducedMotion = usePrefersReducedMotion()
  const [paused, setPaused] = useState(false)
  const [hovered, setHovered] = useState(false)
  const [focused, setFocused] = useState(false)

  const running = !paused && !hovered && !focused && !reducedMotion

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!running) return
    const timer = setTimeout(advance, delayMs)
    return () => clearTimeout(timer)
  }, [running, advance, delayMs])

  // spread these on the element that wraps the whole carousel
  const handlers = {
    onMouseEnter: () => setHovered(true),
    onMouseLeave: () => setHovered(false),
    onFocus: (e: FocusEvent<HTMLElement>) => {
      // only keyboard focus pauses; clicking an arrow with the mouse does not
      if (e.target.matches(':focus-visible')) setFocused(true)
    },
    onBlur: (e: FocusEvent<HTMLElement>) => {
      if (!e.currentTarget.contains(e.relatedTarget as Node | null)) setFocused(false)
    },
  }

  return {
    running,
    paused,
    reducedMotion,
    togglePause: () => setPaused((value) => !value),
    handlers,
  }
}

FILE: src/features/landing/pages/HomePage.tsx
========================================================================================
import { useNavigate, useSearch } from '@tanstack/react-router'
import { filtersFromSearch, searchFromFilters, type Filters } from '../filters'
import HeroSlider from '../components/HeroSlider'
import About from '../components/About'
import EventsSection from '../components/EventsSection'
import Organizers from '../components/Organizers'
import Testimonials from '../components/Testimonials'
import Contact from '../components/Contact'

export default function HomePage() {
  const search = useSearch({ from: '/' })
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const filters = filtersFromSearch(search)

  // Typing replaces the history entry so the typeahead does not spam Back.
  function setFilters(next: Filters, options?: { replace?: boolean }) {
    navigate({ to: '/', search: searchFromFilters(next), replace: options?.replace })
  }

  // Committing the search carries the same filters to the full catalogue.
  function showAll(next: Filters) {
    navigate({ to: '/events', search: searchFromFilters(next) })
  }

  return (
    <>
      <HeroSlider filters={filters} setFilters={setFilters} onShowAll={showAll} />
      <About />
      <EventsSection filters={filters} />
      <Organizers />
      <Testimonials />
      <Contact />
    </>
  )
}

  .ticket-row input:first-child { grid-column: 1 / -1; }
}

import React from 'react';

export const AttendeeTable: React.FC<AttendeeTableProps> = ({ attendees }) => {

              <td>{attendee.purchaseDate}</td>
              <td>
                <span className={`status-badge ${attendee.status === 'Checked In' ? 'published' : 'draft'}`}>
                  {attendee.status}
                </span>
              </td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
};

FILE: src/features/organizer/components/AttendeeList.tsx
========================================================================================
import { useMemo } from 'react'

interface AttendeeListProps {
  eventId?: string
}

export function AttendeeList({ eventId }: AttendeeListProps) {
  const attendees = useMemo(() => {
    if (!eventId) return []
    try {
      // 1. Gather all possible user profiles / accounts from localStorage
      const userLookup = new Map()
      for (let i = 0; i < localStorage.length; i++) {
        const key = localStorage.key(i)
        if (!key) continue
        const val = localStorage.getItem(key)
        try {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(val || '')
          const list = Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed : [parsed]
          for (const item of list) {
            if (item && typeof item === 'object') {
              const uid = item.id || item.userId || item.uid
              const uEmail = item.email || item.userEmail || item.buyerEmail
              const uName = item.name || item.userName || item.buyerName || item.fullName
              
              if (uEmail) {
                if (uid) userLookup.set(String(uid), { name: uName || uEmail.split('@')[0], email: uEmail })
                userLookup.set(String(uEmail).toLowerCase(), { name: uName || uEmail.split('@')[0], email: uEmail })
              }
            }
          }
        } catch {}
      }

      // 2. Scan for tickets / bookings matching this eventId
      const rawTickets: any[] = []
      for (let i = 0; i < localStorage.length; i++) {
        const key = localStorage.key(i)
        if (!key) continue
        const val = localStorage.getItem(key)
        try {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(val || '')
          
          function findTickets(obj: any, parentInfo: any = {}) {
            if (!obj || typeof obj !== 'object') return
            if (Array.isArray(obj)) {
              for (const item of obj) findTickets(item, parentInfo)
              return
            }

            const currentInfo = {
              name: obj.userName || obj.buyerName || obj.name || obj.customerName || parentInfo.name,
              email: obj.userEmail || obj.buyerEmail || obj.email || obj.customerEmail || parentInfo.email,
              userId: obj.userId || obj.user_id || parentInfo.userId,
            }

            const isMatch = 
              obj.eventId === eventId || 
              obj.event_id === eventId || 
              obj.id === eventId || 
              (obj.event && obj.event.id === eventId)

            // If it looks like a ticket item
            if (isMatch && (obj.ticketNumber || obj.type || obj.typeName || obj.ticketType || obj.price !== undefined)) {
              if (!obj.password && !obj.role && !obj.ticketTypes) {
                rawTickets.push({ ...obj, ...currentInfo })
              }
            }

            for (const k of Object.keys(obj)) {
              if (typeof obj[k] === 'object' && obj[k] !== null) {
                findTickets(obj[k], currentInfo)
              }
            }
          }

          findTickets(parsed)
        } catch {}
      }

      // 3. Map and format with actual user details resolved
      const uniqueMap = new Map()
      for (const t of rawTickets) {
        const tid = t.id || t.ticketNumber || Math.random().toString()
        if (!uniqueMap.has(tid)) {
          let resolvedName = t.name
          let resolvedEmail = t.email

          // Try lookup by userId or email
          if (t.userId && userLookup.has(String(t.userId))) {
            const u = userLookup.get(String(t.userId))
            resolvedName = u.name
            resolvedEmail = u.email
          } else if (resolvedEmail && userLookup.has(String(resolvedEmail).toLowerCase())) {
            const u = userLookup.get(String(resolvedEmail).toLowerCase())
            resolvedName = u.name || resolvedName
          }

          uniqueMap.set(tid, {
            id: tid,
            ticketNumber: t.ticketNumber || String(tid).slice(0, 8).toUpperCase(),
            typeName: t.typeName || t.ticketTypeName || t.type || 'Standard',
            buyerName: resolvedName && resolvedName !== 'Customer' ? resolvedName : (resolvedEmail ? resolvedEmail.split('@')[0] : 'Event Attendee'),
            buyerEmail: resolvedEmail || 'attendee@ticket.com',
            purchasedAt: t.createdAt || t.date ? new Date(t.createdAt || t.date).toLocaleString() : new Date().toLocaleString(),
            checkedIn: t.checkedIn || false,
          })
        }
      }

      return Array.from(uniqueMap.values())
    } catch {
      return []
    }
  }, [eventId])

  if (attendees.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm sm:p-8 text-center">
        <h3 className="text-xl font-bold">Ticket Purchases & Attendees</h3>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-500">No ticket purchases recorded for this specific event yet.</p>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm sm:p-8">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <h3 className="text-xl font-bold">Ticket Purchases & Attendees</h3>
        <span className="rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700">
          {attendees.length} total sold for this event
        </span>
      </div>
      <div className="mt-6 overflow-x-auto">
        <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse">
          <thead>
            <tr className="border-b border-slate-200 text-xs font-semibold uppercase text-slate-500">
              <th className="pb-3 pr-4">Ticket #</th>
              <th className="pb-3 px-4">Buyer Name</th>
              <th className="pb-3 px-4">Email</th>
              <th className="pb-3 px-4">Type</th>
              <th className="pb-3 px-4">Purchased At</th>
              <th className="pb-3 pl-4">Status</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100 text-sm">
            {attendees.map((att) => (
              <tr key={att.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50/50">
                <td className="py-3.5 pr-4 font-mono font-semibold text-navy">{att.ticketNumber}</td>
                <td className="py-3.5 px-4 font-medium text-slate-900">{att.buyerName}</td>
                <td className="py-3.5 px-4 text-slate-600">{att.buyerEmail}</td>
                <td className="py-3.5 px-4 text-slate-600">{att.typeName}</td>
                <td className="py-3.5 px-4 text-slate-500">{att.purchasedAt}</td>
                <td className="py-3.5 pl-4">
                  <span className={`inline-flex rounded-full px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold ${
                    att.checkedIn ? 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-700' : 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700'
                  }`}>
                    {att.checkedIn ? 'Checked In' : 'Valid'}
                  </span>
                </td>
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


========================================================================================
FILE: src/features/organizer/components/EventForm.tsx
========================================================================================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { totalCapacity, type NewEventInput } from '../../../lib/backendOrganizer';

interface EventFormProps {
  onSubmit: (event: NewEventInput) => Promise<void> | void;
  onCancel: () => void;
}

interface TicketRow {
  name: string;
  price: string;
  quantity: string;
}

const emptyRow = (name = ''): TicketRow => ({ name, price: '', quantity: '' });

export const EventForm: React.FC<EventFormProps> = ({ onSubmit, onCancel }) => {
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [description, setDescription] = useState('');
  const [venue, setVenue] = useState('');
  const [location, setLocation] = useState('');
  const [startDate, setStartDate] = useState('');
  const [startTime, setStartTime] = useState('');
  const [endDate, setEndDate] = useState('');
  const [endTime, setEndTime] = useState('');
  const [rows, setRows] = useState<TicketRow[]>([emptyRow('General')]);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<'Published' | 'Draft'>('Draft');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);

  const updateRow = (index: number, patch: Partial<TicketRow>) =>
    setRows(rows.map((row, i) => (i === index ? { ...row, ...patch } : row)));

  const ticketTypes = rows.map((row) => ({
    name: row.name.trim(),
    price: Number(row.price),
    quantity: Number(row.quantity),
  }));
  const capacity = totalCapacity(ticketTypes.filter((type) => Number.isFinite(type.quantity)));
  const startAt = startDate && startTime ? `${startDate}T${startTime}` : '';
  const endAt = endDate && endTime ? `${endDate}T${endTime}` : '';

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!name.trim() || !description.trim() || !venue.trim() || !location.trim() || !startAt || !endAt) {
      setError('Fill in the title, description, venue, city, start date and time, and end date and time.');
      return;
    }
    const startDateTime = new Date(startAt);
    const endDateTime = new Date(endAt);
    if (Number.isNaN(startDateTime.getTime()) || Number.isNaN(endDateTime.getTime())) {
      setError('Enter a valid start and end date and time.');
      return;
    }
    if (endDateTime <= startDateTime) {
      setError('The event must end after it starts.');
      return;
    }
    const badType = ticketTypes.find(
      (type) => !type.name || !(type.price >= 0) || !Number.isInteger(type.quantity) || type.quantity < 1,
    );
    if (badType || rows.some((row) => row.price === '' || row.quantity === '')) {
      setError('Every ticket type needs a name, a price (0 or more) and a whole number of tickets.');
      return;
    }
    setError('');
    setSaving(true);
    try {
      await onSubmit({
        name,
        description,
        venue,
        location,
        startAt: startDateTime.toISOString(),
        endAt: endDateTime.toISOString(),
        ticketTypes,
        status,
      });
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Could not save the event. Try again.');
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <form className="event-creation-form" onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
      {error && <div className="form-error-banner">{error}</div>}

      <div className="form-group">
        <label>Event Title</label>
        <input type="text" value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g. Afrobeat Showcase 2026" />
      </div>

      <div className="form-group">
        <label>Description</label>
        <textarea rows={3} value={description} onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)} placeholder="What is this event about?" />
      </div>

      <div className="form-group">
        <label>Venue</label>
        <input type="text" value={venue} onChange={(e) => setVenue(e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g. Bingu International Conference Centre" />
      </div>

      <div className="form-group">
        <label>City and country</label>
        <input type="text" value={location} onChange={(e) => setLocation(e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g. Lilongwe, Malawi" />
      </div>

      <div className="form-row">
        <div className="form-group">
          <label>Start date</label>
          <input type="date" value={startDate} onChange={(e) => setStartDate(e.target.value)} />
        </div>
        <div className="form-group">
          <label>Start time</label>
          <input type="time" value={startTime} onChange={(e) => setStartTime(e.target.value)} />
        </div>
        <div className="form-group">
          <label>End date</label>
          <input type="date" value={endDate} onChange={(e) => setEndDate(e.target.value)} />
        </div>
        <div className="form-group">
          <label>End time</label>
          <input type="time" value={endTime} onChange={(e) => setEndTime(e.target.value)} />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="form-group">
        <label>Ticket types (prices in MWK)</label>
        {rows.map((row, index) => (
          <div className="ticket-row" key={index}>
            <input type="text" aria-label="Ticket type name" value={row.name} onChange={(e) => updateRow(index, { name: e.target.value })} placeholder="Name, e.g. VIP" />
            <input type="number" min="0" aria-label="Price" value={row.price} onChange={(e) => updateRow(index, { price: e.target.value })} placeholder="Price" />
            <input type="number" min="1" aria-label="Number of tickets" value={row.quantity} onChange={(e) => updateRow(index, { quantity: e.target.value })} placeholder="How many" />
            {rows.length > 1 && (
              <button type="button" className="btn-action" onClick={() => setRows(rows.filter((_, i) => i !== index))}>Remove</button>
            )}
          </div>
        ))}
        <div className="ticket-row-footer">
          <button type="button" className="btn-action" onClick={() => setRows([...rows, emptyRow()])}>+ Add ticket type</button>
          <span className="form-hint">Total capacity: {capacity.toLocaleString()} tickets</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="form-group">
        <label>Visibility Status</label>
        <select value={status} onChange={(e) => setStatus(e.target.value as 'Published' | 'Draft')}>
          <option value="Draft">Save as Draft</option>
          <option value="Published">Publish Directly</option>
        </select>
      </div>

      <div className="form-actions">
        <button type="button" className="btn-secondary" onClick={onCancel} disabled={saving}>Cancel</button>
        <button type="submit" className="btn-primary" disabled={saving}>{saving ? 'Saving...' : 'Create Event'}</button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
};

FILE: src/features/organizer/components/EventTable.tsx
========================================================================================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import type { EventItem } from '../Data/Events';

interface EventTableProps {
  events: EventItem[];
  onPublish?: (id: string) => Promise<void>;
  onCancel?: (id: string) => Promise<void>;
}

export const EventTable: React.FC<EventTableProps> = ({ events, onPublish, onCancel }) => {
  const [busyId, setBusyId] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  const run = async (id: string, action?: (id: string) => Promise<void>) => {
    if (!action) return;
    setBusyId(id);
    setError('');
    try {
      await action(id);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'That did not work. Try again.');
    } finally {
      setBusyId('');
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="table-responsive">
      {error && <div className="form-error-banner">{error}</div>}
      <table className="dashboard-table">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>Event Image</th>
            <th>Event Name</th>
            <th>Date & Time</th>
            <th>Venue</th>
            <th>Tickets Sold</th>
            <th>Status</th>
            <th>Actions</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {events.map((event) => (
            <tr key={event.id}>
              <td>
                <div className="placeholder-img-frame">📅</div>
              </td>
              <td className="font-semibold">{event.name}</td>
              <td>{event.date}</td>
              <td>{event.venue}</td>
              <td>{event.ticketsSold.toLocaleString()}</td>
              <td>
                <span className={`status-badge ${event.status.toLowerCase()}`}>
                  ● {event.status}
                </span>
              </td>
              <td>
                <div className="action-buttons">
                  <button className="btn-action">👁️ View</button>
                  <button className="btn-action">✏️ Edit</button>
                  {event.status === 'Draft' && onPublish && (
                    <button className="btn-action" disabled={busyId === event.id} onClick={() => run(event.id, onPublish)}>🚀 Publish</button>
                  )}
                  {event.status !== 'Cancelled' && onCancel && (
                    <button
                      className="btn-action"
                      disabled={busyId === event.id}
                      onClick={() => { if (window.confirm(`Cancel "${event.name}"? Customers will no longer be able to buy tickets.`)) void run(event.id, onCancel); }}
                    >
                      ✖ Cancel
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>
              </td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
};

import React from 'react';
import type { EventItem } from '../Data/Events';

export const SalesChart: React.FC<{ events: EventItem[] }> = ({ events }) => {
  const maximum = Math.max(1, ...events.map((event) => event.ticketsSold));

  return (
    <div className="sales-summary-container">
      <div className="chart-header">
        <h4>Tickets sold by event</h4>
        <span className="text-muted">Live data from your events</span>
      </div>
      {events.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="text-muted">No event sales data available.</p>
      ) : (
        <ul className="space-y-3">
          {events.map((event) => (
            <li key={event.id}>
              <div className="mb-1 flex justify-between gap-3 text-sm">
                <span className="truncate">{event.name}</span>
                <strong>{event.ticketsSold.toLocaleString()}</strong>
              </div>
              <div className="h-2 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-slate-100">
                <div
                  className="h-full rounded-full bg-navy"
                  style={{ width: `${(event.ticketsSold / maximum) * 100}%` }}
                />
              </div>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};
