FILE: src/features/organizer/hooks/useAttendee.test.ts
========================================================================================
// @vitest-environment jsdom
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { saveEvent, type EventInput } from '../../../lib/events'
import { DEMO_CUSTOMER_ID, issueBooking } from '../../../lib/tickets'
import { buildAttendeePurchases, fetchBackendAttendees, filterAttendees } from './useAttendee'

const ORGANIZER = { id: 'u-2', name: 'Nyasa Events' }

const eventInput: EventInput = {
  title: 'Organizer Test Fest',
  description: 'A test event',
  category: 'Festivals',
  city: 'Lilongwe',
  venue: 'Test Grounds',
  date: '2026-12-01',
  time: '18:00',
  organizer: 'Nyasa Events',
  ownerId: ORGANIZER.id,
  image: '',
  status: 'Published',
  ticketTypes: [
    { id: 'general', name: 'General', price: 1000, capacity: 10, sold: 0 },
    { id: 'vip', name: 'VIP', price: 5000, capacity: 5, sold: 0 },
  ],
}

beforeEach(() => {
  window.localStorage.clear()
})

afterEach(() => {
  vi.unstubAllGlobals()
})

describe('fetchBackendAttendees', () => {
  it('loads sold tickets for the organizer events and excludes unowned or unsold tickets', async () => {
    const fetchMock = vi.fn(async (input: RequestInfo | URL) => {
      const url = String(input)
      let body: unknown
      if (url.includes('/events/mine')) {
        body = {
          data: [{ id: '42', title: 'Organizer Test Fest', venue: 'Test Grounds', startAt: '2026-12-01T18:00:00.000Z', status: 'PUBLISHED' }],
          meta: { totalPages: 1 },
        }
      } else if (url.endsWith('/events/42/sales-summary')) {
        body = { totals: { sold: 2 } }
      } else if (url.endsWith('/tickets/events/42')) {
        body = [
          { ticketNumber: 'TKT-1', ticketTypeId: 'vip', customerId: 'customer-7', status: 'ISSUED', issuedAt: '2026-11-08T10:00:00.000Z', checkedInAt: null },
          { ticketNumber: 'TKT-2', ticketTypeId: 'vip', customerId: null, status: 'ISSUED', issuedAt: '2026-11-08T10:00:00.000Z', checkedInAt: null },
          { ticketNumber: 'TKT-3', ticketTypeId: 'vip', customerId: 'customer-8', status: 'CANCELLED', issuedAt: '2026-11-08T10:00:00.000Z', checkedInAt: null },
        ]
      } else if (url.endsWith('/events/42/ticket-types')) {
        body = [{ ticketTypeId: 'vip', name: 'VIP', price: 25000 }]
      } else {
        throw new Error(`Unexpected request: ${url}`)
      }

      return new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status: 200, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })
    })
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchMock)

    const attendees = await fetchBackendAttendees()

    expect(attendees).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(attendees[0]).toMatchObject({
      id: 'TKT-1',
      name: 'Customer #customer-7',
      event: 'Organizer Test Fest',
      eventId: '42',
      ticketType: 'VIP',
      quantity: 1,
      amount: 25000,
      status: 'Registered',
    })
    expect(fetchMock.mock.calls.some(([url]) => String(url).endsWith('/tickets/events/42'))).toBe(true)
  })
})

describe('buildAttendeePurchases', () => {
  it('only returns purchases for the organizer own events', () => {
    const purchases = buildAttendeePurchases(ORGANIZER)

    expect(purchases).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(purchases[0]).toMatchObject({
      name: 'Chimwemwe Banda',
      email: 'customer@example.com',
      event: 'Lake of Stars Live',
      eventId: 'lake-of-stars',
      ticketType: 'General',
      quantity: 1,
      amount: 35500,
      status: 'Registered',
    })
    expect(purchases[0].purchasedAt).toBeTruthy()
    expect(purchases[0].purchaseDate).not.toBe('Unknown')
  })

  it('returns nothing without a signed-in organizer or owned events', () => {
    expect(buildAttendeePurchases(null)).toEqual([])
    expect(buildAttendeePurchases({ id: 'u-3', name: 'Gate Staff' })).toEqual([])
  })

  it('includes purchases for newly created events owned by the organizer', () => {
    const event = saveEvent(eventInput)
    issueBooking({
      userId: DEMO_CUSTOMER_ID,
      event,
      ticketType: event.ticketTypes[1],
      quantity: 2,
      holder: 'Chimwemwe Banda',
    })

    const purchases = buildAttendeePurchases(ORGANIZER)
    const created = purchases.find((purchase) => purchase.eventId === event.id)

    expect(created).toMatchObject({
      ticketType: 'VIP',
      quantity: 2,
      amount: 10500,
    })
  })

  it('does not leak another organizer event into the results', () => {
    const event = saveEvent({ ...eventInput, ownerId: 'u-4' })
    issueBooking({
      userId: DEMO_CUSTOMER_ID,
      event,
      ticketType: event.ticketTypes[0],
      quantity: 1,
      holder: 'Chimwemwe Banda',
    })

    const purchases = buildAttendeePurchases(ORGANIZER)
    expect(purchases.some((purchase) => purchase.eventId === event.id)).toBe(false)
  })
})

describe('filterAttendees', () => {
  it('filters by customer name, email and event title', () => {
    const purchases = buildAttendeePurchases(ORGANIZER)

    expect(filterAttendees(purchases, { search: 'chimwemwe' })).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(filterAttendees(purchases, { search: 'customer@example.com' })).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(filterAttendees(purchases, { search: 'lake of stars' })).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(filterAttendees(purchases, { search: 'no such customer' })).toHaveLength(0)
  })

  it('filters by event and ticket type', () => {
    const purchases = buildAttendeePurchases(ORGANIZER)

    expect(filterAttendees(purchases, { eventId: 'lake-of-stars' })).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(filterAttendees(purchases, { eventId: 'dev-summit' })).toHaveLength(0)
    expect(filterAttendees(purchases, { ticketType: 'General' })).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(filterAttendees(purchases, { ticketType: 'VIP' })).toHaveLength(0)
    expect(filterAttendees(purchases, { eventId: 'all', ticketType: 'all' })).toHaveLength(1)
  })
})


========================================================================================
FILE: src/features/organizer/hooks/useAttendee.ts
========================================================================================
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { useAuth } from '../../authentication';
import { listAccounts } from '../../authentication/authStore';
import { isEventOwnedBy, listAllEvents } from '../../../lib/events';
import { listBookings, listTickets } from '../../../lib/tickets';
import { api, USE_BACKEND } from '../../../lib/backend';
import { listMyEvents } from '../../../lib/backendOrganizer';
import type { AttendeeFilters, AttendeeItem } from '../Data/Attendee';

export interface AttendeeUser {
  id: string;
  name: string;
}

interface BackendEventTicket {
  ticketNumber: string;
  ticketTypeId: string;
  customerId: string | null;
  status: 'ISSUED' | 'USED' | 'CANCELLED' | 'REFUNDED';
  issuedAt: string;
  checkedInAt: string | null;
}

interface BackendTicketType {
  ticketTypeId: string;
  name: string;
  price: number | string;
}

function formatPurchaseDate(value: string): string {
  const date = new Date(value);
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return 'Unknown';
  return date.toLocaleString('en-GB', { dateStyle: 'medium', timeStyle: 'short' });
}

// In demo mode, one row per confirmed booking joined with the buyer and tickets.
export function buildAttendeePurchases(user: AttendeeUser | null): AttendeeItem[] {
  if (!user) return [];

  const ownedEvents = listAllEvents().filter((event) => isEventOwnedBy(event, user));
  const eventById = new Map(ownedEvents.map((event) => [event.id, event]));
  if (eventById.size === 0) return [];

  const tickets = listTickets();
  const ticketById = new Map(tickets.map((ticket) => [ticket.id, ticket]));
  const accountById = new Map(listAccounts().map((account) => [account.id, account]));

  return listBookings()
    .filter((booking) => booking.status === 'confirmed' && eventById.has(booking.eventId))
    .map((booking) => {
      const bookingTickets = booking.ticketIds
        .map((id) => ticketById.get(id))
        .filter((ticket) => ticket !== undefined);
      const ticketTypes = [...new Set(bookingTickets.map((ticket) => ticket.ticketType))];
      const account = accountById.get(booking.userId);
      const holder = bookingTickets[0]?.holder;
      const allCheckedIn =
        bookingTickets.length > 0 && bookingTickets.every((ticket) => ticket.status === 'used');

      return {
        id: booking.id,
        name: account?.name ?? holder ?? 'Guest',
        email: account?.email ?? '',
        event: eventById.get(booking.eventId)?.title ?? booking.eventTitle,
        eventId: booking.eventId,
        ticketType: ticketTypes.length > 0 ? ticketTypes.join(', ') : 'Unknown',
        quantity: booking.quantity,
        amount: booking.total,
        purchaseDate: formatPurchaseDate(booking.createdAt),
        purchasedAt: booking.createdAt,
        status: allCheckedIn ? 'Checked In' : 'Registered',
      } satisfies AttendeeItem;
    })
    .sort((a, b) => new Date(b.purchasedAt).getTime() - new Date(a.purchasedAt).getTime());
}

export async function fetchBackendAttendees(): Promise<AttendeeItem[]> {
  const events = await listMyEvents();
  const attendeesByEvent = await Promise.all(
    events.map(async (event) => {
      const [tickets, ticketTypes] = await Promise.all([
        api<BackendEventTicket[]>(`/tickets/events/${encodeURIComponent(event.id)}`),
        api<BackendTicketType[]>(`/events/${encodeURIComponent(event.id)}/ticket-types`),
      ]);
      const ticketTypeById = new Map(ticketTypes.map((ticketType) => [ticketType.ticketTypeId, ticketType]));

      return tickets.flatMap((ticket) => {
        if (!ticket.customerId || ticket.status === 'CANCELLED' || ticket.status === 'REFUNDED') return [];
        const ticketType = ticketTypeById.get(ticket.ticketTypeId);
        const purchasedAt = ticket.issuedAt;

        return [{
          id: ticket.ticketNumber,
          name: `Customer #${ticket.customerId}`,
          email: '',
          event: event.name,
          eventId: event.id,
          ticketType: ticketType?.name ?? 'Unknown',
          quantity: 1,
          amount: Number(ticketType?.price ?? 0),
          purchaseDate: formatPurchaseDate(purchasedAt),
          purchasedAt,
          status: ticket.status === 'USED' || ticket.checkedInAt ? 'Checked In' : 'Registered',
        } satisfies AttendeeItem];
      });
    }),
  );

  return attendeesByEvent.flat().sort(
    (a, b) => new Date(b.purchasedAt).getTime() - new Date(a.purchasedAt).getTime(),
  );
}

export function filterAttendees(
  attendees: AttendeeItem[],
  filters: AttendeeFilters,
): AttendeeItem[] {
  const search = filters.search?.trim().toLowerCase() ?? '';

  return attendees.filter((attendee) => {
    if (filters.eventId && filters.eventId !== 'all' && attendee.eventId !== filters.eventId) {
      return false;
    }
    if (
      filters.ticketType &&
      filters.ticketType !== 'all' &&
      !attendee.ticketType.split(', ').includes(filters.ticketType)
    ) {
      return false;
    }
    if (!search) return true;
    return [attendee.name, attendee.email, attendee.event, attendee.ticketType, attendee.id].some(
      (value) => value.toLowerCase().includes(search),
    );
  });
}

export const useAttendees = () => {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [eventId, setEventId] = useState('all');
  const [ticketType, setTicketType] = useState('all');

  const backendAttendees = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['organizer-attendees', user?.id],
    queryFn: fetchBackendAttendees,
    enabled: USE_BACKEND && !!user,
    refetchInterval: USE_BACKEND ? 30_000 : false,
  });
  const localAttendees = useMemo(
    () => (USE_BACKEND ? [] : buildAttendeePurchases(user)),
    [user],
  );
  const allAttendees = useMemo(
    () => (USE_BACKEND ? backendAttendees.data ?? [] : localAttendees),
    [backendAttendees.data, localAttendees],
  );
  const attendees = useMemo(
    () => filterAttendees(allAttendees, { search, eventId, ticketType }),
    [allAttendees, search, eventId, ticketType],
  );

  const eventOptions = useMemo(() => {
    const byId = new Map(allAttendees.map((attendee) => [attendee.eventId, attendee.event]));
    return [...byId.entries()]
      .map(([id, title]) => ({ id, title }))
      .sort((a, b) => a.title.localeCompare(b.title));
  }, [allAttendees]);

  const ticketTypeOptions = useMemo(
    () =>
      [...new Set(allAttendees.flatMap((attendee) => attendee.ticketType.split(', ')))].sort((a, b) =>
        a.localeCompare(b),
      ),
    [allAttendees],
  );

  return {
    attendees,
    totalAttendees: allAttendees.length,
    checkedInCount: allAttendees.filter((attendee) => attendee.status === 'Checked In').length,
    loading: USE_BACKEND && backendAttendees.isLoading,
    error: USE_BACKEND && backendAttendees.error instanceof Error ? backendAttendees.error.message : '',
    search,
    setSearch,
    eventId,
    setEventId,
    ticketType,
    setTicketType,
    eventOptions,
    ticketTypeOptions,
  };
};


========================================================================================
FILE: src/features/organizer/hooks/useEventStats.ts
========================================================================================
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import {
  cancelOrganizerEvent,
  createOrganizerEvent,
  listMyEvents,
  publishEvent,
  type NewEventInput,
} from '../../../lib/backendOrganizer';

const MY_EVENTS_KEY = ['events', 'mine'];

export const useEvents = () => {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [filterStatus, setFilterStatus] = useState('All');

  const remote = useQuery({ queryKey: MY_EVENTS_KEY, queryFn: listMyEvents });
  const events = remote.data ?? [];

  // The public catalogue and the organizer list both change after any of these actions.
  const refresh = () => queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['events'] });

  const create = useMutation({ mutationFn: createOrganizerEvent, onSuccess: refresh });
  const publish = useMutation({ mutationFn: publishEvent, onSuccess: refresh });
  const cancel = useMutation({ mutationFn: cancelOrganizerEvent, onSuccess: refresh });

  const addEvent = async (newEvent: NewEventInput): Promise<void> => {
    await create.mutateAsync(newEvent);
  };

  const publishExisting = async (id: string): Promise<void> => {
    await publish.mutateAsync(id);
  };

  const cancelExisting = async (id: string): Promise<void> => {
    await cancel.mutateAsync(id);
  };

  const filteredEvents = events.filter(item => {
    const matchesSearch = item.name.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) || item.venue.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase());
    const matchesStatus = filterStatus === 'All' || item.status === filterStatus;
    return matchesSearch && matchesStatus;
  });

  const totalTickets = events.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.ticketsSold, 0);

  return {
    events: filteredEvents,
    totalEvents: events.length,
    publishedCount: events.filter(e => e.status === 'Published').length,
    draftCount: events.filter(e => e.status === 'Draft').length,
    totalTickets,
    search,
    setSearch,
    filterStatus,
    setFilterStatus,
    addEvent,
    publishEvent: publishExisting,
    cancelEvent: cancelExisting,
    loading: remote.isLoading,
    loadError: remote.error instanceof Error ? remote.error.message : '',
  };
};

FILE: src/features/organizer/hooks/useSalesSeries.test.ts

// @vitest-environment jsdom
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { BOOKINGS_KEY, type BookingRecord } from '../../../lib/tickets'
import { buildSalesSeries } from './useSalesSeries'

const NOW = new Date(2026, 9, 26, 12, 0, 0) // 26 Oct 2026, local time

function localTimestamp(daysAgo: number, hour = 12): string {
  const date = new Date(NOW)
  date.setDate(date.getDate() - daysAgo)
  date.setHours(hour, 0, 0, 0)
  return date.toISOString()
}

function booking(patch: Partial<BookingRecord> & { id: string }): BookingRecord {
  return {
    userId: 'u-1',
    eventId: 'evt-test',
    eventTitle: 'Test Concert',
    venue: 'Test Venue',
    date: '2026-11-14',
    quantity: 1,
    subtotal: 35000,
    serviceFee: 500,
    total: 35500,
    status: 'confirmed',
    ticketIds: [],
    createdAt: localTimestamp(0),
    ...patch,
  }
}

describe('buildSalesSeries', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    window.localStorage.clear()
  })

  it('groups confirmed sales for the selected event into the last 7 days', () => {
    window.localStorage.setItem(
      BOOKINGS_KEY,
      JSON.stringify([
        booking({ id: 'b-today', quantity: 2, createdAt: localTimestamp(0, 9) }),
        booking({ id: 'b-two-days', quantity: 1, createdAt: localTimestamp(2, 15) }),
        // other event — must not appear
        booking({ id: 'b-other', eventId: 'evt-other', quantity: 5, createdAt: localTimestamp(1) }),
        // cancelled — must not appear
        booking({ id: 'b-cancelled', status: 'cancelled', quantity: 3, createdAt: localTimestamp(3) }),
        // outside the window — must not appear
        booking({ id: 'b-old', quantity: 4, createdAt: localTimestamp(8) }),
      ]),
    )

    const series = buildSalesSeries('evt-test', NOW)

    expect(series).toHaveLength(7)
    expect(series.map((point) => point.date)).toEqual([
      '2026-10-20',
      '2026-10-21',
      '2026-10-22',
      '2026-10-23',
      '2026-10-24',
      '2026-10-25',
      '2026-10-26',
    ])
    expect(series.reduce((sum, point) => sum + point.tickets, 0)).toBe(3)
    expect(series[6].tickets).toBe(2)
    expect(series[4].tickets).toBe(1)
  })

  it('returns a full zero-filled week when there are no sales', () => {
    const series = buildSalesSeries('evt-test', NOW)
    expect(series).toHaveLength(7)
    expect(series.every((point) => point.tickets === 0)).toBe(true)
  })
})

FILE: src/features/organizer/hooks/useSalesSeries.ts
========================================================================================
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { listBookings } from '../../../lib/tickets'

export interface SalesPoint {
  date: string // YYYY-MM-DD, local calendar day
  label: string // short weekday, used on the chart axis
  tickets: number
}

export const SALES_WINDOW_DAYS = 7

function localDateKey(date: Date): string {
  const year = date.getFullYear()
  const month = String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')
  const day = String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')
  return `${year}-${month}-${day}`
}

// Groups confirmed ticket sales for one event into the last seven local days.
// Exported separately so the calculation can be unit tested with a fixed clock.
export function buildSalesSeries(eventId: string, now: Date = new Date()): SalesPoint[] {
  const days: SalesPoint[] = []
  for (let offset = SALES_WINDOW_DAYS - 1; offset >= 0; offset -= 1) {
    const date = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate() - offset)
    days.push({
      date: localDateKey(date),
      label: date.toLocaleDateString('en-GB', { weekday: 'short' }),
      tickets: 0,
    })
  }

  const byDate = new Map(days.map((day) => [day.date, day]))

  for (const booking of listBookings()) {
    if (booking.status !== 'confirmed' || booking.eventId !== eventId) continue
    const createdAt = new Date(booking.createdAt)
    if (Number.isNaN(createdAt.getTime())) continue
    const point = byDate.get(localDateKey(createdAt))
    if (point) point.tickets += booking.quantity
  }

  return days
}

// Keyed by event so changing the selector refreshes the chart.
export function useSalesSeries(eventId?: string) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['sales-series', eventId],
    queryFn: () => buildSalesSeries(eventId ?? ''),
    enabled: !!eventId,
  })
}

import { useAttendees } from '../hooks/useAttendee';

function initials(name: string | undefined): string {
  return name?.trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 2).map((part) => part[0].toUpperCase()).join('') || 'OR';
}

export const OrganizerDashboardPage: React.FC = () => {

  const { user } = useAuth();

    attendees, totalAttendees, checkedInCount,

    ticketType: atTicketType, setTicketType: setAtTicketType,
    eventOptions, ticketTypeOptions, loading: attendeeLoading, error: attendeeError,
  } = useAttendees();

    await addEvent(newEvent);
    setCurrentTab('events');
  };

  return (
    <div className="dashboard-root">
      {/* Sidebar Architecture Layout */}
      <aside className="dashboard-sidebar">
        <div className="sidebar-brand">
          <div className="brand-logo">FF6</div>
          <div className="brand-info">
            <span className="panel-title">FF6 Ticket</span>
            <span className="panel-subtitle">Organizer Panel</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="user-profile-badge">
          <div className="avatar-placeholder">{initials(user?.name)}</div>
          <div className="user-details">
            <span className="user-name">{user?.name ?? 'Organizer'}</span>
            <span className="user-role">Organizer ▼</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <nav className="sidebar-nav">
          <button className={`nav-link-btn ${currentTab === 'events' ? 'active' : ''}`} onClick={() => setCurrentTab('events')}>
            <span className="nav-icon">📅</span> My Events
          </button>
          <button className={`nav-link-btn ${currentTab === 'create' ? 'active' : ''}`} onClick={() => setCurrentTab('create')}>
            <span className="nav-icon">➕</span> Create Event
          </button>
          <button className={`nav-link-btn ${currentTab === 'staff' ? 'active' : ''}`} onClick={() => setCurrentTab('staff')}>
            <span className="nav-icon">👥</span> Staff Roster
          </button>
          <button className={`nav-link-btn ${currentTab === 'attendees' ? 'active' : ''}`} onClick={() => setCurrentTab('attendees')}>
            <span className="nav-icon">🎟️</span> Attendees

          <CreateEventPage onEventCreated={handleEventFormSubmission} onCancel={() => setCurrentTab('events')} />
        ) : (
          <div className="page-content-wrapper">
            
            {/* Context Dependent Metric Row Blocks */}
            {currentTab === 'events' && (
              <>
                <div className="content-header">
                  <div>
                    <h2 className="view-title">My Events</h2>
                    <p className="view-subtitle">Manage and track your active performance profiles.</p>
                  </div>
                  <button className="btn-primary-header" onClick={() => setCurrentTab('create')}>+ Create Event</button>
                </div>
                <div className="metrics-grid">
                  <StatCard title="Total Events" value={totalEvents} icon="📊" color="#3b82f6" />
                  <StatCard title="Published" value={publishedCount} icon="✅" color="#10b981" />
                  <StatCard title="Draft" value={draftCount} icon="🕒" color="#64748b" />
                  <StatCard title="Total Tickets Sold" value={totalTickets.toLocaleString()} icon="📈" color="#f59e0b" />
                </div>
                <div className="filter-toolbar">
                  <SearchBar value={evSearch} onChange={setEvSearch} placeholder="Search events by profile name or venue location..." />
                  <select className="toolbar-dropdown" value={filterStatus} onChange={(e) => setFilterStatus(e.target.value)}>
                    <option value="All">All Statuses</option>
                    <option value="Published">Published Only</option>
                    <option value="Draft">Drafts Only</option>
                  </select>
                </div>
                {evError && <div className="form-error-banner">{evError}</div>}
                {evLoading ? <p className="view-subtitle">Loading your events...</p> : <EventTable events={events} onPublish={publishEvent} onCancel={cancelEvent} />}
              </>
            )}

            {currentTab === 'staff' && (
              <>
                <div className="content-header">
                  <div>
                    <h2 className="view-title">My Staff Roster</h2>
                    <p className="view-subtitle">Manage your staff and the events they are allowed to scan.</p>
                  </div>
                  <Link to="/organizer/staff" className="btn-primary-header">Create or manage staff</Link>
                </div>
                <p role="status" className="form-error-banner">
                  Staff accounts and event assignments are not supported by the connected backend yet.
                  No local staff records are shown or saved.
                </p>
              </>
            )}

            {currentTab === 'attendees' && (
              <>
                <div className="content-header">
                  <div>
                    <h2 className="view-title">Customers &amp; Purchases</h2>
                    <p className="view-subtitle">Track ticket sales and customers across your events.</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="metrics-grid">
                  <StatCard title="Total Attendees" value={totalAttendees} icon="🎟️" color="#3b82f6" />
                  <StatCard title="Checked In" value={checkedInCount} icon="✓" color="#10b981" />
                  <StatCard title="Attendance Rate" value={`${Math.round((checkedInCount / (totalAttendees || 1)) * 100)}%`} icon="📊" color="#a855f7" />
                </div>
                <div className="filter-toolbar">
                  <SearchBar value={atSearch} onChange={setAtSearch} placeholder="Search by customer, email, event or ticket..." />
                  <select className="toolbar-dropdown" value={atEventId} onChange={(e) => setAtEventId(e.target.value)}>
                    <option value="all">All Events</option>
                    {eventOptions.map((event) => (
                      <option key={event.id} value={event.id}>{event.title}</option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                  <select className="toolbar-dropdown" value={atTicketType} onChange={(e) => setAtTicketType(e.target.value)}>
                    <option value="all">All Ticket Types</option>
                    {ticketTypeOptions.map((type) => (
                      <option key={type} value={type}>{type}</option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>
                <div className="split-view-layout">
                  <div className="split-table-side">
                    {attendeeLoading
                      ? <p className="view-subtitle">Loading ticket purchases...</p>
                      : attendeeError
                        ? <div role="alert" className="form-error-banner">{attendeeError}</div>
                        : <AttendeeTable attendees={attendees} />}
                  </div>
                  <div className="split-analytics-side">
                    <SalesChart events={events} />
                  </div>
                </div>
              </>
            )}

          </div>
        )}
      </main>
    </div>
  );
};


========================================================================================
FILE: src/features/organizer/pages/OrganizerKycPage.tsx
========================================================================================
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import { useNavigate } from '@tanstack/react-router'
import { useAuth } from '../../authentication'
import { getKYC, saveKYC } from '../../../lib/verification'

// Shown to an organizer the first time they log in. The details are saved and accepted
// immediately: there is no admin review step.
export function OrganizerKycPage() {
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const existing = user ? getKYC(user.id) : null

  const [businessName, setBusinessName] = useState(existing?.businessName ?? '')
  const [phoneNumber, setPhoneNumber] = useState(existing?.phoneNumber ?? '')
  const [nationalId, setNationalId] = useState(existing?.nationalId ?? '')
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  if (!user) return null

  function submit(event: FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault()
    if (!user) return
    if (!businessName.trim()) return setError('Enter your business or organization name.')
    if (!/^[+\d][\d\s-]{6,}$/.test(phoneNumber.trim())) return setError('Enter a valid phone number, for example +265 999 000 000.')
    if (nationalId.trim().length < 5) return setError('Enter your national ID number.')
    saveKYC({
      userId: user.id,
      businessName: businessName.trim(),
      phoneNumber: phoneNumber.trim(),
      nationalId: nationalId.trim(),
      status: 'Verified',
      submittedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    })
    void navigate({ to: '/organizer/dashboard' })
  }

  const field = 'mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2.5 text-slate-900 focus:border-navy focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-navy/20'

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 px-4 pb-16 pt-24">
      <form onSubmit={submit} className="mx-auto max-w-lg rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm sm:p-8" noValidate>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-navy-dark">Confirm your details</h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-600">
          Welcome, {user.name}. Before you create events, tell us about your business. This takes a minute and you only do it once.
        </p>

        {error && (
          <div role="alert" className="mt-5 rounded-lg border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">{error}</div>
        )}

        <label className="mt-5 block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">
          Business or organization name
          <input className={field} value={businessName} onChange={(e) => setBusinessName(e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g. Nyasa Events" autoComplete="organization" />
        </label>
        <label className="mt-4 block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">
          Phone number
          <input className={field} value={phoneNumber} onChange={(e) => setPhoneNumber(e.target.value)} placeholder="+265 999 000 000" inputMode="tel" autoComplete="tel" />
        </label>
        <label className="mt-4 block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">
          National ID number
          <input className={field} value={nationalId} onChange={(e) => setNationalId(e.target.value)} placeholder="Your national ID" />
        </label>

        <button type="submit" className="mt-6 w-full rounded-lg bg-navy px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-white hover:bg-navy-dark">
          Save and continue
        </button>
      </form>
    </div>
  )
}

FILE: src/features/tickets/bookingMockData.ts
========================================================================================
import { listBookingsForUser, type BookingRecord } from '../../lib/tickets'
import { withDelay } from '../../mocks/delay'
import type { Booking } from './bookingTypes'

function toBookingView(record: BookingRecord): Booking {
  return {
    id: record.id,
    eventTitle: record.eventTitle,
    venue: record.venue,
    date: record.date,
    quantity: record.quantity,
    subtotal: record.subtotal,
    serviceFee: record.serviceFee,
    total: record.total,
    status: record.status,
  }
}

export async function fetchBookings(userId?: string): Promise<Booking[]> {
  // No signed-in user means nothing to show; never fall back to every booking.
  const records = userId ? listBookingsForUser(userId) : []
  return withDelay(records.map(toBookingView), 400)
}

export type BookingStatus = 'confirmed' | 'cancelled';

export type Booking = {
  id: string;
  eventTitle: string;
  venue: string;
  date: string; // ISO date, e.g. 2026-09-12
  quantity: number;
  subtotal: number; // tickets only, in MK
  serviceFee: number; // in MK
  total: number; // what the customer paid, in MK
  status: BookingStatus;
};

FILE: src/features/tickets/components/BookingCard.tsx

import type { Booking } from '../bookingTypes';
import styles from './BookingCard.module.css';

function formatDate(iso: string) {
  return new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString('en-GB', {
    day: 'numeric',
    month: 'short',
    year: 'numeric',
  });
}

export function BookingCard({ booking }: { booking: Booking }) {
  const statusClass =
    booking.status === 'confirmed' ? styles.confirmed : styles.cancelled;

  return (
    <article className={styles.card}>
      <div className={styles.info}>
        <h3 className={styles.title}>{booking.eventTitle}</h3>
        <p className={styles.meta}>
          {booking.venue} · {formatDate(booking.date)}
        </p>
        <p className={styles.meta}>
          {booking.id} · {booking.quantity} ticket{booking.quantity > 1 ? 's' : ''}
        </p>
        <p className={styles.meta}>
          Subtotal MK {booking.subtotal.toLocaleString()} · Service fee MK{' '}
          {booking.serviceFee.toLocaleString()}
        </p>
      </div>
      <div className={styles.side}>
        <strong className={styles.total}>MK {booking.total.toLocaleString()}</strong>
        <span className={`${styles.badge} ${statusClass}`}>{booking.status}</span>
      </div>
    </article>
  );
}

FILE: src/features/tickets/components/TicketDetail.tsx
========================================================================================
import { useRef } from 'react';
import { QRCodeCanvas } from 'qrcode.react';
import type { Ticket } from '../ticketTypes';
import { formatDate } from '../ticketFormat';
import { downloadTicketImage, ticketFromView } from '../ticketImage';
import { ticketQrValue } from '../../../lib/tickets';
import styles from './TicketDetail.module.css';

// The ticket itself: status, event, QR code and the download button.
// The full details (venue, price, purchase time...) are in TicketInfoTable.
export function TicketDetail({ ticket }: { ticket: Ticket }) {
  const qrRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement>(null);
  const statusClass = ticket.status === 'valid' ? styles.valid : styles.used;

  return (
    <article className={styles.card}>
      <div className={styles.info}>
        <span className={`${styles.badge} ${statusClass}`}>{ticket.status}</span>
        <h2 className={styles.title}>{ticket.eventTitle}</h2>
        <p className={styles.venue}>
          {ticket.venue}, {ticket.city}
        </p>
        <p className={styles.venue}>
          {formatDate(ticket.date)} · {ticket.time}
        </p>

        <button
          type="button"
          className={styles.download}
          onClick={() => void downloadTicketImage(ticketFromView(ticket), qrRef.current, ticket.qrCodeDataUrl)}
        >
          Download ticket
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className={styles.qrBox}>
        {ticket.qrCodeDataUrl ? (
          <img
            src={ticket.qrCodeDataUrl}
            width={168}
            height={168}
            alt=""
            style={{ width: 168, height: 168, objectFit: 'contain' }}
          />
        ) : ticket.status === 'valid' ? (
          <QRCodeCanvas
            ref={qrRef}
            value={ticketQrValue(ticket.id)}
            size={168}
            level="M"
          />
        ) : (
          <p className={styles.qrNote}>A QR code is unavailable for this ticket.</p>
        )}
        <p className={styles.qrId}>{ticket.id}</p>
        <p className={styles.qrNote}>
          {ticket.status === 'valid'
            ? 'Show this code at the entrance'
            : ticket.status === 'used'
              ? 'This ticket has already been used'
              : `This ticket is ${ticket.status}`}
        </p>
      </div>
    </article>
  );
}

export function TicketInfoTable({ ticket }: { ticket: Ticket }) {

            </tr>
            <tr>
              <th scope="row">Time</th>
              <td>{ticket.time}</td>
            </tr>
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>

        <table className={styles.table}>
          <caption className={styles.caption}>Ticket details</caption>
          <tbody>
            <tr>
              <th scope="row">Ticket ID</th>

              <th scope="row">Entry</th>

            </tr>
            <tr>
              <th scope="row">Holder</th>
              <td>{ticket.holder}</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <th scope="row">Price</th>
              <td>{formatMoney(ticket.price)}</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <th scope="row">Status</th>

                <span className={`${styles.badge} ${statusClass}`}>{ticket.status}</span>
              </td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <th scope="row">Purchased</th>
              <td>{ticket.purchasedAt ? formatDateTime(ticket.purchasedAt) : '—'}</td>
            </tr>
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/features/tickets/components/TicketsTable.tsx

import { Link } from '@tanstack/react-router';
import type { Ticket } from '../ticketTypes';
import { formatDate, formatDateTime, formatMoney } from '../ticketFormat';
import styles from './TicketsTable.module.css';

export function TicketsTable({ tickets }: { tickets: Ticket[] }) {
  return (
    <div className={styles.wrap}>
      <table className={styles.table}>
        <caption className="sr-only">Your tickets</caption>
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th scope="col">Event</th>
            <th scope="col">Date</th>
            <th scope="col">Venue</th>
            <th scope="col">Type</th>
            <th scope="col">Price</th>
            <th scope="col">Status</th>
            <th scope="col">Purchased</th>
            <th scope="col">
              <span className="sr-only">Open</span>
            </th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {tickets.map((t, index) => (
            <tr key={t.id || index}>
              <td>
                {t.id ? (
                  <Link to="/tickets/$ticketId" params={{ ticketId: t.id }} className={styles.link}>
                    {t.eventTitle || 'Unknown Event'}
                  </Link>
                ) : (
                  <span className={styles.event}>{t.eventTitle || 'Unknown Event'}</span>
                )}
              </td>
              <td>{t.date ? formatDate(t.date) : 'TBD'}</td>
              <td>{t.venue ? `${t.venue}, ${t.city}` : '—'}</td>
              <td>{t.ticketType || 'Standard'}</td>
              <td>{formatMoney(t.price ?? 0)}</td>
              <td>
                <span
                  className={`${styles.badge} ${t.status === 'valid' ? styles.valid : styles.used}`}
                >
                  {t.status || 'valid'}
                </span>
              </td>
              <td>{t.purchasedAt ? formatDateTime(t.purchasedAt) : '—'}</td>
              <td>
                {t.id && (
                  <Link to="/tickets/$ticketId" params={{ ticketId: t.id }} className={styles.link}>
                    View ticket
                  </Link>
                )}
              </td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}


========================================================================================
FILE: src/features/tickets/hooks/useBookings.ts
========================================================================================
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { fetchBookings } from '../bookingMockData';
import { useAuth } from '../../authentication';

export function useBookings() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const userId = user?.id;
  return useQuery({
    // Keyed per user so switching accounts never shows the previous cache.
    queryKey: ['bookings', userId],
    queryFn: () => fetchBookings(userId),
    enabled: !!userId,
  });
}


========================================================================================
FILE: src/features/tickets/hooks/useTickets.ts
========================================================================================
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { fetchTickets } from '../ticketData';
import { useAuth } from '../../authentication';

export function useTickets() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const userId = user?.id;
  return useQuery({
    // Keyed per user so switching accounts never shows the previous cache.
    queryKey: ['tickets', userId],
    queryFn: () => fetchTickets(userId, user?.name ?? ''),
    enabled: !!userId,
  });
}

FILE: src/features/tickets/pages/MyTicketsPage.tsx
========================================================================================
import { useState } from 'react';
import { TicketsTable } from '../components/TicketsTable';
import { useTickets } from '../hooks/useTickets';
import { isUpcoming } from '../ticketFormat';
import styles from './MyTicketsPage.module.css';

type Tab = 'all' | 'upcoming' | 'past';

const tabs: { key: Tab; label: string }[] = [
  { key: 'all', label: 'All' },
  { key: 'upcoming', label: 'Upcoming' },
  { key: 'past', label: 'Past' },
];

export function MyTicketsPage() {
  const { data, isLoading, isError, refetch } = useTickets();
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>('all');

  const tickets = data ?? [];
  const visible = tickets.filter((t) => {
    if (tab === 'all') return true;
    return tab === 'upcoming' ? isUpcoming(t.date) : !isUpcoming(t.date);
  });

  return (
    <div className={styles.page}>
      <header className={styles.header}>
        <h1 className={styles.heading}>My Tickets</h1>
        <p className={styles.sub}>Open a ticket to see its QR code, details and entry status</p>
      </header>

      {isLoading && <p className={styles.state}>Loading your tickets...</p>}

      {isError && (
        <p className={styles.state}>
          Could not load tickets.{' '}
          <button type="button" className={styles.retry} onClick={() => refetch()}>
            Try again
          </button>
        </p>
      )}

      {!isLoading && !isError && tickets.length === 0 && (
        <p className={styles.state}>You have no tickets yet.</p>
      )}

      {tickets.length > 0 && (
        <section className={styles.section}>
          <div className={styles.filters}>
            {tabs.map((tabItem) => (
              <button
                key={tabItem.key}
                type="button"
                className={`${styles.pill} ${tab === tabItem.key ? styles.pillActive : ''}`}
                onClick={() => setTab(tabItem.key)}
              >
                {tabItem.label}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>

          {visible.length === 0 ? (
            <p className={styles.state}>No tickets to show.</p>
          ) : (
            <TicketsTable tickets={visible} />
          )}
        </section>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export default MyTicketsPage;

import { Link, useParams } from '@tanstack/react-router';
import { TicketDetail } from '../components/TicketDetail';
import { TicketInfoTable } from '../components/TicketInfoTable';
import { useTickets } from '../hooks/useTickets';
import styles from './TicketDetailPage.module.css';

export function TicketDetailPage() {
  const { ticketId } = useParams({ strict: false }) as { ticketId?: string };
  const { data, isLoading, isError, refetch } = useTickets();
  const ticket = data?.find((t) => t.id === ticketId);

  return (
    <div className={styles.page}>
      <Link to="/tickets" className={styles.back}>
        ← My tickets
      </Link>
      <h1 className={styles.heading}>Ticket details</h1>

      {isLoading && <p className={styles.state}>Loading your ticket...</p>}

      {isError && (
        <p className={styles.state}>
          Could not load this ticket.{' '}
          <button type="button" className={styles.retry} onClick={() => refetch()}>
            Try again
          </button>
        </p>
      )}

      {!isLoading && !isError && !ticket && (
        <p className={styles.state}>
          We could not find that ticket. It may belong to a different account.
        </p>
      )}

      {ticket && (
        <>
          <TicketDetail ticket={ticket} />
          <TicketInfoTable ticket={ticket} />
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export default TicketDetailPage;


========================================================================================
FILE: src/features/tickets/ticketData.ts
========================================================================================
import { api } from '../../lib/backend'
import { fetchBackendEvent } from '../../lib/backendEvents'
import type { TicketRecord } from '../../lib/tickets'
import type { Ticket, TicketStatus } from './ticketTypes'

interface BackendHistoryTicket {
  ticketNumber: string
  eventId: string
  ticketTypeId: string
  status: 'ISSUED' | 'USED' | 'CANCELLED' | 'REFUNDED'
  issuedAt: string
  checkedInAt: string | null
  qrCodeDataUrl?: string
}

export function toTicketView(record: TicketRecord): Ticket {
  return {
    id: record.id,
    eventTitle: record.eventName,
    venue: record.venue,
    city: record.city,
    date: record.date,
    time: record.time,
    ticketType: record.ticketType,
    seat: `${record.ticketType} Admission`,
    price: record.price,
    holder: record.holder,
    status: record.status,
    purchasedAt: record.createdAt,
    qrCodeDataUrl: record.qrCodeDataUrl,
  }
}

export async function fetchTickets(userId?: string, userName = ''): Promise<Ticket[]> {
  if (!userId) return []

  const records = await api<BackendHistoryTicket[]>('/tickets/history')
  const eventIds = [...new Set(records.map((ticket) => ticket.eventId))]
  const events = await Promise.all(eventIds.map((id) => fetchBackendEvent(id)))
  const eventById = new Map(events.map((event) => [event.id, event]))

  return records.flatMap((record) => {
    const event = eventById.get(record.eventId)
    const ticketType = event?.ticketTypes.find((type) => type.id === record.ticketTypeId)
    if (!event || !ticketType) return []

    const status: TicketStatus = {
      ISSUED: 'valid',
      USED: 'used',
      CANCELLED: 'cancelled',
      REFUNDED: 'refunded',
    }[record.status]

    return [{
      id: record.ticketNumber,
      eventTitle: event.title,
      venue: event.venue,
      city: event.city,
      date: event.date,
      time: event.time,
      ticketType: ticketType.name,
      seat: `${ticketType.name} Admission`,
      price: ticketType.price,
      holder: userName,
      status,
      purchasedAt: record.issuedAt,
      qrCodeDataUrl: record.qrCodeDataUrl,
    } satisfies Ticket]
  })
}


========================================================================================
FILE: src/features/tickets/ticketFormat.ts
========================================================================================
// Event dates are calendar dates without a time. Format them in UTC so the
// same ticket never renders on two different days (issue #73).
export function formatDate(iso: string): string {
  if (!iso) return 'TBD';
  const date = new Date(`${iso}T00:00:00Z`);
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return 'TBD';
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-GB', {
    day: '2-digit',
    month: 'short',
    year: 'numeric',
    timeZone: 'UTC',
  }).format(date);
}

export function formatMoney(amount: number | undefined | null): string {
  if (amount === undefined || amount === null || isNaN(amount)) return 'MK 0';
  return `MK ${amount.toLocaleString()}`;
}

// A moment in time (for example when a ticket was bought), shown in the
// viewer's own time zone: "24 Oct 2026, 18:00".
export function formatDateTime(iso: string | undefined | null): string {
  if (!iso) return '—';
  const moment = new Date(iso);
  if (Number.isNaN(moment.getTime())) return '—';
  const day = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-GB', {
    day: '2-digit',
    month: 'short',
    year: 'numeric',
  }).format(moment);
  const time = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-GB', {
    hour: '2-digit',
    minute: '2-digit',
    hour12: false,
  }).format(moment);
  return `${day}, ${time}`;
}

// true when the event date is today or later
export function isUpcoming(iso: string): boolean {
  if (!iso) return false;
  const today = new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-CA'); // YYYY-MM-DD
  return iso >= today;
}

FILE: src/features/tickets/ticketImage.test.ts

// @vitest-environment jsdom
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import type { TicketRecord } from '../../lib/tickets'
import {
  TICKET_IMAGE_HEIGHT,
  TICKET_IMAGE_SCALE,
  TICKET_IMAGE_WIDTH,
  renderTicketImage,
  ticketFromRecord,
  ticketFromView,
  ticketImageBlob,
} from './ticketImage'

const sample: TicketRecord = {
  id: 'TM-2026-004871',
  userId: 'u-1',
  eventId: 'lake-of-stars',
  eventName: 'Lake of Stars Live',
  date: '2026-11-14',
  time: '17:00',
  venue: 'Cape Maclear Beach',
  city: 'Mangochi',
  ticketType: 'General',
  price: 35000,
  holder: 'Chimwemwe Banda',
  status: 'valid',
  createdAt: '2026-10-24T16:00:00.000Z',
}

// jsdom has no canvas backend, so provide just enough 2D context for the
// drawing calls plus a fake PNG encoder.
const context = new Proxy(
  {},
  {
    get: () => () => {},
    set: () => true,
  },
) as unknown as CanvasRenderingContext2D

const originalToBlob = HTMLCanvasElement.prototype.toBlob

beforeAll(() => {
  vi.spyOn(HTMLCanvasElement.prototype, 'getContext').mockReturnValue(context)
  Object.defineProperty(HTMLCanvasElement.prototype, 'toBlob', {
    configurable: true,
    value(callback: BlobCallback) {
      callback(new Blob(['fake-png-bytes'], { type: 'image/png' }))
    },
  })
})

afterAll(() => {
  vi.restoreAllMocks()
  if (originalToBlob) {
    Object.defineProperty(HTMLCanvasElement.prototype, 'toBlob', {
      configurable: true,
      value: originalToBlob,
    })
  }
})

describe('shared ticket image', () => {
  it('renders a 2x card for high-DPI screens', () => {
    const canvas = renderTicketImage(ticketFromRecord(sample), null)
    expect(canvas).not.toBeNull()
    expect(canvas?.width).toBe(TICKET_IMAGE_WIDTH * TICKET_IMAGE_SCALE)
    expect(canvas?.height).toBe(TICKET_IMAGE_HEIGHT * TICKET_IMAGE_SCALE)
  })

  it('produces a non-empty PNG blob', async () => {
    const blob = await ticketImageBlob(ticketFromRecord(sample), null)
    expect(blob).not.toBeNull()
    expect(blob?.type).toBe('image/png')
    expect(blob?.size).toBeGreaterThan(0)
  })

  it('normalises the record and view models to the same image data', () => {
    const vipRecord = { ...sample, ticketType: 'VIP' }
    const fromRecord = ticketFromRecord(vipRecord)
    const fromView = ticketFromView({
      id: vipRecord.id,
      eventTitle: vipRecord.eventName,
      venue: vipRecord.venue,
      city: vipRecord.city,
      date: vipRecord.date,
      time: vipRecord.time,
      ticketType: vipRecord.ticketType,
      seat: 'VIP Section',
      price: vipRecord.price,
      holder: vipRecord.holder,
      status: vipRecord.status,
    })

    expect(fromView).toEqual(fromRecord)
  })
})


========================================================================================
FILE: src/features/tickets/ticketImage.ts
========================================================================================
import type { TicketRecord } from '../../lib/tickets'
import type { Ticket, TicketStatus } from './ticketTypes'
import { formatDate, formatMoney } from './ticketFormat'

// One normalised shape used by both call sites (My Tickets and checkout).
export interface TicketImageData {
  id: string
  eventTitle: string
  venue: string
  city: string
  date: string
  time: string
  ticketType: string
  entry: string
  price: number
  holder: string
  status: TicketStatus
}

export function ticketFromRecord(record: TicketRecord): TicketImageData {
  return {
    id: record.id,
    eventTitle: record.eventName,
    venue: record.venue,
    city: record.city,
    date: record.date,
    time: record.time,
    ticketType: record.ticketType,
    entry: `${record.ticketType} Admission`,
    price: record.price,
    holder: record.holder,
    status: record.status,
  }
}

export function ticketFromView(ticket: Ticket): TicketImageData {
  return {
    id: ticket.id,
    eventTitle: ticket.eventTitle,
    venue: ticket.venue,
    city: ticket.city,
    date: ticket.date,
    time: ticket.time,
    ticketType: ticket.ticketType,
    // Keep this identical to ticketFromRecord so both download paths match.
    entry: `${ticket.ticketType} Admission`,
    price: ticket.price,
    holder: ticket.holder,
    status: ticket.status,
  }
}

// Logical size of the ticket card. It is rendered at 2x so the PNG stays
// crisp on phones and high-DPI displays.
export const TICKET_IMAGE_WIDTH = 1000
export const TICKET_IMAGE_HEIGHT = 420
export const TICKET_IMAGE_SCALE = 2

type CanvasFactory = (width: number, height: number) => HTMLCanvasElement

interface RenderOptions {
  scale?: number
  createCanvas?: CanvasFactory
}

function createCanvasElement(width: number, height: number): HTMLCanvasElement {
  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas')
  canvas.width = width
  canvas.height = height
  return canvas
}

function roundRect(
  context: CanvasRenderingContext2D,
  x: number,
  y: number,
  width: number,
  height: number,
  radius: number,
): void {
  context.beginPath()
  context.roundRect(x, y, width, height, radius)
}

export function renderTicketImage(
  ticket: TicketImageData,
  qrCanvas: HTMLCanvasElement | null,
  options: RenderOptions = {},
): HTMLCanvasElement | null {
  const scale = options.scale ?? TICKET_IMAGE_SCALE
  const createCanvas = options.createCanvas ?? createCanvasElement
  const canvas = createCanvas(
    Math.round(TICKET_IMAGE_WIDTH * scale),
    Math.round(TICKET_IMAGE_HEIGHT * scale),
  )
  const context = canvas.getContext('2d')
  if (!context) return null

  // Draw in logical units; the scale handles high-DPI output.
  context.scale(scale, scale)

  // Card background and border
  context.fillStyle = '#ffffff'
  context.fillRect(0, 0, TICKET_IMAGE_WIDTH, TICKET_IMAGE_HEIGHT)
  context.strokeStyle = '#d9e2ef'
  context.lineWidth = 2
  roundRect(context, 1, 1, TICKET_IMAGE_WIDTH - 2, TICKET_IMAGE_HEIGHT - 2, 20)
  context.stroke()

  // Navy header band, clipped to the rounded card
  context.save()
  roundRect(context, 1, 1, TICKET_IMAGE_WIDTH - 2, TICKET_IMAGE_HEIGHT - 2, 20)
  context.clip()
  context.fillStyle = '#12326b'
  context.fillRect(0, 0, TICKET_IMAGE_WIDTH, 70)
  context.restore()

  context.fillStyle = '#ffffff'
  context.font = 'bold 24px sans-serif'
  context.textBaseline = 'middle'
  context.fillText('TICKET MASTER', 32, 36)

  // Status badge
  const valid = ticket.status === 'valid'
  context.fillStyle = valid ? '#dcfce7' : '#e2e8f0'
  roundRect(context, 834, 20, 134, 32, 16)
  context.fill()
  context.fillStyle = valid ? '#15803d' : '#475569'
  context.font = 'bold 16px sans-serif'
  context.textAlign = 'center'
  context.fillText(valid ? 'Valid' : 'Used', 901, 37)
  context.textAlign = 'start'
  context.textBaseline = 'alphabetic'

  // Event title and place
  context.fillStyle = '#0a1f47'
  context.font = 'bold 30px sans-serif'
  context.fillText(ticket.eventTitle, 32, 132, 600)
  context.fillStyle = '#64748b'
  context.font = '20px sans-serif'
  context.fillText(`${ticket.venue}, ${ticket.city}`, 32, 164, 600)

  // Details grid: date/time/ticket then entry/price/holder
  const details: Array<[string, string, number, number, number]> = [
    ['Date', formatDate(ticket.date), 32, 222, 190],
    ['Time', ticket.time, 250, 222, 170],
    ['Ticket', ticket.ticketType, 448, 222, 200],
    ['Entry', ticket.entry, 32, 300, 190],
    ['Price', formatMoney(ticket.price), 250, 300, 170],
    ['Holder', ticket.holder, 448, 300, 200],
  ]

  for (const [label, value, x, y, maxWidth] of details) {
    context.fillStyle = '#64748b'
    context.font = '16px sans-serif'
    context.fillText(label, x, y)
    context.fillStyle = '#0a1f47'
    context.font = 'bold 18px sans-serif'
    context.fillText(value, x, y + 24, maxWidth)
  }

  // QR panel
  context.strokeStyle = '#d9e2ef'
  context.lineWidth = 2
  context.setLineDash([6, 5])
  roundRect(context, 690, 40, 270, 340, 18)
  context.stroke()
  context.setLineDash([])

  if (qrCanvas) {
    context.drawImage(qrCanvas, 735, 66, 180, 180)
  }

  context.textAlign = 'center'
  context.fillStyle = '#0a1f47'
  context.font = 'bold 17px sans-serif'
  context.fillText(ticket.id, 825, 278, 240)
  context.fillStyle = '#64748b'
  context.font = '15px sans-serif'
  context.fillText('Show this code at the entrance', 825, 306, 240)
  context.textAlign = 'start'

  return canvas
}

export function ticketImageBlob(
  ticket: TicketImageData,
  qrCanvas: HTMLCanvasElement | null,
  options: RenderOptions = {},
): Promise<Blob | null> {
  const canvas = renderTicketImage(ticket, qrCanvas, options)
  if (!canvas) return Promise.resolve(null)

  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    canvas.toBlob((blob) => resolve(blob), 'image/png')
  })
}

function qrCanvasFromDataUrl(dataUrl: string): Promise<HTMLCanvasElement | null> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    const image = new Image()
    image.onload = () => {
      const canvas = createCanvasElement(360, 360)
      const context = canvas.getContext('2d')
      if (!context) {
        resolve(null)
        return
      }
      context.fillStyle = '#ffffff'
      context.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height)
      context.drawImage(image, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height)
      resolve(canvas)
    }
    image.onerror = () => resolve(null)
    image.src = dataUrl
  })
}

export async function downloadTicketImage(
  ticket: TicketImageData,
  qrCanvas: HTMLCanvasElement | null,
  qrDataUrl?: string,
): Promise<void> {
  const codeCanvas = qrCanvas ?? (qrDataUrl ? await qrCanvasFromDataUrl(qrDataUrl) : null)
  const blob = await ticketImageBlob(ticket, codeCanvas)
  if (!blob) return

  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob)
  const link = document.createElement('a')
  link.href = url
  link.download = `${ticket.id}.png`
  link.click()
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url)
}

========================================================================================
// @vitest-environment jsdom
import { act } from 'react'
import { createRoot } from 'react-dom/client'
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import type { TicketRecord } from '../../lib/tickets'
import { TicketInfoTable } from './components/TicketInfoTable'
import { formatDateTime } from './ticketFormat'
import { toTicketView } from './ticketData'
import type { Ticket } from './ticketTypes'

;(globalThis as { IS_REACT_ACT_ENVIRONMENT?: boolean }).IS_REACT_ACT_ENVIRONMENT = true

const record: TicketRecord = {
  id: 'TM-2026-K7QX4M2WP9TB',
  userId: 'u-1',
  eventId: 'lake-of-stars',
  eventName: 'Lake of Stars Live',
  date: '2026-11-14',
  time: '17:00',
  venue: 'Cape Maclear Beach',
  city: 'Mangochi',
  ticketType: 'General',
  price: 35000,
  holder: 'Chimwemwe Banda',
  status: 'valid',
  createdAt: '2026-10-24T16:00:00.000Z',
}

function render(ticket: Ticket) {
  const container = document.createElement('div')
  document.body.appendChild(container)
  const root = createRoot(container)
  act(() => root.render(<TicketInfoTable ticket={ticket} />))
  return {
    container,
    unmount: () => {
      act(() => root.unmount())
      container.remove()
    },
  }
}

describe('purchase date and time', () => {
  it('formats a timestamp as date and time', () => {
    expect(formatDateTime('2026-10-24T16:00:00.000Z')).toMatch(/^\d{2} [A-Za-z]{3} 2026, \d{2}:\d{2}$/)
  })

  it('shows a dash for a missing or broken timestamp', () => {
    expect(formatDateTime('')).toBe('—')
    expect(formatDateTime('not a date')).toBe('—')
  })

  it('is carried from the stored ticket into the ticket view', () => {
    expect(toTicketView(record).purchasedAt).toBe('2026-10-24T16:00:00.000Z')
  })
})

describe('TicketInfoTable', () => {
  let cleanup: (() => void) | undefined
  afterEach(() => cleanup?.())

  it('shows the event and ticket details in two tables', () => {
    const { container, unmount } = render(toTicketView(record))
    cleanup = unmount

    expect(container.querySelectorAll('table')).toHaveLength(2)
    const headings = [...container.querySelectorAll('th')].map((th) => th.textContent)
    expect(headings).toEqual(
      expect.arrayContaining(['Event', 'Venue', 'Date', 'Time', 'Ticket ID', 'Ticket type', 'Holder', 'Price', 'Status', 'Purchased']),
    )
  })

  it('shows when the ticket was purchased', () => {
    const { container, unmount } = render(toTicketView(record))
    cleanup = unmount

    const row = [...container.querySelectorAll('tr')].find((tr) => tr.querySelector('th')?.textContent === 'Purchased')
    expect(row?.querySelector('td')?.textContent).toMatch(/^\d{2} [A-Za-z]{3} 2026, \d{2}:\d{2}$/)
  })

  it('shows a dash when the purchase time is unknown', () => {
    const { container, unmount } = render({ ...toTicketView(record), purchasedAt: undefined })
    cleanup = unmount

    const row = [...container.querySelectorAll('tr')].find((tr) => tr.querySelector('th')?.textContent === 'Purchased')
    expect(row?.querySelector('td')?.textContent).toBe('—')
  })
})

export type TicketStatus = 'valid' | 'used' | 'cancelled' | 'refunded';

export type Ticket = {
  id: string;
  eventTitle: string;
  venue: string;
  city: string;
  date: string; // ISO date, e.g. 2026-11-20
  time: string; // 24h, e.g. 09:00
  ticketType: string;
  seat: string;
  price: number; // in MK
  holder: string;
  status: TicketStatus;
  purchasedAt?: string; // ISO timestamp of the purchase
  qrCodeDataUrl?: string; // server-issued QR image, when the backend owns the ticket
};

FILE: src/features/toast/ToastProvider.tsx
========================================================================================
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { ToastContext, type Toast, type ToastVariant } from './useToast';
import { ToastViewport } from './components/ToastViewport';

const AUTO_DISMISS_MS = 4000;

export function ToastProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [toasts, setToasts] = useState<Toast[]>([]);
  const nextId = useRef(1);
  const timeoutIds = useRef<Set<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>>(new Set());

  const dismissToast = useCallback((id: number) => {
    setToasts((current) => current.filter((toast) => toast.id !== id));
  }, []);

  const showToast = useCallback(
    (message: string, variant: ToastVariant = 'info') => {
      const id = nextId.current++;
      setToasts((current) => [...current, { id, message, variant }]);
      const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
        timeoutIds.current.delete(timeoutId);
        dismissToast(id);
      }, AUTO_DISMISS_MS);
      timeoutIds.current.add(timeoutId);
    },
    [dismissToast],
  );

  // clear pending timers when the provider unmounts
  useEffect(() => {
    const timeouts = timeoutIds.current;
    return () => {
      timeouts.forEach((timeoutId) => clearTimeout(timeoutId));
      timeouts.clear();
    };
  }, []);

  return (
    <ToastContext.Provider value={{ toasts, showToast, dismissToast }}>
      {children}
      <ToastViewport toasts={toasts} onDismiss={dismissToast} />
    </ToastContext.Provider>
  );
}

FILE: src/features/toast/components/ToastViewport.tsx
========================================================================================
import type { Toast } from '../useToast';
import styles from './ToastViewport.module.css';

const ICONS: Record<Toast['variant'], string> = {
  success: '✓',
  error: '!',
  info: 'i',
};

export function ToastViewport({
  toasts,
  onDismiss,
}: {
  toasts: Toast[];
  onDismiss: (id: number) => void;
}) {
  if (toasts.length === 0) return null;

  return (
    <div className={styles.viewport} role="region" aria-label="Notifications">
      {toasts.map((toast) => (
        <div
          key={toast.id}
          role="status"
          aria-live="polite"
          className={`${styles.toast} ${styles[toast.variant]}`}
        >
          <span className={styles.icon}>{ICONS[toast.variant]}</span>
          <p className={styles.message}>{toast.message}</p>
          <button
            type="button"
            aria-label="Dismiss notification"
            onClick={() => onDismiss(toast.id)}
            className={styles.close}
          >
            ×
          </button>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/features/toast/useToast.ts
========================================================================================
import { createContext, useContext } from 'react';

export type ToastVariant = 'success' | 'error' | 'info';

export interface Toast {
  id: number;
  message: string;
  variant: ToastVariant;
}

export interface ToastValue {
  toasts: Toast[];
  showToast: (message: string, variant?: ToastVariant) => void;
  dismissToast: (id: number) => void;
}

export const ToastContext = createContext<ToastValue | null>(null);

export function useToast(): ToastValue {
  const value = useContext(ToastContext);
  if (!value) throw new Error('useToast must be used inside <ToastProvider>');
  return value;
}

========================================================================================
FILE: src/features/validation/hooks/useQrScanner.ts
========================================================================================
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'

interface DetectedBarcode {
  rawValue: string
}

interface BarcodeDetectorLike {
  detect: (source: CanvasImageSource) => Promise<DetectedBarcode[]>
}

interface BarcodeDetectorConstructor {
  new (options?: { formats?: string[] }): BarcodeDetectorLike
}

export type QrScannerStatus =
  | 'idle'
  | 'starting'
  | 'scanning'
  | 'unsupported'
  | 'denied'
  | 'error'

const SCAN_INTERVAL_MS = 400
const REPEAT_COOLDOWN_MS = 2500

function getBarcodeDetectorConstructor(): BarcodeDetectorConstructor | undefined {
  return (window as unknown as { BarcodeDetector?: BarcodeDetectorConstructor }).BarcodeDetector
}

export function useQrScanner(onDetected: (code: string) => void) {
  const videoRef = useRef<HTMLVideoElement>(null)
  const streamRef = useRef<MediaStream | null>(null)
  const detectorRef = useRef<BarcodeDetectorLike | null>(null)
  const timerRef = useRef<number | null>(null)
  const lastCodeRef = useRef<{ code: string; at: number }>({ code: '', at: 0 })
  const onDetectedRef = useRef(onDetected)

  const [status, setStatus] = useState<QrScannerStatus>('idle')
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    onDetectedRef.current = onDetected
  }, [onDetected])

  const stop = useCallback(() => {
    if (timerRef.current !== null) {
      window.clearInterval(timerRef.current)
      timerRef.current = null
    }
    if (streamRef.current) {
      streamRef.current.getTracks().forEach((track) => track.stop())
      streamRef.current = null
    }
    if (videoRef.current) {
      videoRef.current.srcObject = null
    }
    detectorRef.current = null
    setStatus('idle')
  }, [])

  const start = useCallback(async () => {
    setError(null)

    const Detector = getBarcodeDetectorConstructor()
    if (!Detector) {
      setStatus('unsupported')
      setError('QR scanning is not supported in this browser. Enter the ticket ID manually below.')
      return
    }
    if (!navigator.mediaDevices?.getUserMedia) {
      setStatus('unsupported')
      setError('Camera access is not available here. Enter the ticket ID manually below.')
      return
    }

    setStatus('starting')
    try {
      const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({
        video: { facingMode: 'environment' },
        audio: false,
      })
      streamRef.current = stream

      const video = videoRef.current
      if (!video) {
        stop()
        return
      }
      video.srcObject = stream
      await video.play()

      detectorRef.current = new Detector({ formats: ['qr_code'] })
      setStatus('scanning')

      timerRef.current = window.setInterval(async () => {
        const currentVideo = videoRef.current
        const detector = detectorRef.current
        if (!currentVideo || !detector || currentVideo.readyState < 2) return
        try {
          const codes = await detector.detect(currentVideo)
          const value = codes[0]?.rawValue?.trim()
          if (!value) return
          const now = Date.now()
          if (value === lastCodeRef.current.code && now - lastCodeRef.current.at < REPEAT_COOLDOWN_MS) {
            return
          }
          lastCodeRef.current = { code: value, at: now }
          onDetectedRef.current(value)
        } catch {
          // Ignore transient decoding errors and keep scanning.
        }
      }, SCAN_INTERVAL_MS)
    } catch (caught) {
      stop()
      const denied = caught instanceof DOMException && caught.name === 'NotAllowedError'
      setStatus(denied ? 'denied' : 'error')
      setError(
        denied
          ? 'Camera permission was denied. Enter the ticket ID manually below.'
          : 'Could not start the camera. Enter the ticket ID manually below.',
      )
    }
  }, [stop])

  useEffect(() => stop, [stop])

  return { videoRef, status, error, start, stop }
}

import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'

vi.mock('../../../lib/backend', () => ({ USE_BACKEND: true, api: vi.fn() }))
vi.mock('../../../lib/backendTickets', async (importOriginal) => {
  const actual = await importOriginal<typeof import('../../../lib/backendTickets')>()
  return { ...actual, validateBackendTicket: vi.fn() }
})

import { validateBackendTicket } from '../../../lib/backendTickets'
import { validateTicket } from './useTicketValidator'

const validate = vi.mocked(validateBackendTicket)

const accepted = {
  accepted: true,
  reason: null,
  ticketId: '10',
  eventId: '4',
  validatedAt: '2026-11-06T10:00:00.000Z',
  details: { ticketNumber: 'TKT-ABC123', ticketType: 'General', eventTitle: 'Lake of Stars' },
}

describe('validateTicket with the backend', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    validate.mockReset()
  })

  it('accepts a backend ticket once and rejects the same ticket on the next scan', async () => {
    validate
      .mockResolvedValueOnce(accepted)
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({
        accepted: false,
        reason: 'ALREADY_USED',
        ticketId: '10',
        eventId: '4',
        validatedAt: null,
        details: accepted.details,
      })

    expect((await validateTicket('qr-token', '4')).status).toBe('valid')
    expect((await validateTicket('qr-token', '4')).status).toBe('used')
    expect(validate).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2)
  })

  it('rejects a ticket for another event', async () => {
    validate.mockResolvedValueOnce({
      accepted: false,
      reason: 'WRONG_EVENT',
      ticketId: '10',
      eventId: '4',
      validatedAt: null,
      details: { ...accepted.details, eventTitle: 'Another event' },
    })

    const result = await validateTicket('qr-token', '9')

    expect(result.status).toBe('wrong_event')
    expect(result.message).toContain('Another event')
  })
})

FILE: src/features/validation/hooks/useTicketValidator.test.ts
========================================================================================
// @vitest-environment jsdom
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { issueTicket, normalizeTicketCode, ticketQrValue } from '../../../lib/tickets'
import { signTicket } from '../../../lib/ticketSignature'
import { validateTicket } from './useTicketValidator'

// validateTicket requires a logged-in staff user who is assigned to the event.
// Seed both the session (tm_user) and the account row (tm_accounts) before each test.
function loginAsStaff(assignedEventIds: string[] = ['event-one', 'lake-of-stars', 'dev-summit']) {
  window.localStorage.setItem(
    'tm_user',
    JSON.stringify({ id: 'u-3', name: 'Gate Staff', email: 'staff@example.com', role: 'staff' }),
  )
  window.localStorage.setItem(
    'tm_accounts',
    JSON.stringify([
      {
        id: 'u-3',
        name: 'Gate Staff',
        email: 'staff@example.com',
        password: 'password123',
        role: 'staff',
        status: 'active',
        organizerId: 'u-2',
        assignedEventIds,
      },
      {
        id: 'u-2',
        name: 'Nyasa Events',
        email: 'organizer@example.com',
        password: 'password123',
        role: 'organizer',
        status: 'active',
      },
    ]),
  )
}

function newTicket(eventId = 'event-one') {
  return issueTicket({
    userId: 'u-test',
    eventId,
    eventName: 'Event One',
    date: '2026-11-14',
    time: '18:00',
    venue: 'Test Venue',
    city: 'Blantyre',
    ticketType: 'General',
    price: 35000,
    holder: 'Test Holder',
  })
}

describe('ticket ids', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    window.localStorage.clear()
    loginAsStaff()
  })

  it('are long, random looking and unique', () => {
    const ids = Array.from({ length: 40 }, () => newTicket().id)
    for (const id of ids) expect(id).toMatch(/^TM-2026-[A-HJ-NP-Z2-9]{12}$/)
    expect(new Set(ids).size).toBe(40)
  })
})

describe('normalizeTicketCode', () => {
  it('accepts the plain, prefixed and signed forms', () => {
    expect(normalizeTicketCode('TM-2026-ABCDEFGHJKLM')).toBe('TM-2026-ABCDEFGHJKLM')
    expect(normalizeTicketCode('ticket:tm-2026-abcdefghjklm')).toBe('TM-2026-ABCDEFGHJKLM')
    expect(normalizeTicketCode('TICKET:TM-2026-ABCDEFGHJKLM.0123456789')).toBe('TM-2026-ABCDEFGHJKLM')
  })
})

describe('validateTicket', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    window.localStorage.clear()
    loginAsStaff()
  })

  it('accepts a ticket scanned from its signed QR code, once', async () => {
    const ticket = newTicket()
    const qr = ticketQrValue(ticket.id)
    expect(qr).toBe('TICKET:' + ticket.id + '.' + signTicket(ticket.id, ticket.eventId))

    expect((await validateTicket(qr, 'event-one')).status).toBe('valid')
    expect((await validateTicket(qr, 'event-one')).status).toBe('used')
  })

  it('rejects a forged tag with a specific message', async () => {
    const ticket = newTicket()
    const result = await validateTicket('TICKET:' + ticket.id + '.0000000000', 'event-one')
    expect(result.status).toBe('invalid')
    expect(result.tampered).toBe(true)
    expect(result.title).toBe('Tampered ticket')
    expect(result.ticket).toBeUndefined()
  })

  it('does not admit the ticket after a forged scan', async () => {
    const ticket = newTicket()
    await validateTicket('TICKET:' + ticket.id + '.0000000000', 'event-one')
    expect((await validateTicket(ticketQrValue(ticket.id), 'event-one')).status).toBe('valid')
  })

  it('rejects another ticket\'s tag on this ticket id', async () => {
    const first = newTicket()
    const second = newTicket()
    const forged = 'TICKET:' + second.id + '.' + signTicket(first.id, first.eventId)
    expect((await validateTicket(forged, 'event-one')).tampered).toBe(true)
  })

  it('rejects a tag that belongs to a different event', async () => {
    const ticket = newTicket('event-one')
    const forged = 'TICKET:' + ticket.id + '.' + signTicket(ticket.id, 'another-event')
    expect((await validateTicket(forged, 'event-one')).tampered).toBe(true)
  })

  it('rejects an altered id that still carries a tag as tampered', async () => {
    const ticket = newTicket()
    const altered = 'TICKET:' + ticket.id.slice(0, -1) + (ticket.id.endsWith('A') ? 'B' : 'A') + '.' + signTicket(ticket.id, ticket.eventId)
    const result = await validateTicket(altered, 'event-one')
    expect(result.status).toBe('invalid')
    expect(result.tampered).toBe(true)
  })

  it('rejects an unknown id as invalid', async () => {
    const result = await validateTicket('TICKET:TM-2026-ZZZZZZZZZZZZ', 'event-one')
    expect(result.status).toBe('invalid')
    expect(result.tampered).toBeUndefined()
    expect(result.title).toBe('Invalid ticket')
  })

  it('still accepts a plain ticket id without a tag (legacy form)', async () => {
    const ticket = newTicket()
    expect((await validateTicket(ticket.id, 'event-one')).status).toBe('valid')
  })

  it('still accepts the seeded demo ticket, plain or from its signed QR code', async () => {
    expect((await validateTicket('TM-2026-K7QX4ZP9R2WM', 'lake-of-stars')).status).toBe('valid')
    expect((await validateTicket(ticketQrValue('TM-2026-H3VN8DC5T6YB'), 'dev-summit')).status).toBe('valid')
  })
})

import type { TicketValidationResult } from '../../../lib/tickets'
import { backendValidationToResult, validateBackendTicket } from '../../../lib/backendTickets'

export async function validateTicket(rawCode: string, eventId: string): Promise<TicketValidationResult> {
  const response = await validateBackendTicket(rawCode, eventId)
  return backendValidationToResult(response)
}

export { ScannerPage } from './pages/ScannerPage'
export { validateTicket } from './hooks/useTicketValidator'
export { useQrScanner } from './hooks/useQrScanner'
