========================================================================================
FILE: src/features/validation/pages/ScannerPage.tsx
========================================================================================
import { useCallback, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import type { FormEvent, ReactNode } from 'react'
import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import type { TicketValidationResult } from '../../../lib/tickets'
import { useEvents } from '../../events/hooks/useEvents'
import { ScanResult } from '../components/ScanResult'
import { useQrScanner } from '../hooks/useQrScanner'
import { validateTicket } from '../hooks/useTicketValidator'

export function ScannerPage(): ReactNode {
  const [result, setResult] = useState<TicketValidationResult | null>(null)
  const [history, setHistory] = useState<TicketValidationResult[]>([])
  const [manualCode, setManualCode] = useState('')
  const [checking, setChecking] = useState(false)
  const checkingRef = useRef(false)
  const queryClient = useQueryClient()
  const { data: backendEvents = [], isLoading: loadingEvents } = useEvents()
  const events = backendEvents
  const [selectedEventId, setSelectedEventId] = useState(events[0]?.id ?? '')
  const selectedEvent = events.find((e) => e.id === selectedEventId) ?? events[0]
  const activeEventId = selectedEvent?.id ?? ''

  const handleCode = useCallback(
    async (rawCode: string) => {
      const outcome = await (async (): Promise<TicketValidationResult> => {
        if (!activeEventId) {
          return {
            status: 'invalid',
            title: 'No event selected',
            message: 'Choose the event you are checking tickets for.',
          }
        }
        try {
          return await validateTicket(rawCode, activeEventId)
        } catch (caught) {
          return {
            status: 'invalid',
            title: 'Could not check ticket',
            message: caught instanceof Error ? caught.message : 'The ticket could not be checked. Try again.',
          }
        }
      })()

      setResult(outcome)
      setHistory((previous) => [outcome, ...previous].slice(0, 6))
      setManualCode('')
      if (outcome.status === 'valid') {
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['event-stats'] })
      }
    },
    [queryClient, activeEventId],
  )

  const checkCode = useCallback(
    (rawCode: string) => {
      if (checkingRef.current) return
      checkingRef.current = true
      setChecking(true)
      void handleCode(rawCode).finally(() => {
        checkingRef.current = false
        setChecking(false)
      })
    },
    [handleCode],
  )

  const { videoRef, status, error, start, stop } = useQrScanner(checkCode)

  function handleManualSubmit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault()
    checkCode(manualCode)
  }

  const checkedIn = history.filter((item) => item.status === 'valid').length

  const scanning = status === 'scanning'

  if (!selectedEvent && loadingEvents) {
    return (
      <div>
        <h1>Scan tickets</h1>
        <div className="alert info" style={{ marginTop: 14 }}>Loading events…</div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  if (!selectedEvent) {
    return (
      <div>
        <h1>Scan tickets</h1>
        <div className="alert info" style={{ marginTop: 14 }}>
          No events are assigned to you yet. Ask your organizer to assign you to an event.
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="row">
        <div>
          <h1>Scan tickets</h1>
          <p className="m">{selectedEvent.venue}</p>
          <label className="m" style={{ display: 'block', marginTop: 8 }}>
            Event gate
            <select
              className="in"
              style={{ marginTop: 4 }}
              value={selectedEvent.id}
              onChange={(event) => {
                setSelectedEventId(event.target.value)
                setResult(null)
                setHistory([])
              }}
            >
              {events.map((event) => (
                <option key={event.id} value={event.id}>
                  {event.title}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </label>
        </div>
        <div className="card stat" style={{ minWidth: 160 }}>
          <span className="m">Checked in this session</span>
          <b>{checkedIn}</b>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="split" style={{ marginTop: 14 }}>
        <div className="g">
          <div className="cam">
            <video ref={videoRef} playsInline muted className="cam-video" />
            {!scanning && (
              <div className="cam-hint">
                <div className="cam-frame" />
                <p>Point the camera at a ticket QR code to validate it.</p>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="row" style={{ gap: 10 }}>
            {scanning ? (
              <button type="button" className="btn o" onClick={stop}>
                Stop camera
              </button>
            ) : (
              <button
                type="button"
                className="btn"
                onClick={start}
                disabled={status === 'starting' || checking}
              >
                {status === 'starting' ? 'Starting camera…' : 'Start camera'}
              </button>
            )}
          </div>

          {error && <div className="alert error">{error}</div>}

          <form className="row" style={{ gap: 8 }} onSubmit={handleManualSubmit}>
            <input
              className="in"
              style={{ margin: 0 }}
              placeholder="Paste or scan the ticket QR token"
              value={manualCode}
              onChange={(event) => setManualCode(event.target.value)}
            />
            <button type="submit" className="btn" disabled={!manualCode.trim() || checking}>
              {checking ? 'Checking…' : 'Validate'}
            </button>
          </form>

          <div className="alert info">
            <>
              <b>Server-checked tickets</b>
              <p className="m">The backend records each scan, so a ticket is accepted only once.</p>
            </>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div>
          {result ? (
            <ScanResult result={result} />
          ) : (
            <div className="alert info">Scan or enter a ticket to see the validation result.</div>
          )}

          <h4>Other results</h4>
          {history.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="m">No scans yet.</p>
          ) : (
            history.map((item, index) => (
              <ScanResult key={`${item.status}-${index}`} result={item} compact />
            ))
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

FILE: src/lib/adminUsers.ts
========================================================================================
// lib/adminUsers.ts
// User management for administrators. Every function:
//   1. verifies the caller is an administrator,
//   2. validates the request,
//   3. writes the audit record,
//   4. only then changes the data.
import { assertAdmin, recordAudit, type AuditActor } from './audit'
import { listAllKYC } from './verification'

export type UserRole = 'customer' | 'organizer' | 'staff' | 'admin'
export const USER_ROLES: UserRole[] = ['customer', 'organizer', 'staff', 'admin']

/** Shape of the accounts your auth store knows about. */
export interface AccountLike {
  id: string
  name: string
  email: string
  role: string
}

export interface ManagedUser {
  id: string
  name: string
  email: string
  role: UserRole
  disabled: boolean
  createdAt: string
}

const STORAGE_KEY = 'admin_managed_users_v1'

function normalizeRole(value: unknown): UserRole {
  const v = String(value ?? '').toLowerCase()
  return v === 'admin' || v === 'organizer' || v === 'staff' ? v : 'customer'
}

function readStore(): ManagedUser[] | null {
  try {
    const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY)
    if (!raw) return null
    const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(raw)
    return Array.isArray(parsed) ? (parsed as ManagedUser[]) : null
  } catch {
    return null
  }
}

function writeStore(users: ManagedUser[]): void {
  window.localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(users))
}

const DELETED_KEY = 'admin_deleted_ids_v1'

function readDeleted(): string[] {
  try {
    const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(window.localStorage.getItem(DELETED_KEY) ?? '[]')
    return Array.isArray(parsed) ? (parsed as string[]) : []
  } catch {
    return []
  }
}

/** First run: start from organizer KYC records. Real accounts are merged in by load(). */
function seedFromKyc(): ManagedUser[] {
  const now = new Date().toISOString()
  return listAllKYC().map((kyc) => ({
    id: String(kyc.userId),
    name: kyc.businessName,
    email: '',
    role: 'organizer' as UserRole,
    disabled: false,
    createdAt: now,
  }))
}

function load(actor: AuditActor, accounts: AccountLike[] = []): ManagedUser[] {
  let users = readStore() ?? seedFromKyc()
  const deleted = readDeleted()
  const now = new Date().toISOString()
  // Merge in real accounts (and fill in names/emails for placeholder records).
  for (const acc of accounts) {
    const id = String(acc.id)
    if (deleted.includes(id)) continue
    const existing = users.find((u) => u.id === id)
    if (!existing) {
      users = [...users, { id, name: acc.name, email: acc.email, role: normalizeRole(acc.role), disabled: false, createdAt: now }]
    } else if (!existing.email) {
      users = users.map((u) => (u.id === id ? { ...u, name: acc.name, email: acc.email } : u))
    }
  }
  // The verified administrator must always exist in the list (needed for last-admin protection).
  if (!users.some((u) => u.id === String(actor.id))) {
    users = [...users, { id: String(actor.id), name: actor.name, email: '', role: 'admin', disabled: false, createdAt: now }]
  }
  writeStore(users)
  return users
}

function activeAdminCount(users: ManagedUser[]): number {
  return users.filter((u) => u.role === 'admin' && !u.disabled).length
}

function requireTarget(users: ManagedUser[], userId: string): ManagedUser {
  const target = users.find((u) => u.id === userId)
  if (!target) throw new Error('User not found.')
  return target
}

// ---------- public API ----------

export function listUsers(actor: AuditActor | null | undefined, accounts: AccountLike[] = []): ManagedUser[] {
  assertAdmin(actor)
  return load(actor, accounts)
}

/** Use this in your login flow so disabled accounts cannot sign in. */
export function isUserDisabled(userId: string): boolean {
  const users = readStore()
  return !!users?.find((u) => u.id === String(userId))?.disabled
}

export function setUserDisabled(actor: AuditActor | null | undefined, userId: string, disabled: boolean): void {
  assertAdmin(actor)
  const users = load(actor)
  const target = requireTarget(users, userId)
  if (target.id === String(actor.id)) throw new Error('You cannot change your own account status.')
  if (disabled && target.role === 'admin' && activeAdminCount(users) <= 1) {
    throw new Error('You cannot disable the last active administrator.')
  }
  recordAudit(actor, {
    action: disabled ? 'USER_DISABLED' : 'USER_ENABLED',
    targetType: 'user',
    targetId: target.id,
    targetLabel: target.name,
  })
  writeStore(users.map((u) => (u.id === target.id ? { ...u, disabled } : u)))
}

export function deleteUser(actor: AuditActor | null | undefined, userId: string): void {
  assertAdmin(actor)
  const users = load(actor)
  const target = requireTarget(users, userId)
  if (target.id === String(actor.id)) throw new Error('You cannot delete your own account.')
  if (target.role === 'admin' && activeAdminCount(users) <= 1) {
    throw new Error('You cannot delete the last active administrator.')
  }
  recordAudit(actor, {
    action: 'USER_DELETED',
    targetType: 'user',
    targetId: target.id,
    targetLabel: target.name,
    details: `Role at deletion: ${target.role}`,
  })
  window.localStorage.setItem(DELETED_KEY, JSON.stringify([...readDeleted(), target.id]))
  writeStore(users.filter((u) => u.id !== target.id))
}

export function changeUserRole(actor: AuditActor | null | undefined, userId: string, newRole: UserRole): void {
  assertAdmin(actor)
  if (!USER_ROLES.includes(newRole)) throw new Error('Invalid role.')
  const users = load(actor)
  const target = requireTarget(users, userId)
  if (target.id === String(actor.id)) throw new Error('You cannot change your own role.')
  if (target.role === newRole) return
  if (target.role === 'admin' && newRole !== 'admin' && activeAdminCount(users) <= 1) {
    throw new Error('You cannot remove the last active administrator.')
  }
  recordAudit(actor, {
    action: 'ROLE_CHANGED',
    targetType: 'user',
    targetId: target.id,
    targetLabel: target.name,
    details: `${target.role} → ${newRole}`,
  })
  writeStore(users.map((u) => (u.id === target.id ? { ...u, role: newRole } : u)))
}

export function createAdmin(actor: AuditActor | null | undefined, input: { name: string; email: string }): void {
  assertAdmin(actor)
  const name = input.name.trim()
  const email = input.email.trim().toLowerCase()
  if (!name) throw new Error('Name is required.')
  if (!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email)) throw new Error('Enter a valid email address.')
  const users = load(actor)
  if (users.some((u) => u.email.toLowerCase() === email)) throw new Error('A user with this email already exists.')
  const id = `admin-${Date.now()}`
  recordAudit(actor, {
    action: 'ADMIN_CREATED',
    targetType: 'user',
    targetId: id,
    targetLabel: name,
    details: email,
  })
  writeStore([...users, { id, name, email, role: 'admin', disabled: false, createdAt: new Date().toISOString() }])
}

import { QueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';

export const queryClient = new QueryClient({
  defaultOptions: {
    queries: { staleTime: 60_000, refetchOnWindowFocus: false },
  },
});

FILE: src/lib/audit.ts

// lib/audit.ts
// Audit log. Only administrators can read or write it.
// Records can never be edited. Administrators may delete records, but every deletion is itself
// recorded (who, when, how many) and those deletion records can never be deleted.

export type AuditAction =
  | 'USER_DISABLED'
  | 'USER_ENABLED'
  | 'USER_DELETED'
  | 'ROLE_CHANGED'
  | 'ADMIN_CREATED'
  | 'KYC_VERIFIED'
  | 'KYC_REJECTED'
  | 'EVENT_APPROVED'
  | 'EVENT_REJECTED'
  | 'EVENT_UNPUBLISHED'
  | 'ACCESS_DENIED'
  | 'LOGS_DELETED'

export type AuditTargetType = 'user' | 'event' | 'organizer' | 'system'

export interface AuditActor {
  id: string
  name: string
  role: string
}

export interface AuditEntry {
  id: string
  timestamp: string // ISO 8601
  actorId: string
  actorName: string
  action: AuditAction
  targetType: AuditTargetType
  targetId: string
  targetLabel: string
  details: string
  prevHash: string
  hash: string
}

export interface AuditInput {
  action: AuditAction
  targetType: AuditTargetType
  targetId: string
  targetLabel: string
  details?: string
}

const STORAGE_KEY = 'audit_log_v1'
const GENESIS_HASH = '0'.repeat(14)

export const AUDIT_ACTION_LABELS: Record<AuditAction, string> = {
  USER_DISABLED: 'Account disabled',
  USER_ENABLED: 'Account enabled',
  USER_DELETED: 'User deleted',
  ROLE_CHANGED: 'Role changed',
  ADMIN_CREATED: 'Administrator created',
  KYC_VERIFIED: 'Organizer verified',
  KYC_REJECTED: 'Organizer rejected',
  EVENT_APPROVED: 'Event approved',
  EVENT_REJECTED: 'Event rejected',
  EVENT_UNPUBLISHED: 'Event unpublished',
  ACCESS_DENIED: 'Access denied',
  LOGS_DELETED: 'Audit records deleted',
}

// ---------- helpers ----------

function hashString(input: string): string {
  let h1 = 0xdeadbeef
  let h2 = 0x41c6ce57
  for (let i = 0; i < input.length; i++) {
    const ch = input.charCodeAt(i)
    h1 = Math.imul(h1 ^ ch, 2654435761)
    h2 = Math.imul(h2 ^ ch, 1597334677)
  }
  h1 = Math.imul(h1 ^ (h1 >>> 16), 2246822507) ^ Math.imul(h2 ^ (h2 >>> 13), 3266489909)
  h2 = Math.imul(h2 ^ (h2 >>> 16), 2246822507) ^ Math.imul(h1 ^ (h1 >>> 13), 3266489909)
  return (4294967296 * (2097151 & h2) + (h1 >>> 0)).toString(16).padStart(14, '0')
}

function computeHash(entry: Omit<AuditEntry, 'hash'>): string {
  return hashString(
    [
      entry.id,
      entry.timestamp,
      entry.actorId,
      entry.actorName,
      entry.action,
      entry.targetType,
      entry.targetId,
      entry.targetLabel,
      entry.details,
      entry.prevHash,
    ].join('|'),
  )
}

function newId(): string {
  if (typeof crypto !== 'undefined' && typeof crypto.randomUUID === 'function') {
    return crypto.randomUUID()
  }
  return `${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10)}`
}

function readAll(): AuditEntry[] {
  try {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined') return []
    const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY)
    if (!raw) return []
    const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(raw)
    return Array.isArray(parsed) ? (parsed as AuditEntry[]) : []
  } catch {
    return []
  }
}

function writeAll(entries: AuditEntry[]): void {
  window.localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(entries))
}

function append(actor: AuditActor, input: AuditInput): AuditEntry {
  const entries = readAll()
  const prevHash = entries.length > 0 ? entries[entries.length - 1].hash : GENESIS_HASH
  const base: Omit<AuditEntry, 'hash'> = {
    id: newId(),
    timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
    actorId: String(actor.id),
    actorName: actor.name,
    action: input.action,
    targetType: input.targetType,
    targetId: String(input.targetId),
    targetLabel: input.targetLabel,
    details: input.details ?? '',
    prevHash,
  }
  const entry: AuditEntry = { ...base, hash: computeHash(base) }
  writeAll([...entries, entry])
  return entry
}

// ---------- public API ----------

export function isAdmin(actor: AuditActor | null | undefined): actor is AuditActor {
  return !!actor && String(actor.role).toLowerCase() === 'admin'
}

export function assertAdmin(actor: AuditActor | null | undefined): asserts actor is AuditActor {
  if (!isAdmin(actor)) {
    throw new Error('Access denied: administrator privileges are required.')
  }
}

/**
 * Reads the signed-in user from the app's stored session.
 * Matches authStore.ts (key 'tm_user'). The dashboard itself uses useAuth().
 */
export function getCurrentActor(): AuditActor | null {
  try {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined') return null
    const keys = ['tm_user']
    for (const key of keys) {
      const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(key)
      if (!raw) continue
      const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as Record<string, unknown>
      const candidate = (parsed && typeof parsed === 'object' && parsed.user && typeof parsed.user === 'object'
        ? parsed.user
        : parsed) as Record<string, unknown>
      const id = candidate.id ?? candidate.userId ?? candidate.uid ?? candidate.email
      const role = candidate.role
      if (id && typeof role === 'string') {
        const name = (candidate.name ?? candidate.fullName ?? candidate.email ?? 'Administrator') as string
        return { id: String(id), name: String(name), role }
      }
    }
  } catch {
    // ignore malformed session data
  }
  return null
}

/** Record an administrative action. Throws if the caller is not an administrator. */
export function recordAudit(actor: AuditActor | null | undefined, input: AuditInput): AuditEntry {
  assertAdmin(actor)
  return append(actor, input)
}

/** Record that a non-admin tried to reach an admin-only resource. */
export function recordDeniedAccess(actor: AuditActor, resource: string): void {
  try {
    append(actor, {
      action: 'ACCESS_DENIED',
      targetType: 'system',
      targetId: resource,
      targetLabel: resource,
      details: `Attempted access with role "${actor.role}"`,
    })
  } catch {
    // never let logging break the page
  }
}

/** Newest first. Ordinary users get nothing: this throws for non-admins. */
export function listAuditLogs(actor: AuditActor | null | undefined): AuditEntry[] {
  assertAdmin(actor)
  return readAll().slice().reverse()
}

/** Checks the hash chain. Returns the id of the first altered record, or null if intact. */
export function verifyAuditLog(actor: AuditActor | null | undefined): { intact: boolean; brokenAt: string | null } {
  assertAdmin(actor)
  const entries = readAll()
  let prev = GENESIS_HASH
  for (const entry of entries) {
    const { hash, ...rest } = entry
    if (entry.prevHash !== prev || computeHash(rest) !== hash) {
      return { intact: false, brokenAt: entry.id }
    }
    prev = hash
  }
  return { intact: true, brokenAt: null }
}

/**
 * Delete audit records by id (single, a week, a month, or everything).
 * - Admin only.
 * - Records of type LOGS_DELETED are protected and are never removed.
 * - The remaining records are re-chained so integrity checks still pass.
 * - The deletion itself is written to the log (who, when, how many, which scope).
 * Returns the number of records removed.
 */
export function deleteAuditEntries(actor: AuditActor | null | undefined, ids: string[], scope: string): number {
  assertAdmin(actor)
  const idSet = new Set(ids)
  const all = readAll()
  const remaining = all.filter((e) => !(idSet.has(e.id) && e.action !== 'LOGS_DELETED'))
  const removed = all.length - remaining.length
  if (removed === 0) return 0

  let prev = GENESIS_HASH
  const rechained: AuditEntry[] = remaining.map((e) => {
    const base: Omit<AuditEntry, 'hash'> = {
      id: e.id,
      timestamp: e.timestamp,
      actorId: e.actorId,
      actorName: e.actorName,
      action: e.action,
      targetType: e.targetType,
      targetId: e.targetId,
      targetLabel: e.targetLabel,
      details: e.details,
      prevHash: prev,
    }
    const hash = computeHash(base)
    prev = hash
    return { ...base, hash }
  })
  writeAll(rechained)

  append(actor, {
    action: 'LOGS_DELETED',
    targetType: 'system',
    targetId: 'audit-log',
    targetLabel: 'Audit Log',
    details: `Deleted ${removed} record${removed === 1 ? '' : 's'}: ${scope}`,
  })
  return removed
}

FILE: src/lib/backend.ts
========================================================================================
// Application data always comes from the API; PayChangu keeps its own pending payment state.
export const USE_BACKEND = true

// The dev server forwards /be/* to the backend (see vite.config.ts), so the browser
// never calls port 3000 directly and there is no CORS problem.
export const API = '/be'

const TOKEN_KEY = 'tm_token'
export const NO_SERVER = 'Cannot reach the server. Is the backend running?'

export function getToken(): string | null {
  try {
    return localStorage.getItem(TOKEN_KEY)
  } catch {
    return null
  }
}

export function setToken(token: string | null): void {
  try {
    if (token) localStorage.setItem(TOKEN_KEY, token)
    else localStorage.removeItem(TOKEN_KEY)
  } catch {
    // storage unavailable: the user simply has to log in again
  }
}

function messageFrom(status: number, data: unknown): string {
  const message = data && typeof data === 'object' && 'message' in data ? (data as { message: unknown }).message : null
  if (status >= 500) return NO_SERVER
  if (Array.isArray(message)) return message.join('. ')
  if (typeof message === 'string') return message
  return `Request failed (${status})`
}

// JSON request to the backend. Adds the login token when there is one.
export async function api<T>(path: string, options: { method?: string; body?: unknown } = {}): Promise<T> {
  const headers: Record<string, string> = {}
  const token = getToken()
  if (token) headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`
  if (options.body !== undefined) headers['Content-Type'] = 'application/json'

  let response: Response
  try {
    response = await fetch(API + path, {
      method: options.method ?? 'GET',
      headers,
      body: options.body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(options.body),
    })
  } catch {
    throw new Error(NO_SERVER)
  }

  let data: unknown = null
  try {
    data = await response.json()
  } catch {
    data = null
  }
  if (!response.ok) {
    const error = new Error(messageFrom(response.status, data)) as Error & { status: number }
    error.status = response.status
    throw error
  }
  return data as T
}


========================================================================================
FILE: src/lib/backendEvents.ts
========================================================================================
import { api } from './backend'
import type { StoredEvent } from './events'
import type { TicketType } from '../types/event'

// Published events from the backend, converted to the shape the pages already use.
const KNOWN_CITIES = ['Lilongwe', 'Blantyre', 'Mzuzu', 'Zomba', 'Mangochi', 'Salima']
const IMAGES = ['/images/events/event-1.webp', '/images/events/event-2.jpg', '/images/events/event-3.webp']

interface BackendEvent {
  id: string
  title: string
  description: string
  venue: string
  location: string
  startAt: string
  status?: 'DRAFT' | 'PUBLISHED' | 'CANCELLED'
}

interface BackendTicketType {
  ticketTypeId: string
  name: string
  price: number | string
  quantity: number
  remaining: number
}

interface Page {
  data: BackendEvent[]
  meta: { totalPages: number }
}

function toTicketType(type: BackendTicketType): TicketType {
  return {
    id: String(type.ticketTypeId),
    name: type.name,
    price: Number(type.price),
    capacity: type.quantity,
    sold: Math.max(0, type.quantity - type.remaining),
  }
}

// The backend has no category, image or organizer name yet.
function toStoredEvent(event: BackendEvent, ticketTypes: BackendTicketType[]): StoredEvent {
  const start = new Date(event.startAt)
  const location = event.location ?? ''
  return {
    id: String(event.id),
    title: event.title,
    description: event.description,
    category: '',
    city: KNOWN_CITIES.find((city) => location.toLowerCase().includes(city.toLowerCase())) ?? location,
    venue: event.venue,
    date: start.toLocaleDateString('en-CA'),
    time: start.toLocaleTimeString('en-GB', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }),
    organizer: '',
    image: IMAGES[(Number(event.id) || 0) % IMAGES.length],
    status: event.status === 'DRAFT' ? 'Draft' : event.status === 'CANCELLED' ? 'Cancelled' : 'Published',
    ticketTypes: ticketTypes.map(toTicketType),
  }
}

// All published events with their ticket types.
export async function fetchPublishedEvents(): Promise<StoredEvent[]> {
  const events: BackendEvent[] = []
  let page = 1
  let totalPages = 1
  do {
    const result = await api<Page>(`/events?page=${page}&limit=50`)
    events.push(...result.data)
    totalPages = result.meta.totalPages
    page += 1
  } while (page <= totalPages)

  return Promise.all(
    events.map(async (event) => toStoredEvent(event, await api<BackendTicketType[]>(`/events/${event.id}/ticket-types`))),
  )
}

export async function fetchBackendEvent(eventId: string): Promise<StoredEvent> {
  const event = await api<BackendEvent>(`/events/${encodeURIComponent(eventId)}`)
  const ticketTypes = await api<BackendTicketType[]>(`/events/${encodeURIComponent(eventId)}/ticket-types`)
  return toStoredEvent(event, ticketTypes)
}

import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import {
  cancelOrganizerEvent,
  createOrganizerEvent,
  formatEventDate,
  listMyEvents,
  publishEvent,
  totalCapacity,
  type NewEventInput,
} from './backendOrganizer'

type Route = (url: string, init?: RequestInit) => unknown

function stubBackend(route: Route) {
  const calls: { url: string; method: string; body: unknown }[] = []
  const fetchMock = vi.fn(async (url: string, init?: RequestInit) => {
    calls.push({ url, method: init?.method ?? 'GET', body: init?.body ? JSON.parse(String(init.body)) : undefined })
    const result = route(url, init)
    if (result instanceof Response) return result
    return new Response(JSON.stringify(result), { status: 200, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })
  })
  vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchMock)
  return calls
}

const INPUT: NewEventInput = {
  name: ' Sample Gig ',
  description: 'A sample event',
  venue: 'Bingu',
  location: 'Lilongwe, Malawi',
  startAt: '2026-12-20T18:00:00.000Z',
  endAt: '2026-12-20T22:00:00.000Z',
  ticketTypes: [
    { name: 'General', price: 5000, quantity: 80 },
    { name: 'VIP', price: 15000, quantity: 20 },
  ],
  status: 'Published',
}

afterEach(() => {
  vi.unstubAllGlobals()
})

describe('totalCapacity', () => {
  it('adds up the ticket types', () => {
    expect(totalCapacity(INPUT.ticketTypes)).toBe(100)
  })
})

describe('formatEventDate', () => {
  it('returns the original text when the date is not valid', () => {
    expect(formatEventDate('not a date')).toBe('not a date')
  })
})

describe('createOrganizerEvent', () => {
  it('creates the event, then publishes it, then generates its tickets', async () => {
    const calls = stubBackend((url) => (url.endsWith('/events') ? { id: '7' } : {}))
    const result = await createOrganizerEvent(INPUT)
    expect(result.id).toBe('7')
    expect(calls.map((call) => `${call.method} ${call.url}`)).toEqual([
      'POST /be/events',
      'POST /be/events/7/publish',
      'POST /be/tickets/events/7/generate',
    ])
    expect(calls[0].body).toMatchObject({
      title: 'Sample Gig',
      capacity: 100,
      ticketTypes: [
        { name: 'General', price: 5000, currency: 'MWK', quantity: 80, perOrderLimit: 10 },
        { name: 'VIP', price: 15000, currency: 'MWK', quantity: 20, perOrderLimit: 10 },
      ],
    })
  })

  it('only saves a draft when the status is Draft', async () => {
    const calls = stubBackend(() => ({ id: '8' }))
    await createOrganizerEvent({ ...INPUT, status: 'Draft' })
    expect(calls).toHaveLength(1)
  })
})

describe('publishEvent', () => {
  it('tells the organizer to retry when tickets could not be generated', async () => {
    stubBackend((url) =>
      url.includes('/generate')
        ? new Response(JSON.stringify({ message: 'event has no active ticket types' }), { status: 409 })
        : {},
    )
    await expect(publishEvent('7')).rejects.toThrow(/published but its tickets could not be created/)
  })
})

describe('listMyEvents', () => {
  it('maps backend events and reads tickets sold from the sales summary', async () => {
    stubBackend((url) => {
      if (url.includes('/events/mine')) {
        return {
          data: [
            { id: '1', title: 'One', venue: 'A', startAt: '2026-12-20T18:00:00.000Z', status: 'PUBLISHED' },
            { id: '2', title: 'Two', venue: 'B', startAt: '2026-12-21T18:00:00.000Z', status: 'DRAFT' },
            { id: '3', title: 'Three', venue: 'C', startAt: '2026-12-22T18:00:00.000Z', status: 'CANCELLED' },
          ],
          meta: { totalPages: 1 },
        }
      }
      if (url.includes('/events/1/')) return { totals: { sold: 12 } }
      return new Response('{}', { status: 500 })
    })
    const events = await listMyEvents()
    expect(events.map((event) => [event.name, event.status, event.ticketsSold])).toEqual([
      ['One', 'Published', 12],
      ['Two', 'Draft', 0],
      ['Three', 'Cancelled', 0],
    ])
  })
})

describe('cancelOrganizerEvent', () => {
  it('sends a DELETE for the event', async () => {
    const calls = stubBackend(() => ({}))
    await cancelOrganizerEvent('7')
    expect(`${calls[0].method} ${calls[0].url}`).toBe('DELETE /be/events/7')
  })
})

FILE: src/lib/backendOrganizer.ts
========================================================================================
import { api } from './backend'
import type { EventItem } from '../features/organizer/Data/Events'

// Organizer side of the backend: create, publish, cancel and list the organizer's own events.

export interface NewTicketType {
  name: string
  price: number
  quantity: number
  perOrderLimit?: number
}

export interface NewEventInput {
  name: string
  description: string
  venue: string
  location: string
  startAt: string // ISO date-time
  endAt: string // ISO date-time
  ticketTypes: NewTicketType[]
  status: 'Published' | 'Draft'
}

interface BackendMyEvent {
  id: string
  title: string
  venue: string
  startAt: string
  status: 'DRAFT' | 'PUBLISHED' | 'CANCELLED'
}

interface MyEventsPage {
  data: BackendMyEvent[]
  meta: { totalPages: number }
}

interface SalesSummary {
  totals: { sold: number }
}

const STATUS: Record<BackendMyEvent['status'], EventItem['status']> = {
  DRAFT: 'Draft',
  PUBLISHED: 'Published',
  CANCELLED: 'Cancelled',
}

// "10 May 2026 • 19:00", the same style the dashboard table already shows
export function formatEventDate(iso: string): string {
  const date = new Date(iso)
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return iso
  const day = date.toLocaleDateString('en-GB', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' })
  const time = date.toLocaleTimeString('en-GB', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })
  return `${day} • ${time}`
}

// The backend wants the total capacity as well. It is simply the sum of the ticket types.
export function totalCapacity(ticketTypes: NewTicketType[]): number {
  return ticketTypes.reduce((sum, type) => sum + type.quantity, 0)
}

async function soldFor(eventId: string): Promise<number> {
  const summary = await api<SalesSummary>(`/events/${eventId}/sales-summary`)
  return summary.totals.sold
}

// Every event of the logged-in organizer, in all statuses, with tickets sold.
export async function listMyEvents(): Promise<EventItem[]> {
  const events: BackendMyEvent[] = []
  let page = 1
  let totalPages = 1
  do {
    const result = await api<MyEventsPage>(`/events/mine?page=${page}&limit=50`)
    events.push(...result.data)
    totalPages = result.meta.totalPages
    page += 1
  } while (page <= totalPages)

  return Promise.all(
    events.map(async (event) => ({
      id: String(event.id),
      name: event.title,
      date: formatEventDate(event.startAt),
      venue: event.venue,
      ticketsSold: await soldFor(event.id),
      status: STATUS[event.status] ?? 'Draft',
    })),
  )
}

// Publishing makes the event public. Tickets only exist after "generate", so both run together.
// Generating again is safe: it only creates tickets that are still missing.
export async function publishEvent(eventId: string): Promise<void> {
  await api(`/events/${eventId}/publish`, { method: 'POST' })
  try {
    await api(`/tickets/events/${eventId}/generate`, { method: 'POST' })
  } catch (error) {
    const reason = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'unknown error'
    throw new Error(`The event is published but its tickets could not be created (${reason}). Press Publish again to retry.`)
  }
}

export async function createOrganizerEvent(input: NewEventInput): Promise<{ id: string }> {
  const created = await api<{ id: string }>('/events', {
    method: 'POST',
    body: {
      title: input.name.trim(),
      description: input.description.trim(),
      venue: input.venue.trim(),
      location: input.location.trim(),
      startAt: input.startAt,
      endAt: input.endAt,
      capacity: totalCapacity(input.ticketTypes),
      ticketTypes: input.ticketTypes.map((type) => ({
        name: type.name.trim(),
        price: type.price,
        currency: 'MWK',
        quantity: type.quantity,
        perOrderLimit: type.perOrderLimit ?? 10,
      })),
    },
  })
  if (input.status === 'Published') await publishEvent(created.id)
  return { id: String(created.id) }
}

export async function cancelOrganizerEvent(eventId: string): Promise<void> {
  await api(`/events/${eventId}`, { method: 'DELETE' })
}

import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import {
  backendTicketToRecord,
  backendValidationToResult,
  extractQrToken,
  validateBackendTicket,
} from './backendTickets'
import type { Event, TicketType } from '../types/event'

const TOKEN = '3d594650-3f1c-4c8d-9e4a-1b2c3d4e5f60'

function stubResponse(body: unknown) {
  const fetchMock = vi.fn(async () => new Response(JSON.stringify(body), {
    status: 200,
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
  }))
  vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchMock)
  return fetchMock
}

afterEach(() => {
  vi.unstubAllGlobals()
})

describe('extractQrToken', () => {
  it('accepts the raw backend UUID and the legacy signed form', () => {
    expect(extractQrToken(TOKEN)).toBe(TOKEN)
    expect(extractQrToken(`TICKET:${TOKEN}.AAAAAAAAAA`)).toBe(TOKEN)
    expect(extractQrToken('  ')).toBe('')
  })
})

describe('validateBackendTicket', () => {
  it('sends the UUID and event id to the backend validation endpoint', async () => {
    const fetchMock = stubResponse({
      accepted: true,
      reason: null,
      ticketId: '10',
      eventId: '4',
      validatedAt: '2026-11-06T10:00:00.000Z',
      details: { ticketNumber: 'TKT-ABC123', ticketType: 'General', eventTitle: 'Lake of Stars' },
    })

    const result = await validateBackendTicket(`TICKET:${TOKEN}.IGNORED`, '4')

    expect(result.accepted).toBe(true)
    expect(fetchMock).toHaveBeenCalledOnce()
    const [url, init] = fetchMock.mock.calls[0] as unknown as [string, RequestInit]
    expect(url).toBe('/be/tickets/validate')
    expect(JSON.parse(String(init.body))).toEqual({ qrToken: TOKEN, eventId: '4' })
  })
})

describe('backendValidationToResult', () => {
  it('maps an accepted scan to the valid scanner state', () => {
    const result = backendValidationToResult({
      accepted: true,
      reason: null,
      ticketId: '10',
      eventId: '4',
      validatedAt: '2026-11-06T10:00:00.000Z',
      details: { ticketNumber: 'TKT-ABC123', ticketType: 'VIP' },
    })
    expect(result.status).toBe('valid')
    expect(result.message).toContain('TKT-ABC123')
    expect(result.message).toContain('VIP')
  })

  it('maps the backend rejection reasons to scanner states', () => {
    const base = {
      accepted: false,
      ticketId: '10',
      eventId: '4',
      validatedAt: null,
      details: { ticketNumber: 'TKT-ABC123', eventTitle: 'Lake of Stars' },
    }
    expect(backendValidationToResult({ ...base, reason: 'ALREADY_USED' }).status).toBe('used')
    expect(backendValidationToResult({ ...base, reason: 'WRONG_EVENT' }).status).toBe('wrong_event')
    expect(backendValidationToResult({ ...base, reason: 'NOT_FOUND' }).status).toBe('invalid')
    expect(backendValidationToResult({ ...base, reason: 'CANCELLED' }).title).toBe('Cancelled ticket')
    expect(backendValidationToResult({ ...base, reason: 'EVENT_CANCELLED' }).title).toBe('Event cancelled')
  })
})

describe('backendTicketToRecord', () => {
  it('keeps the server ticket number and QR image', () => {
    const event: Event = {
      id: '4',
      title: 'Lake of Stars',
      description: '',
      category: 'Community',
      city: 'Mangochi',
      venue: 'Cape Maclear',
      date: '2026-11-14',
      time: '17:00',
      organizer: 'Organizer',
      image: '',
      ticketTypes: [],
    }
    const ticketType: TicketType = {
      id: '7',
      name: 'VIP',
      price: 50000,
      capacity: 20,
      sold: 1,
    }

    const ticket = backendTicketToRecord(
      { ticketNumber: 'TKT-ABC123', qrCodeDataUrl: 'data:image/png;base64,abc', status: 'ISSUED' },
      { userId: '9', event, ticketType, holder: ' Test Holder ' },
    )

    expect(ticket.id).toBe('TKT-ABC123')
    expect(ticket.holder).toBe('Test Holder')
    expect(ticket.status).toBe('valid')
    expect(ticket.qrCodeDataUrl).toBe('data:image/png;base64,abc')
  })
})


========================================================================================
FILE: src/lib/backendTickets.ts
========================================================================================
import type { Event, TicketType } from '../types/event'
import type { TicketRecord, TicketValidationResult } from './tickets'
import { api } from './backend'

// Endpoints and response shapes from the ticket service. The backend owns ticket
// issuance, the secret QR token and the single-use check-in state.

export interface BackendPurchasedTicket {
  ticketNumber: string
  qrCodeDataUrl: string
  status: string
}

export interface BackendScanDetails {
  ticketNumber?: string
  ticketType?: string
  eventTitle?: string
  checkedInAt?: string | null
  customer?: { name: string; email: string } | null
}

export interface BackendValidationResponse {
  accepted: boolean
  reason: string | null
  ticketId: string | null
  eventId: string | null
  validatedAt: string | null
  details?: BackendScanDetails | null
}

// A backend QR code contains the secret UUID. Accept the raw value as well as
// older TICKET:<id>.<tag> strings so manually pasted codes still work.
export function extractQrToken(raw: string): string {
  const cleaned = raw.trim().replace(/^TICKET:/i, '')
  const dot = cleaned.lastIndexOf('.')
  return (dot === -1 ? cleaned : cleaned.slice(0, dot)).trim()
}

export function purchaseBackendTicket(
  eventId: string,
  ticketTypeId: string,
): Promise<BackendPurchasedTicket> {
  return api<BackendPurchasedTicket>('/tickets/purchase', {
    method: 'POST',
    body: { eventId, ticketTypeId },
  })
}

export function validateBackendTicket(
  rawCode: string,
  eventId: string,
): Promise<BackendValidationResponse> {
  const qrToken = extractQrToken(rawCode)
  if (!qrToken) {
    return Promise.reject(new Error('Enter or scan a ticket QR code.'))
  }
  return api<BackendValidationResponse>('/tickets/validate', {
    method: 'POST',
    body: { qrToken, ...(eventId ? { eventId } : {}) },
  })
}

export function backendTicketToRecord(
  purchased: BackendPurchasedTicket,
  input: {
    userId: string
    event: Event
    ticketType: TicketType
    holder: string
  },
): TicketRecord {
  const { userId, event, ticketType, holder } = input
  return {
    id: purchased.ticketNumber,
    userId,
    eventId: event.id,
    eventName: event.title,
    date: event.date,
    time: event.time,
    venue: event.venue,
    city: event.city,
    ticketType: ticketType.name,
    price: ticketType.price,
    holder: holder.trim() || 'Guest',
    status: purchased.status === 'USED' ? 'used' : 'valid',
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    qrCodeDataUrl: purchased.qrCodeDataUrl,
  }
}

export function backendValidationToResult(
  response: BackendValidationResponse,
): TicketValidationResult {
  const details = response.details
  const ticketNumber = details?.ticketNumber ?? response.ticketId ?? 'This ticket'

  if (response.accepted) {
    const type = details?.ticketType ? ` · ${details.ticketType}` : ''
    return {
      status: 'valid',
      title: 'Valid',
      message: `${ticketNumber}${type} · admitted, attendance recorded.`,
    }
  }

  switch (response.reason) {
    case 'ALREADY_USED':
      return {
        status: 'used',
        title: 'Already used',
        message: `${ticketNumber} has already been admitted and cannot be accepted again.`,
      }
    case 'WRONG_EVENT':
      return {
        status: 'wrong_event',
        title: 'Wrong event',
        message: `${ticketNumber} is for ${details?.eventTitle ?? 'a different event'}, not this event.`,
      }
    case 'CANCELLED':
      return {
        status: 'invalid',
        title: 'Cancelled ticket',
        message: `${ticketNumber} has been cancelled and cannot be admitted.`,
      }
    case 'EVENT_CANCELLED':
      return {
        status: 'invalid',
        title: 'Event cancelled',
        message: 'This event has been cancelled, so the ticket cannot be admitted.',
      }
    case 'NOT_FOUND':
      return {
        status: 'invalid',
        title: 'Invalid ticket',
        message: 'This QR code is not a ticket issued for this platform.',
      }
    default:
      return {
        status: 'invalid',
        title: 'Invalid ticket',
        message: 'The ticket could not be accepted for this event.',
      }
  }
}

FILE: src/lib/events.test.ts
========================================================================================
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import {
  EVENTS_KEY,
  getEvent,
  incrementSold,
  listAllEvents,
  listEvents,
  resetEvents,
  saveEvent,
  type EventInput,
} from './events'

const baseInput: EventInput = {
  title: 'Test Fest',
  description: 'A test event',
  category: 'Festivals',
  city: 'Lilongwe',
  venue: 'Test Grounds',
  date: '2026-12-01',
  time: '18:00',
  organizer: 'QA',
  image: '',
  status: 'Published',
  ticketTypes: [{ id: 'general', name: 'General', price: 1000, capacity: 10, sold: 0 }],
}

beforeEach(() => {
  window.localStorage.clear()
})

describe('events store', () => {
  it('seeds published events on first read', () => {
    const events = listEvents()
    expect(events.length).toBeGreaterThan(0)
    expect(events.every((event) => event.status === 'Published')).toBe(true)
    expect(window.localStorage.getItem(EVENTS_KEY)).not.toBeNull()
  })

  it('creates a published event that appears in the public catalogue', () => {
    const created = saveEvent(baseInput)
    expect(getEvent(created.id)?.title).toBe('Test Fest')
    expect(listEvents().some((event) => event.id === created.id)).toBe(true)
  })

  it('keeps drafts out of the public catalogue but visible to the organizer', () => {
    const draft = saveEvent({ ...baseInput, status: 'Draft' })
    expect(listEvents().some((event) => event.id === draft.id)).toBe(false)
    expect(listAllEvents().some((event) => event.id === draft.id)).toBe(true)
  })

  it('updates an existing event in place, preserving its id', () => {
    const created = saveEvent(baseInput)
    saveEvent({ ...baseInput, id: created.id, title: 'Renamed Fest' })
    expect(getEvent(created.id)?.title).toBe('Renamed Fest')
    expect(listAllEvents().filter((event) => event.id === created.id)).toHaveLength(1)
  })

  it('increments sold seats but never exceeds capacity', () => {
    const created = saveEvent(baseInput)
    incrementSold(created.id, 'general', 3)
    expect(getEvent(created.id)?.ticketTypes[0].sold).toBe(3)
    incrementSold(created.id, 'general', 99)
    expect(getEvent(created.id)?.ticketTypes[0].sold).toBe(10)
  })

  it('resets back to the seed catalogue', () => {
    saveEvent({ ...baseInput, status: 'Draft' })
    resetEvents()
    expect(listAllEvents().some((event) => event.title === 'Test Fest')).toBe(false)
  })

  it('returns undefined for an unknown event id', () => {
    expect(getEvent('does-not-exist')).toBeUndefined()
  })
})

FILE: src/lib/events.ts
========================================================================================
import { events as SEED_EVENTS } from '../mocks/events'
import type { Event, TicketType } from '../types/event'

export type EventStatus = 'Draft' | 'Pending' | 'Published' | 'Cancelled'

export interface StoredEvent extends Event {
  status: EventStatus
}

export interface EventInput {
  id?: string
  title: string
  description: string
  category: string
  city: string
  venue: string
  date: string
  time: string
  organizer: string
  ownerId?: string
  image: string
  status: EventStatus
  ticketTypes: TicketType[]
}

export const EVENTS_KEY = 'tm.ticket-master.events'

const seed = (): StoredEvent[] => SEED_EVENTS.map((event) => ({ ...event, status: 'Published' }))

function hasStorage(): boolean {
  return typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof window.localStorage !== 'undefined'
}

function readEvents(): StoredEvent[] {
  if (!hasStorage()) return seed()
  const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(EVENTS_KEY)
  if (!raw) {
    const initial = seed()
    window.localStorage.setItem(EVENTS_KEY, JSON.stringify(initial))
    return initial
  }
  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as StoredEvent[]
    return Array.isArray(parsed) && parsed.length > 0 ? parsed : seed()
  } catch {
    const initial = seed()
    window.localStorage.setItem(EVENTS_KEY, JSON.stringify(initial))
    return initial
  }
}

function writeEvents(items: StoredEvent[]): void {
  if (!hasStorage()) return
  window.localStorage.setItem(EVENTS_KEY, JSON.stringify(items))
}

export function listAllEvents(): StoredEvent[] {
  return readEvents()
}

export function listEvents(): StoredEvent[] {
  return readEvents().filter((event) => event.status === 'Published')
}

export function getEvent(id: string): StoredEvent | undefined {
  return readEvents().find((event) => event.id === id)
}

function makeId(title: string): string {
  const slug = title.toLowerCase().trim().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/^-|-$/g, '')
  return slug || `event-${Date.now()}`
}

export function saveEvent(input: EventInput): StoredEvent {
  const events = readEvents()
  const existing = input.id ? events.find((event) => event.id === input.id) : undefined
  const saved: StoredEvent = {
    id: existing?.id ?? input.id ?? makeId(input.title),
    title: input.title.trim(),
    description: input.description,
    category: input.category,
    city: input.city,
    venue: input.venue,
    date: input.date,
    time: input.time,
    organizer: input.organizer,
    ownerId: existing?.ownerId ?? input.ownerId,
    image: input.image || '/images/events/event-1.webp',
    status: input.status,
    ticketTypes: input.ticketTypes.map((type) => ({ ...type })),
  }
  if (existing) {
    writeEvents(events.map((event) => (event.id === existing.id ? saved : event)))
  } else {
    writeEvents([saved, ...events])
  }
  return saved
}

export function deleteEvent(id: string): void {
  writeEvents(readEvents().filter((event) => event.id !== id))
}

export function incrementSold(eventId: string, ticketTypeId: string, quantity: number): void {
  const events = readEvents()
  const event = events.find((candidate) => candidate.id === eventId)
  if (!event) return
  event.ticketTypes = event.ticketTypes.map((type) =>
    type.id === ticketTypeId ? { ...type, sold: Math.min(type.capacity, type.sold + quantity) } : type,
  )
  writeEvents([...events])
}

export function resetEvents(): void {
  writeEvents(seed())
}


// An event belongs to the organizer account saved on it. Older events that have no
// owner yet fall back to matching the organizer name.
export function isEventOwnedBy(
  event: { ownerId?: string; organizer: string },
  user: { id: string; name: string },
): boolean {
  return event.ownerId ? event.ownerId === user.id : event.organizer === user.name
}

FILE: src/lib/paychangu.ts
========================================================================================
// Browser side of the PayChangu integration. It never sees the secret key: it calls our
// own /api/paychangu/* routes (server/paychangu.ts), and remembers what the customer was
// buying until PayChangu sends them back.

export interface PendingPayment {
  txRef: string
  eventId: string
  ticketTypeId: string
  quantity: number
  holder: string
  userId: string
  amount: number // total in MWK, what we expect PayChangu to confirm
  createdAt: string
  status: 'pending' | 'completed'
  ticketIds?: string[]
}

export interface VerifiedPayment {
  status: string // 'success' | 'pending' | 'failed' | ...
  tx_ref: string
  amount: number
  currency: string
  channel: string | null
  reference: string | null
}

export interface InitiateInput {
  amount: number
  email: string
  first_name: string
  last_name: string
  tx_ref: string
  callback_url: string
  return_url: string
  title: string
  description: string
}

export const PENDING_PAYMENTS_KEY = 'tm.ticket-master.pending-payments'

function readPending(): PendingPayment[] {
  try {
    const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(PENDING_PAYMENTS_KEY)
    const parsed = raw ? (JSON.parse(raw) as PendingPayment[]) : []
    return Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed : []
  } catch {
    return []
  }
}

function writePending(items: PendingPayment[]): void {
  try {
    window.localStorage.setItem(PENDING_PAYMENTS_KEY, JSON.stringify(items.slice(0, 50)))
  } catch {
    // storage unavailable: the payment page will show a "could not find your order" message
  }
}

export function savePendingPayment(payment: PendingPayment): void {
  writePending([payment, ...readPending().filter((item) => item.txRef !== payment.txRef)])
}

export function getPendingPayment(txRef: string): PendingPayment | undefined {
  return readPending().find((item) => item.txRef === txRef)
}

export function latestPendingPayment(): PendingPayment | undefined {
  return readPending().find((item) => item.status === 'pending')
}

export function removePendingPayment(txRef: string): void {
  writePending(readPending().filter((item) => item.txRef !== txRef))
}

export function markPaymentCompleted(txRef: string, ticketIds: string[]): void {
  writePending(
    readPending().map((item) => (item.txRef === txRef ? { ...item, status: 'completed', ticketIds } : item)),
  )
}

// Used when a multi-ticket order is partially fulfilled. The payment stays pending
// so a retry can claim only the tickets that are still missing.
export function addPendingTickets(txRef: string, ticketIds: string[]): void {
  if (ticketIds.length === 0) return
  writePending(
    readPending().map((item) =>
      item.txRef === txRef
        ? { ...item, ticketIds: Array.from(new Set([...(item.ticketIds ?? []), ...ticketIds])) }
        : item,
    ),
  )
}

export function makeTxRef(): string {
  const random = Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10)
  return `TM-${Date.now().toString(36)}-${random}`.toUpperCase()
}

async function api<T>(path: string, init?: RequestInit): Promise<T> {
  let response: Response
  try {
    response = await fetch(path, init)
  } catch {
    throw new Error('Could not reach the payment service. Check your connection and try again.')
  }
  let body: unknown = null
  try {
    body = await response.json()
  } catch {
    body = null
  }
  if (!response.ok) {
    const message =
      body && typeof body === 'object' && 'error' in body && typeof (body as { error: unknown }).error === 'string'
        ? (body as { error: string }).error
        : 'Payment service error.'
    throw new Error(message)
  }
  return body as T
}

export function initiatePayment(input: InitiateInput): Promise<{ checkout_url: string; tx_ref: string }> {
  return api('/api/paychangu/initiate', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify(input),
  })
}

export function verifyPayment(txRef: string): Promise<VerifiedPayment> {
  return api(`/api/paychangu/verify/${encodeURIComponent(txRef)}`)
}

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { hmacSha256Hex, isValidTicketTag, signTicket } from './ticketSignature'

describe('hmacSha256Hex', () => {
  // test vectors from RFC 4231
  it('matches RFC 4231 test case 1', () => {
    expect(hmacSha256Hex('\x0b'.repeat(20), 'Hi There')).toBe(
      'b0344c61d8db38535ca8afceaf0bf12b881dc200c9833da726e9376c2e32cff7',
    )
  })

  it('matches RFC 4231 test case 2', () => {
    expect(hmacSha256Hex('Jefe', 'what do ya want for nothing?')).toBe(
      '5bdcc146bf60754e6a042426089575c75a003f089d2739839dec58b964ec3843',
    )
  })

  it('matches the browser crypto for different lengths and long keys', async () => {
    const cases: [string, string][] = [
      ['k', ''],
      ['secret', 'a'],
      ['secret', 'x'.repeat(55)],
      ['secret', 'x'.repeat(56)],
      ['secret', 'x'.repeat(64)],
      ['secret', 'x'.repeat(200)],
      ['L'.repeat(100), 'message with unicode: Chimwemwe Banda - Lilongwe'],
    ]
    for (const [key, message] of cases) {
      const cryptoKey = await crypto.subtle.importKey(
        'raw',
        new TextEncoder().encode(key),
        { name: 'HMAC', hash: 'SHA-256' },
        false,
        ['sign'],
      )
      const signature = await crypto.subtle.sign('HMAC', cryptoKey, new TextEncoder().encode(message))
      const expected = Array.from(new Uint8Array(signature), (b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('')
      expect(hmacSha256Hex(key, message)).toBe(expected)
    }
  })
})

describe('signTicket', () => {
  it('makes a 10 character tag that is stable for the same ticket', () => {
    const tag = signTicket('TM-2026-ABCDEFGHJKLM', 'lake-of-stars')
    expect(tag).toMatch(/^[0-9A-F]{10}$/)
    expect(signTicket('TM-2026-ABCDEFGHJKLM', 'lake-of-stars')).toBe(tag)
    expect(signTicket('tm-2026-abcdefghjklm', 'lake-of-stars')).toBe(tag)
  })

  it('changes when the ticket id or the event changes', () => {
    const tag = signTicket('TM-2026-ABCDEFGHJKLM', 'lake-of-stars')
    expect(signTicket('TM-2026-ABCDEFGHJKLN', 'lake-of-stars')).not.toBe(tag)
    expect(signTicket('TM-2026-ABCDEFGHJKLM', 'dev-summit')).not.toBe(tag)
  })

  it('only accepts the matching tag', () => {
    const tag = signTicket('TM-2026-ABCDEFGHJKLM', 'lake-of-stars')
    expect(isValidTicketTag('TM-2026-ABCDEFGHJKLM', 'lake-of-stars', tag)).toBe(true)
    expect(isValidTicketTag('TM-2026-ABCDEFGHJKLM', 'lake-of-stars', tag.toLowerCase())).toBe(true)
    expect(isValidTicketTag('TM-2026-ABCDEFGHJKLM', 'lake-of-stars', '0000000000')).toBe(false)
    expect(isValidTicketTag('TM-2026-ABCDEFGHJKLM', 'lake-of-stars', '')).toBe(false)
    expect(isValidTicketTag('TM-2026-ABCDEFGHJKLM', 'lake-of-stars', tag + '0')).toBe(false)
  })
})

FILE: src/lib/ticketSignature.ts
========================================================================================
// Ticket signatures for the QR code.
//
// IMPORTANT: this key ships inside the browser bundle, so anyone can read it.
// It only stops casual forgery in the demo. Once there is a backend, ticket
// issuing and signature checking MUST move to the server, with a secret that
// never reaches the browser.
const SIGNING_KEY = 'ticket-master-demo-signing-key-v1'

const K = new Uint32Array([
  0x428a2f98, 0x71374491, 0xb5c0fbcf, 0xe9b5dba5, 0x3956c25b, 0x59f111f1, 0x923f82a4, 0xab1c5ed5,
  0xd807aa98, 0x12835b01, 0x243185be, 0x550c7dc3, 0x72be5d74, 0x80deb1fe, 0x9bdc06a7, 0xc19bf174,
  0xe49b69c1, 0xefbe4786, 0x0fc19dc6, 0x240ca1cc, 0x2de92c6f, 0x4a7484aa, 0x5cb0a9dc, 0x76f988da,
  0x983e5152, 0xa831c66d, 0xb00327c8, 0xbf597fc7, 0xc6e00bf3, 0xd5a79147, 0x06ca6351, 0x14292967,
  0x27b70a85, 0x2e1b2138, 0x4d2c6dfc, 0x53380d13, 0x650a7354, 0x766a0abb, 0x81c2c92e, 0x92722c85,
  0xa2bfe8a1, 0xa81a664b, 0xc24b8b70, 0xc76c51a3, 0xd192e819, 0xd6990624, 0xf40e3585, 0x106aa070,
  0x19a4c116, 0x1e376c08, 0x2748774c, 0x34b0bcb5, 0x391c0cb3, 0x4ed8aa4a, 0x5b9cca4f, 0x682e6ff3,
  0x748f82ee, 0x78a5636f, 0x84c87814, 0x8cc70208, 0x90befffa, 0xa4506ceb, 0xbef9a3f7, 0xc67178f2,
])

function rotr(x: number, n: number): number {
  return (x >>> n) | (x << (32 - n))
}

function sha256(message: Uint8Array): Uint8Array {
  const h = new Uint32Array([
    0x6a09e667, 0xbb67ae85, 0x3c6ef372, 0xa54ff53a, 0x510e527f, 0x9b05688c, 0x1f83d9ab, 0x5be0cd19,
  ])

  // pad: 0x80, zeros, then the message length in bits as 64 bits
  const paddedLength = ((message.length + 9 + 63) >> 6) << 6
  const padded = new Uint8Array(paddedLength)
  padded.set(message)
  padded[message.length] = 0x80
  const view = new DataView(padded.buffer)
  const bitLength = message.length * 8
  view.setUint32(paddedLength - 8, Math.floor(bitLength / 0x100000000))
  view.setUint32(paddedLength - 4, bitLength >>> 0)

  const w = new Uint32Array(64)
  for (let offset = 0; offset < paddedLength; offset += 64) {
    for (let i = 0; i < 16; i += 1) w[i] = view.getUint32(offset + i * 4)
    for (let i = 16; i < 64; i += 1) {
      const s0 = rotr(w[i - 15], 7) ^ rotr(w[i - 15], 18) ^ (w[i - 15] >>> 3)
      const s1 = rotr(w[i - 2], 17) ^ rotr(w[i - 2], 19) ^ (w[i - 2] >>> 10)
      w[i] = w[i - 16] + s0 + w[i - 7] + s1
    }

    let a = h[0]
    let b = h[1]
    let c = h[2]
    let d = h[3]
    let e = h[4]
    let f = h[5]
    let g = h[6]
    let hh = h[7]
    for (let i = 0; i < 64; i += 1) {
      const S1 = rotr(e, 6) ^ rotr(e, 11) ^ rotr(e, 25)
      const ch = (e & f) ^ (~e & g)
      const t1 = (hh + S1 + ch + K[i] + w[i]) | 0
      const S0 = rotr(a, 2) ^ rotr(a, 13) ^ rotr(a, 22)
      const maj = (a & b) ^ (a & c) ^ (b & c)
      const t2 = (S0 + maj) | 0
      hh = g
      g = f
      f = e
      e = (d + t1) | 0
      d = c
      c = b
      b = a
      a = (t1 + t2) | 0
    }
    h[0] += a
    h[1] += b
    h[2] += c
    h[3] += d
    h[4] += e
    h[5] += f
    h[6] += g
    h[7] += hh
  }

  const out = new Uint8Array(32)
  const outView = new DataView(out.buffer)
  for (let i = 0; i < 8; i += 1) outView.setUint32(i * 4, h[i])
  return out
}

function hmacSha256(key: Uint8Array, message: Uint8Array): Uint8Array {
  const blockSize = 64
  const paddedKey = new Uint8Array(blockSize)
  paddedKey.set(key.length > blockSize ? sha256(key) : key)

  const inner = new Uint8Array(blockSize + message.length)
  const outer = new Uint8Array(blockSize + 32)
  for (let i = 0; i < blockSize; i += 1) {
    inner[i] = paddedKey[i] ^ 0x36
    outer[i] = paddedKey[i] ^ 0x5c
  }
  inner.set(message, blockSize)
  outer.set(sha256(inner), blockSize)
  return sha256(outer)
}

function toHex(bytes: Uint8Array): string {
  return Array.from(bytes, (byte) => byte.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('')
}

const encoder = new TextEncoder()

// full HMAC-SHA256 as hex (exported so it can be tested against known values)
export function hmacSha256Hex(key: string, message: string): string {
  return toHex(hmacSha256(encoder.encode(key), encoder.encode(message)))
}

// Short integrity tag for a ticket: 10 hex characters (40 bits) of an
// HMAC over the ticket ID and the event ID.
export function signTicket(ticketId: string, eventId: string): string {
  return hmacSha256Hex(SIGNING_KEY, ticketId.toUpperCase() + '|' + eventId).slice(0, 10).toUpperCase()
}

export function isValidTicketTag(ticketId: string, eventId: string, tag: string): boolean {
  const expected = signTicket(ticketId, eventId)
  const given = tag.toUpperCase()
  if (given.length !== expected.length) return false
  let difference = 0
  for (let i = 0; i < expected.length; i += 1) {
    difference |= expected.charCodeAt(i) ^ given.charCodeAt(i)
  }
  return difference === 0
}

FILE: src/lib/tickets.test.ts

// @vitest-environment jsdom
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import type { Event, TicketType } from '../types/event'
import { calculateTotals } from '../features/checkout/format'
import { SERVICE_FEE } from '../features/checkout/mockData'
import {
  BOOKINGS_KEY,
  DEMO_CUSTOMER_ID,
  TICKETS_KEY,
  issueBooking,
  listBookings,
  listBookingsForUser,
  listTickets,
  listTicketsForUser,
} from './tickets'

const event = {
  id: 'evt-test',
  title: 'Test Concert',
  date: '2026-11-14',
  time: '18:00',
  venue: 'Test Venue',
  city: 'Blantyre',
} as unknown as Event

const ticketType = { id: 'general', name: 'General', price: 35000 } as unknown as TicketType

describe('booking total', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    window.localStorage.clear()
  })

  it('equals the checkout total for one ticket', () => {
    const charged = calculateTotals(ticketType.price, 1)
    const { booking } = issueBooking({
      userId: 'u-test',
      event,
      ticketType,
      quantity: 1,
      holder: 'Test',
    })

    expect(booking.total).toBe(charged.total)
    expect(booking.total).toBe(35500)
    expect(booking.subtotal).toBe(35000)
    expect(booking.serviceFee).toBe(SERVICE_FEE)
  })

  it('equals the checkout total for several tickets', () => {
    const charged = calculateTotals(ticketType.price, 3)
    const { booking } = issueBooking({
      userId: 'u-test',
      event,
      ticketType,
      quantity: 3,
      holder: 'Test',
    })

    expect(booking.total).toBe(charged.total)
    expect(booking.subtotal).toBe(105000)
  })

  it('keeps the stored total when bookings are read back', () => {
    const { booking } = issueBooking({
      userId: 'u-test',
      event,
      ticketType,
      quantity: 2,
      holder: 'Test',
    })
    const saved = listBookings().find((b) => b.id === booking.id)
    expect(saved?.total).toBe(booking.total)
  })

  it('gives older saved bookings a breakdown instead of undefined', () => {
    window.localStorage.setItem(
      BOOKINGS_KEY,
      JSON.stringify([
        {
          id: 'BK-OLD',
          eventTitle: 'Old Event',
          venue: 'Old Venue',
          date: '2026-09-12',
          quantity: 1,
          total: 35500,
          status: 'confirmed',
          ticketIds: [],
        },
      ]),
    )
    const [old] = listBookings()
    expect(old.serviceFee).toBe(SERVICE_FEE)
    expect(old.subtotal).toBe(35000)
    expect(old.total).toBe(35500)
  })
})

describe('ticket ownership', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    window.localStorage.clear()
  })

  it('shows nothing to a brand-new customer', () => {
    expect(listTicketsForUser('u-new')).toEqual([])
    expect(listBookingsForUser('u-new')).toEqual([])
  })

  it('keeps the seeded demo data for the demo customer', () => {
    expect(listTicketsForUser(DEMO_CUSTOMER_ID).map((ticket) => ticket.id)).toEqual(
      expect.arrayContaining(['TM-2026-K7QX4ZP9R2WM', 'TM-2026-H3VN8DC5T6YB', 'TM-2026-W9FJ2MXA4LEU']),
    )
    expect(listBookingsForUser(DEMO_CUSTOMER_ID).length).toBeGreaterThan(0)
  })

  it('stamps the buyer id on the booking and every ticket', () => {
    const { booking, tickets } = issueBooking({
      userId: 'u-buyer',
      event,
      ticketType,
      quantity: 2,
      holder: 'Buyer',
    })

    expect(booking.userId).toBe('u-buyer')
    expect(booking.createdAt).toBeTruthy()
    expect(booking.eventId).toBe(event.id)
    expect(tickets).toHaveLength(2)
    expect(tickets.every((ticket) => ticket.userId === 'u-buyer')).toBe(true)

    const ownedTickets = listTicketsForUser('u-buyer')
    expect(ownedTickets).toHaveLength(2)
    expect(ownedTickets.every((ticket) => ticket.id.startsWith('TM-2026-'))).toBe(true)

    const ownedBookings = listBookingsForUser('u-buyer')
    expect(ownedBookings).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(ownedBookings[0].id).toBe(booking.id)
  })

  it('separates two accounts on the same browser', () => {
    issueBooking({ userId: 'u-a', event, ticketType, quantity: 1, holder: 'A' })
    issueBooking({ userId: 'u-b', event, ticketType, quantity: 3, holder: 'B' })

    expect(listTicketsForUser('u-a')).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(listTicketsForUser('u-b')).toHaveLength(3)
    expect(listBookingsForUser('u-a')).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(listBookingsForUser('u-b')).toHaveLength(1)
  })

  it('keeps unscoped access for staff and admin', () => {
    issueBooking({ userId: 'u-buyer', event, ticketType, quantity: 1, holder: 'Buyer' })

    const all = listTickets()
    expect(all).toHaveLength(listTicketsForUser(DEMO_CUSTOMER_ID).length + 1)
    expect(all.some((ticket) => ticket.userId === 'u-buyer')).toBe(true)
    expect(all.some((ticket) => ticket.userId === DEMO_CUSTOMER_ID)).toBe(true)
  })

  it('back-fills userId on legacy records that stored an owner email', () => {
    window.localStorage.setItem(
      TICKETS_KEY,
      JSON.stringify([
        {
          id: 'TM-OLD-1',
          eventId: event.id,
          eventName: event.title,
          date: event.date,
          time: event.time,
          venue: event.venue,
          city: event.city,
          ticketType: 'General',
          price: 35000,
          holder: 'Legacy',
          ownerEmail: 'customer@example.com',
          status: 'valid',
        },
      ]),
    )

    const legacy = listTicketsForUser(DEMO_CUSTOMER_ID)
    expect(legacy.map((ticket) => ticket.id)).toContain('TM-OLD-1')
    expect(legacy[0].userId).toBe(DEMO_CUSTOMER_ID)
  })

  it('migrates purchases from the pre-v2 storage keys', () => {
    window.localStorage.setItem(
      'tm.ticket-master.tickets',
      JSON.stringify([
        {
          id: 'TM-MIGRATED-1',
          eventId: event.id,
          eventName: event.title,
          date: event.date,
          time: event.time,
          venue: event.venue,
          city: event.city,
          ticketType: 'General',
          price: 35000,
          holder: 'Legacy',
          ownerEmail: 'customer@example.com',
          status: 'valid',
        },
      ]),
    )

    const tickets = listTicketsForUser(DEMO_CUSTOMER_ID)
    expect(tickets.map((ticket) => ticket.id)).toContain('TM-MIGRATED-1')
    expect(window.localStorage.getItem('tm.ticket-master.tickets')).toBeNull()
  })

  it('seeds the demo tickets when an old empty store is found', () => {
    window.localStorage.setItem('tm.ticket-master.tickets', '[]')
    expect(listTicketsForUser(DEMO_CUSTOMER_ID)).toHaveLength(3)
  })
})

import type { Event, TicketType } from '../types/event'
import { calculateTotals } from '../features/checkout/format'
import { SERVICE_FEE } from '../features/checkout/mockData'
import { findUserByEmail } from '../features/authentication/authStore'
import { isValidTicketTag, signTicket } from './ticketSignature'

export type TicketStatus = 'valid' | 'used'

export interface TicketRecord {
  id: string
  userId: string
  eventId: string
  eventName: string
  date: string // ISO date, e.g. 2026-11-14
  time: string // 24h, e.g. 18:00
  venue: string
  city: string
  ticketType: string
  price: number // in MK
  holder: string
  status: TicketStatus
  createdAt: string // ISO timestamp, used for sales reporting
  checkedInAt?: string
  qrCodeDataUrl?: string // backend-issued QR image; local tickets generate their QR in the browser
}

export type BookingStatus = 'confirmed' | 'cancelled'

export interface BookingRecord {
  id: string
  userId: string
  eventId: string
  eventTitle: string
  venue: string
  date: string // ISO date
  quantity: number
  subtotal: number // tickets only, in MK
  serviceFee: number // in MK
  total: number // what the customer paid (subtotal + serviceFee), in MK
  status: BookingStatus
  ticketIds: string[]
  createdAt: string // ISO timestamp, used for sales reporting
}

export type TicketValidationStatus = 'valid' | 'invalid' | 'used' | 'wrong_event'

export interface TicketValidationResult {
  status: TicketValidationStatus
  title: string
  message: string
  ticket?: TicketRecord
  tampered?: boolean // the QR code carried a security tag that did not match
}

// Versioned keys. Records written by older builds are migrated on first read.
export const TICKETS_KEY = 'tm.ticket-master.tickets.v3'
export const BOOKINGS_KEY = 'tm.ticket-master.bookings.v3'
const LEGACY_TICKETS_KEY = 'tm.ticket-master.tickets'
const LEGACY_BOOKINGS_KEY = 'tm.ticket-master.bookings'

// The seeded demo data belongs to the one-tap customer account.
export const DEMO_CUSTOMER_ID = 'u-1'

// A scanned or typed code can look like any of these (all accepted):
//   TM-2026-ABCDEFGHJKLM                  plain ticket id (older form)
//   TICKET:TM-2026-ABCDEFGHJKLM           QR code, unsigned (older form)
//   TICKET:TM-2026-ABCDEFGHJKLM.1A2B3C4D5E   QR code with its security tag
export function parseTicketCode(raw: string): { id: string; tag: string | null } {
  const cleaned = raw.trim().toUpperCase().replace(/^TICKET:/, '')
  const dot = cleaned.lastIndexOf('.')
  if (dot === -1) return { id: cleaned, tag: null }
  return { id: cleaned.slice(0, dot), tag: cleaned.slice(dot + 1) }
}

// the ticket id only, whichever form was scanned
export function normalizeTicketCode(raw: string): string {
  return parseTicketCode(raw).id
}

// Demo timestamps are relative to first load so the "last 7 days" chart has
// something to show whenever the demo is opened.
function demoCreatedAt(daysAgo: number, hour: number): string {
  const date = new Date()
  date.setDate(date.getDate() - daysAgo)
  date.setHours(hour, 0, 0, 0)
  return date.toISOString()
}
