function hasStorage(): boolean {
  return typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof window.localStorage !== 'undefined'
}

function readList<T>(key: string, seed: T[], legacyKey?: string): T[] {
  if (!hasStorage()) return [...seed]

  const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(key)
  if (!raw) {
    // One-time migration from the pre-userId keys. Real purchases win over
    // the demo seed; an empty legacy list means the demo was never seeded.
    if (legacyKey) {
      const legacyRaw = window.localStorage.getItem(legacyKey)
      if (legacyRaw) {
        window.localStorage.removeItem(legacyKey)
        try {
          const legacy = JSON.parse(legacyRaw) as T[]
          if (Array.isArray(legacy) && legacy.length > 0) {
            window.localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(legacy))
            return legacy
          }
        } catch {
          // corrupt legacy data: fall through to the fresh seed
        }
      }
    }
    window.localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(seed))
    return [...seed]
  }

  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as T[]
    return Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed : [...seed]
  } catch {
    window.localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(seed))
    return [...seed]
  }
}

function writeList<T>(key: string, items: T[]): void {
  if (!hasStorage()) return
  window.localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(items))
}

// Old builds stored an owner email instead of a user id. Map it back to the
// account so tickets bought before the upgrade do not disappear.
function legacyOwnerId(email?: string): string {
  if (!email) return ''
  if (!hasStorage()) return email
  return findUserByEmail(email)?.id ?? email
}

type LegacyTicket = Omit<TicketRecord, 'userId' | 'createdAt'> & {
  userId?: string
  createdAt?: string
  ownerEmail?: string
}

type LegacyBooking = Omit<BookingRecord, 'userId' | 'eventId' | 'createdAt'> & {
  userId?: string
  eventId?: string
  createdAt?: string
}

function normalizeTicket(record: LegacyTicket): TicketRecord {
  const userId =
    record.userId ||
    legacyOwnerId(record.ownerEmail) ||
    (DEMO_TICKETS.some((ticket) => ticket.id === record.id) ? DEMO_CUSTOMER_ID : '')

  return {
    ...record,
    userId,
    createdAt: record.createdAt ?? new Date().toISOString(),
  }
}

function normalizeBooking(record: LegacyBooking): BookingRecord {
  const tickets = listTickets()
  const firstTicket = tickets.find((ticket) => ticket.id === record.ticketIds[0])
  const serviceFee =
    typeof record.serviceFee === 'number' ? record.serviceFee : SERVICE_FEE
  const subtotal =
    typeof record.subtotal === 'number'
      ? record.subtotal
      : Math.max(record.total - serviceFee, 0)

  return {
    ...record,
    userId: record.userId || firstTicket?.userId || '',
    eventId: record.eventId || firstTicket?.eventId || '',
    serviceFee,
    subtotal,
    createdAt: record.createdAt ?? new Date().toISOString(),
  }
}

// Unscoped accessors are for staff, admin and organizer reporting.
export function listTickets(): TicketRecord[] {
  return readList<LegacyTicket>(TICKETS_KEY, DEMO_TICKETS, LEGACY_TICKETS_KEY).map(normalizeTicket)
}

// Customer-facing accessor: only the signed-in user's tickets.
export function listTicketsForUser(userId: string): TicketRecord[] {
  return listTickets().filter((ticket) => ticket.userId === userId)
}

export function getTicket(id: string): TicketRecord | undefined {
  const normalized = normalizeTicketCode(id)
  return listTickets().find((ticket) => ticket.id.toUpperCase() === normalized)
}

// What the QR code of a ticket contains: the id plus its security tag.
// A ticket that is not in the store (sample data) gets the plain older form.
export function ticketQrValue(id: string): string {
  const ticket = getTicket(id)
  if (!ticket) return 'TICKET:' + normalizeTicketCode(id)
  return 'TICKET:' + ticket.id + '.' + signTicket(ticket.id, ticket.eventId)
}

// true when the tag from a scanned QR code matches this ticket
export function hasValidSignature(ticket: TicketRecord, tag: string): boolean {
  return isValidTicketTag(ticket.id, ticket.eventId, tag)
}

export function listBookings(): BookingRecord[] {
  return readList<LegacyBooking>(BOOKINGS_KEY, DEMO_BOOKINGS, LEGACY_BOOKINGS_KEY).map(normalizeBooking)
}

// Customer-facing accessor: only the signed-in user's bookings.
export function listBookingsForUser(userId: string): BookingRecord[] {
  return listBookings().filter((booking) => booking.userId === userId)
}

// Clears current and legacy stores so the demo seed is recreated on reload.
export function resetTicketStore(): void {
  if (!hasStorage()) return
  for (const key of [TICKETS_KEY, BOOKINGS_KEY, LEGACY_TICKETS_KEY, LEGACY_BOOKINGS_KEY]) {
    window.localStorage.removeItem(key)
  }
}

// 32 characters (no I, O, 0 or 1) so each random byte maps to one character evenly
const ID_ALPHABET = 'ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789'

// 12 random characters = 60 bits, from the browser's secure random generator
function generateId(prefix: string): string {
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(12)
  crypto.getRandomValues(bytes)
  let serial = ''
  for (const byte of bytes) serial += ID_ALPHABET.charAt(byte & 31)
  return prefix + '-2026-' + serial
}

export interface IssueTicketInput {
  userId: string
  eventId: string
  eventName: string
  date: string
  time: string
  venue: string
  city: string
  ticketType: string
  price: number
  holder: string
}

export function issueTicket(input: IssueTicketInput): TicketRecord {
  const tickets = listTickets()
  let id = generateId('TM')
  while (tickets.some((ticket) => ticket.id === id)) {
    id = generateId('TM')
  }

  const ticket: TicketRecord = {
    ...input,
    id,
    status: 'valid',
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  }

  writeList(TICKETS_KEY, [ticket, ...tickets])
  return ticket
}

// Stores tickets issued by the backend so My Tickets and the confirmation page can
// read them through the same local ticket accessors. The backend remains the source
// of truth; this is only a cache of what it has already issued.
export function saveIssuedTickets(tickets: TicketRecord[]): void {
  if (tickets.length === 0) return
  const ids = new Set(tickets.map((ticket) => ticket.id))
  const remaining = listTickets().filter((ticket) => !ids.has(ticket.id))
  writeList(TICKETS_KEY, [...tickets, ...remaining])
}

export function markTicketUsed(id: string): TicketRecord | undefined {
  const normalized = normalizeTicketCode(id)
  const tickets = listTickets()
  const index = tickets.findIndex((ticket) => ticket.id.toUpperCase() === normalized)
  if (index === -1) return undefined

  const updated: TicketRecord = {
    ...tickets[index],
    status: 'used',
    checkedInAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  }
  tickets[index] = updated
  writeList(TICKETS_KEY, tickets)
  return updated
}

export interface IssueBookingInput {
  userId: string
  event: Event
  ticketType: TicketType
  quantity: number
  holder: string
  serviceFee?: number // defaults to the checkout service fee
}

export interface IssueBookingResult {
  booking: BookingRecord
  tickets: TicketRecord[]
}

// Issues the tickets and the booking that owns them in one step.
export function issueBooking(input: IssueBookingInput): IssueBookingResult {
  const { userId, event, ticketType, quantity, holder, serviceFee } = input
  const createdAt = new Date().toISOString()

  const tickets: TicketRecord[] = []
  for (let index = 0; index < quantity; index += 1) {
    tickets.push(
      issueTicket({
        userId,
        eventId: event.id,
        eventName: event.title,
        date: event.date,
        time: event.time,
        venue: event.venue,
        city: event.city,
        ticketType: ticketType.name,
        price: ticketType.price,
        holder: holder.trim() || 'Guest',
      }),
    )
  }

  const bookings = listBookings()
  let bookingId = generateId('BK')
  while (bookings.some((booking) => booking.id === bookingId)) {
    bookingId = generateId('BK')
  }

  // Same calculation the checkout page uses, so the stored total always
  // matches the amount the customer was charged.
  const totals = calculateTotals(ticketType.price, quantity, serviceFee)

  const booking: BookingRecord = {
    id: bookingId,
    userId,
    eventId: event.id,
    eventTitle: event.title,
    venue: event.venue,
    date: event.date,
    quantity,
    subtotal: totals.subtotal,
    serviceFee: totals.serviceFee,
    total: totals.total,
    status: 'confirmed',
    ticketIds: tickets.map((ticket) => ticket.id),
    createdAt,
  }

  writeList(BOOKINGS_KEY, [booking, ...bookings])
  return { booking, tickets }
}

FILE: src/lib/verification.ts

export type KYCStatus = 'Unverified' | 'Pending' | 'Verified' | 'Rejected';

export interface OrganizerKYC {
  userId: string;
  businessName: string;
  phoneNumber: string;
  nationalId: string;
  status: KYCStatus;
  submittedAt?: string;
}

const KYC_KEY = 'tm.ticket-master.kyc';

function hasStorage(): boolean {
  return typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof window.localStorage !== 'undefined';
}

export function getKYC(userId: string): OrganizerKYC | null {
  if (!hasStorage()) return null;
  const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(KYC_KEY);
  if (!raw) return null;
  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as OrganizerKYC[];
    return parsed.find((k) => k.userId === userId) ?? null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export function saveKYC(data: OrganizerKYC): void {
  if (!hasStorage()) return;
  const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(KYC_KEY);
  let parsed: OrganizerKYC[] = [];
  if (raw) {
    try { parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as OrganizerKYC[]; } catch {}
  }
  const existingIndex = parsed.findIndex((k) => k.userId === data.userId);
  if (existingIndex >= 0) {
    parsed[existingIndex] = data;
  } else {
    parsed.push(data);
  }
  window.localStorage.setItem(KYC_KEY, JSON.stringify(parsed));
}

export function listAllKYC(): OrganizerKYC[] {
  if (!hasStorage()) return [];
  const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(KYC_KEY);
  if (!raw) return [];
  try { return JSON.parse(raw) as OrganizerKYC[]; } catch { return []; }
}

import { StrictMode } from 'react';
import { createRoot } from 'react-dom/client';
import { QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { RouterProvider } from '@tanstack/react-router';
import './index.css';
import { queryClient } from './lib/api/queryClient';
import { router } from './routes';
import { AuthProvider } from './features/authentication';
import { ToastProvider } from './features/toast';

createRoot(document.getElementById('root')!).render(
  <StrictMode>
    <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
      <ToastProvider>
        <AuthProvider>
          <RouterProvider router={router} />
        </AuthProvider>
      </ToastProvider>
    </QueryClientProvider>
  </StrictMode>,
);

FILE: src/mocks/delay.ts

export const withDelay = <T>(data: T, ms = 500): Promise<T> =>
  new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(() => resolve(data), ms))

========================================================================================
FILE: src/routes/index.tsx
========================================================================================
import { createRootRoute, createRoute, createRouter } from '@tanstack/react-router';
import { AppLayout } from '../components/AppLayout';
import { LoginPage, requireAuth } from '../features/authentication';
import { EventsPage, EventDetailsPage } from '../features/events';
import { CheckoutPage, PaymentReturnPage } from '../features/checkout';
import { MyTicketsPage, BookingHistoryPage, TicketDetailPage } from '../features/tickets';
import { ScannerPage } from '../features/validation';
import { OrganizerDashboardPage, CreateEventPage, StaffManagementPage } from '../features/organizer';
import { HomePage } from '../features/landing'
import { validateEventSearch } from '../features/landing/filters';
import { BackendUnavailablePage } from '../components/BackendUnavailablePage';
import NotFoundPage from '../components/NotFoundPage';
import type { ComponentType } from 'react';

const rootRoute = createRootRoute({ component: AppLayout });

const page = <P extends string>(path: P, component: ComponentType<any>, beforeLoad?: ReturnType<typeof requireAuth>) => createRoute({ getParentRoute: () => rootRoute, path, component: component as any, beforeLoad });

const indexRoute = createRoute({
  getParentRoute: () => rootRoute,
  path: '/',
  component: HomePage,
  validateSearch: validateEventSearch,
});
const eventsRoute = createRoute({
  getParentRoute: () => rootRoute,
  path: '/events',
  component: EventsPage,
  validateSearch: validateEventSearch,
});
const loginRoute = createRoute({
  getParentRoute: () => rootRoute,
  path: '/login',
  component: LoginPage,
  validateSearch: (search: Record<string, unknown>): { redirect?: string } => ({ redirect: typeof search.redirect === 'string' ? search.redirect : undefined }),
});
const notFoundRoute = createRoute({ getParentRoute: () => rootRoute, path: '*', component: NotFoundPage })

const routeTree = rootRoute.addChildren([
  indexRoute,
  loginRoute,
  eventsRoute,
  page('/events/$eventId', EventDetailsPage),
  page('/checkout/return', PaymentReturnPage, requireAuth('customer')), // PayChangu sends customers back here
  page('/checkout/$eventId', CheckoutPage, requireAuth('customer')),
  page('/tickets', MyTicketsPage, requireAuth('customer')),         // <-- Added base tickets route
  page('/tickets/$ticketId', TicketDetailPage, requireAuth('customer')), // <-- Kept specific route
  page('/bookings', BookingHistoryPage, requireAuth('customer')),
  page('/organizer/kyc', () => <BackendUnavailablePage title="Organizer verification unavailable" feature="Organizer verification" backTo="/organizer/dashboard" />, requireAuth('organizer')),
  page('/organizer/dashboard', OrganizerDashboardPage, requireAuth('organizer')),
  page('/organizer/events/new', CreateEventPage, requireAuth('organizer')),
  page('/organizer/events/$eventId/edit', CreateEventPage, requireAuth('organizer')),
  page('/organizer/staff', StaffManagementPage, requireAuth('organizer')),
  page('/admin', () => <BackendUnavailablePage title="Admin panel unavailable" feature="Admin management" />, requireAuth('admin')),
  page('/scan', ScannerPage, requireAuth('staff')),
  notFoundRoute,
]);

export const router = createRouter({ routeTree });

declare module '@tanstack/react-router' {
  interface Register { router: typeof router; }
}

========================================================================================
FILE: src/types/event.ts
========================================================================================
export interface TicketType {
  id: string
  name: string
  price: number
  capacity: number
  sold: number
}

export interface Event {
  id: string
  title: string
  category: string
  city: string
  venue: string
  date: string // ISO date, e.g. 2026-11-14
  time: string // 24h, e.g. 18:00
  description: string
  organizer: string
  ownerId?: string // id of the organizer account that owns the event
  image: string
  ticketTypes: TicketType[]
}

// cheapest ticket type price, used for cards, filters and sorting
export function fromPrice(event: Event): number {
  if (event.ticketTypes.length === 0) return 0
  return Math.min(...event.ticketTypes.map((type) => type.price))
}

export function remainingSeats(type: TicketType): number {
  return Math.max(0, type.capacity - type.sold)
}

export function isSoldOut(event: Event): boolean {
  return (
    event.ticketTypes.length > 0 &&
    event.ticketTypes.every((type) => remainingSeats(type) === 0)
  )
}

// true when the event date is today or later
export function isUpcomingEvent(event: Event): boolean {
  const today = new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-CA') // YYYY-MM-DD
  return event.date >= today
}

FILE: vite.config.ts
========================================================================================
import { defineConfig } from 'vitest/config'
import { loadEnv } from 'vite'
import { paychanguApi } from './server/paychangu.ts'
import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react'
import tailwindcss from '@tailwindcss/vite'

// https://vite.dev/config/
// PayChangu secret key: read from .env here on the server side only (no VITE_ prefix = never sent to the browser)
export default defineConfig(({ mode }) => ({
  plugins: [react(), tailwindcss(), paychanguApi(loadEnv(mode, process.cwd(), ''))],
  server: {
    allowedHosts: ['.ngrok-free.app', '.ngrok-free.dev', '.ngrok.app', '.ngrok.io', '.app.github.dev'],
    // Calls to /be/* go to the backend. Change the address with BACKEND_URL in .env.
    proxy: {
      '/be': {
        target: loadEnv(mode, process.cwd(), '').BACKEND_URL || 'http://localhost:3000',
        changeOrigin: true,
        rewrite: (path) => path.replace(/^\/be/, ''),
      },
    },
  },
  test: {
    environment: 'jsdom',
    include: ['src/**/*.test.{ts,tsx}'],
    // The unit tests use the in-browser mock so they run without a backend.
    env: { VITE_USE_BACKEND: 'false' },
  },
}))
